### [1] [DADL: A Declarative Description Language for Enterprise Tool Libraries in LLM Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05247)
*Axel Dunkel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DADL（Dunkel API描述语言）是一种YAML格式的声明式文件，用于统一描述REST API的端点、认证、分页、响应整形和访问分类。通过运行时执行层解释，无需部署独立服务器进程，支持集中化凭证管理和固定大小的Code Mode接口，将工具目录的上下文成本从约142,000 tokens降至约1,000 tokens（142倍缩减）。该规范以CC BY-SA 4.0发布，公共注册表包含20个服务的1,833个工具定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MCP（模型上下文协议）在组织规模下存在两个结构性缺陷：1）每个API集成需作为独立服务器进程部署，带来额外的部署、依赖和凭证管理开销，且大多数只是REST API的薄包装；2）每工具独立注册模式导致上下文窗口消耗随目录规模线性增长，迫使实际部署只能暴露组织实际使用API的一小部分。

Method: 提出DADL（Dunkel API描述语言），采用YAML格式在单个声明式文件中描述REST API的端点、认证、分页、响应整形和访问分类。该文件由运行时执行层解释，无需为每个API部署独立服务器进程或生成集成代码。所有工具共享同一运行时，实现凭证和授权集中管理，并通过固定大小的Code Mode接口向LLM呈现目录。

Result: DADL v0.1规范以CC BY-SA 4.0发布，公共注册表包含20个服务的1,833个工具定义。在该目录上，Code Mode将工具广告的上下文成本从约142,000 tokens降至约1,000 tokens，实现142倍缩减；每次搜索和执行调用的额外成本取决于具体任务。

Conclusion: DADL通过声明式配置和运行时解释，解决了MCP在组织规模下的部署复杂性和上下文窗口消耗问题，构建了一个可版本控制、可审计的企业工具库，任何团队均可通过统一的认证和授权边界扩展、共享和使用API集成。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) is the standard interface between large language model (LLM) agents and external tools. At organizational scale, however, it exposes two structural problems. First, every API integration is shipped as a dedicated server process with its own deployment, dependency tree, and credential handling; recent empirical work shows the overwhelming majority of these servers are thin wrappers around REST APIs. Second, the per-tool registration model causes context window consumption to grow linearly with catalog size, forcing real deployments to expose only a small fraction of the APIs an organization actually uses.
  We present DADL (Dunkel API Description Language), a YAML format describing a REST API's endpoints, authentication, pagination, response shaping, and access classification in a single declarative file. A DADL file is interpreted by an execution layer at runtime; no per-API server process is deployed and no integration code is generated, though the runtime is itself a server. Because all tools share that runtime, credentials and authorization are managed centrally, and the catalog reaches the LLM through a fixed-size Code Mode interface independent of size. The result is an Enterprise Tool Library: a versioned, auditable collection of API integrations any team can extend, share, and consume through one authentication and authorization boundary.
  The DADL v0.1 specification is released under CC BY-SA 4.0, and a public registry contains 1,833 tool definitions across 20 services. On this catalog, Code Mode reduces the context cost of tool advertisement from approximately 142,000 tokens to approximately 1,000, a 142x reduction; the per-call cost of search and execute invocations is additional and depends on the task.

</details>


### [2] [Automated Population-Level Audit Assurance via AI-Based Document Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05252)
*Santosh Vasudevan,Velu Natarajan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于AI文档智能的自动化审计交易测试框架，利用Snowflake Document AI从非结构化PDF对账单中提取结构化数据，与权威数据源进行大规模对账，实现从抽样测试到全量测试的转变，提升审计覆盖率和持续审计能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的手工抽样审计方式对非结构化PDF对账单进行核查，劳动强度大且无法扩展到数百万笔交易量级，亟需一种自动化、可扩展的大规模审计交易测试方法。

Method: 采用Snowflake Document AI，仅需约20份文档的小规模标注语料即可从非结构化PDF对账单中提取结构化数据；将提取的数据与权威源数据集进行对账以识别差异；通过交互式仪表盘和自动化报告呈现结果。

Result: 实现了从基于抽样的测试向全量（总体级）测试的转变，显著提升了审计覆盖率，支持持续审计目标。

Conclusion: 最近的文档智能和分析驱动的审计框架进展，使得可扩展的、近实时的风险识别和持续审计成为可能。

Abstract: Audit transaction testing validates accuracy and completeness of customer-facing statements against internal systems of record. Traditional manual, sample-based review of unstructured PDF statements is labor-intensive and does not scale to millions of transactions. This paper presents an automated framework for large-scale audit transaction testing using AI-based document intelligence. The solution leverages Snowflake Document AI to extract structured data from unstructured PDF statements using a small labeled corpus (approximately 20 documents). Extracted data are reconciled against authoritative source-of-truth datasets to identify discrepancies at scale. Results are surfaced through interactive dashboards and automated reports. The framework enables population-level testing rather than sampling-based approaches, improving audit coverage and supporting continuous assurance objectives. Recent advances in document intelligence and analytics-driven audit frameworks enable scalable, near real-time risk identification and continuous assurance.

</details>


### [3] [PARNESS: A Paper Harness for End-to-End Automated Scientific Research with Dynamic Workflows, Full-Text Indexing, and Cross-Run Knowledge Accumulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05258)
*Yuchen Wang,Zhongzhi Luan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PARNESS 是一个开源框架，通过有向无环图（DAG）内核、全文本PDF解析、知识图谱索引和轻量扩展接口，解决了现有LLM自主科研系统在控制流灵活性、跨领域知识整合和代码-论文关联方面的刚性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM自主科研系统（如AI-Scientist、PaperOrchestra等）在框架层面固化了特定的控制流形状（线性流水线、状态机、单智能体循环等），缺乏对动态、学科特异性工作流（实验、调研、仿真、理论）的灵活支持，且存在LLM上下文限制、论文全文利用不足、论文-代码关联缺失、跨轮次知识无法持久化检索等问题。

Method: 提出四项设计策略：(i) 基于四字段Agent契约的轻量DAG内核，将调度与领域语义解耦，支持用户通过YAML表达任意学科工作流；(ii) 全文本PDF解析与文献库子系统，将正文、图表索引为类型化对象，并支持仅摘要回退；(iii) 基于知识图谱的索引（论文、想法、实验、代码仓库），支持场景类型化检索（相似/矛盾/跨领域/反直觉）；(iv) 轻量扩展接口，允许现代编码智能体替换或新增模块。

Result: PARNESS 是首个结合声明式流水线、全PDF与代码仓库索引、跨轮次知识检索的开源自主科研系统。

Conclusion: 通过解耦控制流与领域语义、增强全文理解、建立论文-代码关联以及支持知识持久化，PARNESS 为构建更灵活、可扩展的自动化科学研究框架提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Recent autonomous research systems -- AI-Scientist, PaperOrchestra, AutoSOTA, DeepResearch, InternAgent, ResearchAgent and others -- show LLM agents can ideate, run experiments and write papers, but each fixes a particular control-flow shape (linear pipeline, state machine, single-agent loop, or fixed-recipe skill pack) at the framework level. We argue this rigidity has five roots: (1) workflows are dynamic and discipline-specific (lab work, surveys, simulations, theory all loop differently); (2) ideation is bounded by LLM context and cross-domain ideation needs knowledge a single context cannot hold; (3) summary-only views miss the paper body, yet full-text access is uneven, so the cumulative corpus must do the work; (4) a paper's open-source repository is often the only complete specification of its experimental scheme, but the paper-to-code link is neglected; (5) no tool persists cross-run knowledge retrievably into a finite LLM context.
  We present PARNESS, an open-source framework built on four design moves. (i) A thin DAG kernel with a four-field Agent contract decouples scheduling from domain semantics, so any discipline's loop is expressible as user-editable YAML. (ii) A full-text PDF-parsing and literature-library subsystem indexes paper bodies, figures and tables as typed objects, with graceful abstract-only fall-back. (iii) A knowledge-graph index over papers, ideas, experiments and code repositories, with scenario-typed retrieval (similar / contradictory / cross-domain / counter-intuitive), surfaces a focused slice into each LLM call. (iv) A small extension surface lets any modern coding agent (Claude Code, Cursor, Copilot, OpenCode) add or replace any module. To our knowledge PARNESS is the first open-source system combining declarative pipelines, full-PDF and code-repository indexing, and cross-run knowledge. Source: https://github.com/gtrhythm/PARNESS

</details>


### [4] [Teaching LLMs Program Semantics via Symbolic Execution Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06184)
*Jonas Bayer,Stefan Zetzsche,Olivier Bouissou,Remi Delmas,Michael Tautschnig,Soonho Kong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个包含500个C验证任务的评估框架，发现现有模型在违规检测方面存在严重缺陷，通过使用符号执行引擎生成的约3000个错误轨迹对Qwen3-8B进行持续预训练，并结合链式思维推理，将违规检测准确率提升17个百分点以上，训练后的8B模型在违规检测上超越4倍大的Qwen3-32B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型在C程序验证任务中整体准确率看似很高，但掩盖了违规检测能力严重不足的问题，且该能力随程序长度增加急剧下降，亟需方法弥补这一差距。

Method: 基于SV-COMP 2025构建500个C验证任务的评估框架，涵盖内存安全、溢出、终止、可达性、数据竞争五种属性；在通用开源C代码上运行Soteria符号执行引擎生成轨迹，用约3000个错误轨迹对Qwen3-8B进行持续预训练，并结合链式思维推理进行推断。

Result: 轨迹训练与链式思维推理的组合使违规检测提升17个百分点以上；训练后的8B模型在违规检测上超越Qwen3-32B（无思维链），整体准确率接近后者；两种方法的组合具有超加性效应；改进跨所有五种属性类型迁移。

Conclusion: 轨迹训练与链式思维推理的组合是提升违规检测的关键，该增益源于轨迹语义而非代码量，且轨迹的整理和格式至关重要。

Abstract: We introduce an evaluation framework of 500 C verification tasks across five property types (memory safety, overflow, termination, reachability, data races) built on SV-COMP 2025, and evaluate 14 models across six families. We find that high overall accuracy masks a critical weakness: while most models reliably confirm properties hold, violation detection varies widely and degrades sharply with program length. To close this gap, we train on formal verification artifacts: running the Soteria symbolic execution engine on generic open-source C code and using the resulting traces for continued pretraining of Qwen3-8B. Just ${\sim}$3,000 bug traces combined with chain-of-thought reasoning at inference time improve violation detection by over 17 percentage points, producing one of the most balanced accuracy profiles among evaluated models. On violation detection, the trained 8B model outperforms the 4$\times$ larger Qwen3-32B without thinking and approaches it in overall accuracy. The interaction between trace training and chain-of-thought is superadditive: neither alone provides meaningful gains, but their combination does. Improvements transfer across all five property types, including ones the training traces do not target. Our 28 configurations confirm the gains stem from trace semantics, not code volume, and that trace curation and format matter.

</details>


### [5] [Bridging Generation and Training: A Systematic Review of Quality Issues in LLMs for Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05267)
*Kaifeng He,Xiaojun Zhang,Peiliang Cai,Mingwei Liu,Yanlin Wang,Chong Wang,Kaifeng Huang,Bihuan Chen,Xin Peng,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过114篇研究的系统文献综述，建立了训练数据质量问题与代码生成缺陷之间的因果映射框架，提出了九维生成代码质量问题和双属性训练数据质量问题分类法，总结了18种典型传播机制，并指出质量保障正从被动过滤转向主动数据治理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在代码生成任务中频繁产生从逻辑缺陷到安全漏洞的质量问题，尽管这些问题常被视为模型层面的局限，但经验证据表明其根源在于训练语料库中的质量瑕疵。然而，训练数据质量问题与生成代码质量问题之间的具体传播机制尚未被系统映射，缺乏统一的因果理解框架。

Method: 对114篇主要研究进行系统文献综述，建立统一的分类体系将生成代码质量问题划分为九个维度，将训练数据质量问题分为代码属性和非代码属性两类；基于该分类法，形式化构建了包含18种典型传播映射机制的因果框架；并综合综述了数据、模型和生成全生命周期中的检测与缓解技术。

Result: 揭示了文献中明确的方法论转变：质量保障正从反应式、基于启发式的生成后过滤，转向主动式、数据驱动的治理与闭环修复。建立了训练数据属性与生成代码质量之间的因果映射关系体系。

Conclusion: 识别了开放挑战并概述了通过集成数据策展与持续评估来开发可靠代码LLM的研究方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) frequently generate defective outputs in code generation tasks, ranging from logical bugs to security vulnerabilities. While these generation failures are often treated as model-level limitations, empirical evidence increasingly traces their root causes to imperfections within the training corpora. Yet, the specific mechanisms linking training data quality issues to generated code quality issues remain largely unmapped. This paper presents a systematic literature review of 114 primary studies to investigate how training data quality issues propagate into code generation. We establish a unified taxonomy that categorizes generated code quality issues across nine dimensions and training data quality issues into code and non-code attributes. Based on this taxonomy, we formalize a causal framework detailing 18 typical propagation mapping mechanisms. Furthermore, we synthesize state-of-the-art detection and mitigation techniques across the data, model, and generation lifecycles. The reviewed literature reveals a clear methodological shift: quality assurance is transitioning from reactive, heuristic-based post-generation filtering toward proactive, data-centric governance and closed-loop repair. Finally, we identify open challenges and outline research directions for developing reliable LLMs for code through integrated data curation and continuous evaluation. Our repository is available at https://github.com/SYSUSELab/From-Data-to-Code.

</details>


### [6] [Is this Build Failure Related to my Patch? An Empirical Study of Unrelated Build Failures in Continuous Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05564)
*Andie Huang,Daniel Alencar da Costa,Grant Dick,Mariam El Mezouar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究分析了来自7个Apache开源项目的77,354个CI构建失败案例，发现开发者平均需花费4小时判断失败是否与自身推送相关。通过分析371个确认无关的构建失败，识别出20%的无关失败由测试无关导致。采用半监督正无标签（PU）学习，基于33个特征构建预测模型，在7个项目中达到了0.70-0.88的精确率、0.30-1.00的召回率、0.44-0.91的F1分数和0.63-0.97的AUC值。CI延迟、重复错误消息和前置评论数量是无关构建失败的重要预测指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CI系统中并发构建的失败可能并非由触发推送的代码变更引起，开发者需耗费大量时间（中位数4小时）判断失败是否与自身推送相关，造成效率浪费。当前缺乏有效方法自动识别无关构建失败。

Method: 从7个Apache开源项目收集77,354个构建失败案例；对371个确认无关的失败进行文档分析；提取33个特征（来自问题报告、评论和提交记录）；采用半监督正无标签（PU）学习构建预测模型。

Result: 模型在7个Apache项目上达到精确率0.70-0.88、召回率0.30-1.00、F1分数0.44-0.91、AUC 0.63-0.97。无关测试失败占无关构建失败的20%。CI延迟、重复错误消息、前置评论数量是重要预测指标。

Conclusion: PU学习可有效帮助开发者识别与当前推送无关的构建失败，减少不必要的调试时间，提升CI系统效率。

Abstract: Continuous Integration (CI) systems often run many builds concurrently. In this setting, a legitimate build failure may not be caused by the code push that triggered it. Such unrelated build failures can waste developer effort because developers must determine whether the failure is actionable for their current change. We study 77,354 CI build failures from seven open source Apache projects to understand and predict unrelated build failures. We find that developers spend a median of 4 hours identifying whether a failure is related or unrelated to their push. We also perform a document analysis of 371 confirmed unrelated build failures sampled from 10,316 potentially unrelated failures. The analysis shows that unrelated test failures account for 20% of the cases in which developers classify build failures as unrelated. To predict unrelated build failures, we extract 33 features from issue reports, issue comments, and commits associated with the triggering push. We build semi-supervised Positive and Unlabeled (PU) learning models for seven Apache projects. The models achieve precision from 0.70 to 0.88, recall from 0.30 to 1.00, F1-score from 0.44 to 0.91, and AUC from 0.63 to 0.97. Feature importance analysis shows that CI latency, repeated error messages, and the number of preceding comments are useful indicators of unrelated build failures. These results show that PU learning can help developers identify build failures that are unlikely to be caused by their current push.

</details>


### [7] [An Empirical Study of Proactive Coding Assistants in Real-World Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05700)
*Lehui Li,Ruixuan Jia,Guo-Ye Yang,Jia Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实证研究发现，LLM模拟的IDE交互轨迹与真实开发者行为在多样性、时间结构和探索模式上存在显著差异；基于真实数据构建的ProCodeBench基准测试表明，当前方法在真实场景下远未达到可靠水平，模拟数据不能替代但可补充真实数据用于训练主动式编码助手。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的编码助手多为被动反应式，而主动式编码助手（能推断开发者潜在意图）的研究受限于缺乏大规模真实开发者行为数据，现有研究多依赖LLM模拟数据，但其保真度未经检验。

Method: 通过自定义VS Code扩展收集1,246名行业开发者连续三天的真实IDE交互轨迹，并构造配对的LLM模拟轨迹进行对比分析；基于真实数据构建ProCodeBench基准，评估多种LLM、检索增强方法和智能体基线；进行数据混合训练实验。

Result: 模拟轨迹与真实轨迹在行为多样性、时间结构和探索模式上存在显著差异；当前方法在真实IDE轨迹下远未达到可靠水平，基于模拟的评估会高估真实性能；模拟数据不能替代但可补充真实数据用于微调。

Conclusion: 真实开发者行为数据对评估和训练主动式编码助手至关重要，模拟数据的使用需谨慎，未来研究应重视真实数据采集与利用。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based coding assistants have made substantial progress, yet most systems remain reactive, requiring developers to explicitly formulate their needs. Proactive coding assistants aim to infer latent developer intent from integrated development environment (IDE) interactions and repository context, thereby reducing interaction overhead and supporting more seamless assistance. However, research in this direction is limited by the scarcity of large-scale real-world developer behavior data. Existing studies therefore often rely on LLM-simulated IDE traces, whose fidelity to real development behavior remains unclear. In this paper, we investigate this simulation-to-reality gap through a large-scale empirical study. We collect real IDE interaction traces from 1{,}246 experienced industry developers over three consecutive days using a custom Visual Studio Code extension, and construct paired LLM-simulated traces for controlled comparison. Our analysis shows that simulated traces differ substantially from real traces in behavioral diversity, temporal structure, and exploratory patterns. Based on the collected data, we introduce \textbf{ProCodeBench}, a real-world benchmark for proactive intent prediction. Experiments with representative LLMs, retrieval-augmented methods, and agentic baselines show that current approaches remain far from reliable under real IDE traces, suggesting that simulation-based evaluation can overestimate real-world performance. Finally, our training study shows that simulated data cannot replace real data, but can complement it when used before real-world fine-tuning. These findings highlight the importance of real developer behavior data for evaluating and training proactive coding assistants.

</details>


### [8] [On Fixing Insecure AI-Generated Code through Model Fine-Tuning and Prompting Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05867)
*Ali Soltanian Fard Jahromi,Amjed Tahir,Peng Liang,Foutse Khomh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了增强AI生成代码安全性的多种策略（微调与提示工程），发现安全改进高度依赖于策略和模型，且修复漏洞时常引入新弱点，不存在普适有效的"防弹"解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成代码的功能表现虽强，但存在大量安全漏洞，而现有缓解策略的效果因模型和场景而异，缺乏系统性评估与理解。

Method: 针对常见弱点枚举（CWE）列表，采用微调和提示工程两种方法对模型输出进行精炼，系统评估不同模型与编程语言下的安全性改进效果，并分析弱点的严重性、共现性以及修复副作用。

Result: 安全改进高度依赖具体策略和模型；部分策略能减少特定类型弱点，但常以引入新弱点为代价；没有任何策略能在所有模型和场景中一致消除弱点。

Conclusion: 当前不存在普遍有效的"防弹"解决方案来确保AI生成代码的安全性，安全增强策略的效果具有显著的情境依赖性。

Abstract: The security of AI-generated code remains a major obstacle to its widespread adoption. Although code generation models achieve strong performance on functional benchmarks, their outputs frequently contain bugs and security weaknesses that undermine their trustworthiness. Prior work has explored a range of approaches to mitigate security issues in AI-generated code, e.g., using static analysis-guided generation and prompt engineering. However, their effectiveness varies widely across models and settings. This paper presents a systematic investigation of strategies for hardening model-generated code against a list of Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE). We assess the extent to which these strategies improve security across models and programming languages, using fine-tuning and prompting approaches for model output refinement. Beyond the prevalence of security weaknesses, we analyse the severity of identified CWEs, their co-occurrence, and the unintended consequences of remediation (i.e., whether fixing certain weaknesses introduces new weaknesses elsewhere in the same code). Our results show that security improvements are highly strategy- and model-dependent. Although some approaches reduce specific classes of weaknesses, they often introduce new weaknesses as side effects of the fixes. Moreover, no strategy consistently eliminates weaknesses across all models and scenarios, highlighting the absence of a universally effective "bulletproof" solution for secure AI-generated code.

</details>


### [9] [Evaluating Non-English Developer Support in Machine Learning for Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05902)
*Jonathan Katzy,Yongcheng Huang,Gopal-Raj Panchu,Maksym Ziemlewski,Paris Loizides,Sander Vermeulen,Arie van Deursen,Maliheh Izadi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型在多语言代码注释生成中的表现，发现非英语环境下生成质量显著下降（语言错误增加最高达15.1倍），且现有自动评估方法（包括神经指标、LLM-as-a-judge等）均无法可靠检测非英语注释中的错误，人类判断仍不可或缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前代码生成与评估主要以英语为中心，缺乏对多语言开发场景（代码中包含非英语自然语言）的系统性研究，亟需理解现有工具在该场景下的能力与局限性。

Method: 评估5个代码LLM（CodeGemma、CodeLlama等）在5种自然语言（荷兰语、英语、希腊语、波兰语、中文）上的代码注释生成能力；对12,500条生成注释进行开放式编码，构建人工标注数据集和26类错误类型分类体系；基于人工标注评估多种自动评估方法（重叠度量、神经度量、多语言/语言特定/代码特定模型扩展、LLM-as-a-judge）的可靠性。

Result: 非英语环境下生成性能显著下降，语言错误增加高达15.1倍，出现频繁的不连贯生成和语义错误；所有自动评估方法均无法提供可靠一致的评估，神经指标无法区分正确注释与错误输出甚至随机噪声，且在非英语场景下倾向于高估质量；LLM-as-a-judge与人类一致性最高，但仍无法可靠捕捉关键语言错误和语义错误。

Conclusion: 生成和评估是非英语多语言代码工具的关键障碍，人类判断在当前仍不可替代。

Abstract: Large Language Models are increasingly used in software engineering, but both code generation and its evaluation remain predominantly English-centric. This leaves a major gap in our understanding of how well current tools support multilingual development, where code contains non-English natural language. In this paper, we investigate non-English code comment generation and the reliability of current methods for evaluating such outputs. We evaluate five code LLMs (CodeGemma, CodeLlama, CodeQwen1.5, GraniteCode, and StarCoder2) across five natural languages: Dutch, English, Greek, Polish and Chinese. We further conduct an open-coding study of 12,500 generated comments, from which we derive a publicly released human-annotated dataset and a taxonomy of 26 error types. We use these human annotations, to evaluate the performance of neural metrics, and LLM-as-a-judge pipelines.
  Our findings show that generative performance deteriorates substantially outside English, with linguistic errors increasing by up to 15.1$\times$, alongside frequent incoherent generations and a rise in semantic errors. More critically, we show that detecting errors in non-English comments underperforms. Across classical overlap-based metrics, off-the-shelf neural metrics, extended neural metrics using newer multilingual, language-specific, and code-specific models, and LLM-as-a-judge pipelines, no automatic approach provides reliable and consistent assessment. Neural metrics fail to distinguish correct comments from incorrect outputs or even random noise, and tend to overestimate quality in non-English settings. LLM-as-a-judge methods achieve the highest agreement with human annotations but fail to reliably capture important language-related and semantic errors. Overall, our results show that evaluation and generation are key barriers for multilingual tooling, and that human judgment remains indispensable.

</details>


### [10] [Schedule-and-Calibrate: Utility-Guided Multi-Task Reinforcement Learning for Code LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06111)
*Yujia Chen,Yang Ye,Xiao Chu,Yuchi Ma,Cuiyun Gao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ASTOR是一个面向多任务代码强化学习的框架，通过任务效用驱动的分层数据调度和自适应策略优化，在单个模型上超越专用专家模型9.0%-9.5%，超越最强多任务基线7.5%-12.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于可验证奖励的强化学习在代码后训练中效果显著，但为每个任务部署独立的专用专家模型成本高昂。已有的多任务强化学习方法将所有代码任务等同对待，依赖固定的数据课程和统一优化策略，限制了多任务训练的有效性。

Method: ASTOR包含两个耦合模块：1) 分层效用路由数据调度模块，通过任务效用信号分层分配训练预算并优先选择高信息量提示；2) 自适应效用校准策略优化模块，动态缩放每个任务的KL正则化，使更新约束与各任务的当前训练状态匹配。

Result: 在两个广泛使用的LLM上，覆盖四个代表性代码任务的实验表明，ASTOR在单个模型上一致提升所有任务性能，超越最佳专用专家模型9.0%-9.5%，超越最强多任务RL基线7.5%-12.8%。

Conclusion: ASTOR通过任务效用驱动的协同机制有效解决了多任务代码强化学习中任务异构性问题，证明了基于效用信号的自适应调度与优化策略能够显著提升多任务训练的效果。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) with verifiable rewards has proven effective at post-training LLMs for coding, yet deploying separate task-specific specialists incurs costs that scale with the number of tasks, motivating a unified multi-task RL (MTRL) approach. However, existing MTRL methods treat all coding tasks uniformly, relying on fixed data curricula under a shared optimization strategy, ultimately limiting the effectiveness of multi-task training. To address these limitations, we propose ASTOR, a multi-tASk code reinforcement learning framework via uTility-driven coORdination. Centered on task utility, a signal capturing each task learning potential and cross-task synergy, ASTOR comprises two coupled modules: 1) Hierarchical Utility-Routed Data Scheduling module hierarchically allocates training budget and prioritizes informative prompts, steering training toward the most valuable data and 2) Adaptive Utility-Calibrated Policy Optimization module dynamically scales per-task KL regularization, matching update constraints to each tasks current training state. Experiments on two widely-used LLMs across four representative coding tasks demonstrate that ASTOR consistently improves a single model across all tasks, outperforming the best task-specific specialist by 9.0%-9.5% and surpassing the strongest MTRL baseline by 7.5%-12.8%.

</details>


### [11] [Breaking, Stale, or Missing? Benchmarking Coding Agents on Project-Level Test Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06125)
*Ye Shang,Quanjun Zhang,Haichuan Hu,Chunrong Fang,Liang Xiao,Zhenyu Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TEBench 是首个项目级的测试演化基准，包含 314 个来自 Defects4J 项目的任务实例，覆盖三种测试演化类型（Test-Breaking、Test-Stale、Test-Missing）。评估发现所有配置（3种工业智能体框架+6种基座模型）的识别F1收敛于45.7%-49.4%，Test-Stale最难（F1约36%），且现有方法依赖"执行-失败-修复"循环，无法处理Stale和Missing测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试演化基准仅以方法级粒度操作，使用预配对输入，绕过了从全项目中定位受影响测试的任务，且完全排除了新增测试的需求，缺乏真实项目级别的测试演化评估。

Method: 通过四阶段流水线从Defects4J项目构建TEBench，包含314个任务实例，每个实例标注三种演化类型（Test-Breaking、Test-Stale、Test-Missing）。评估七种配置（Claude Code、Codex CLI、OpenCode三种智能体框架及六种基座模型）和启发式基线。

Result: 所有配置的识别F1收敛于45.7%-49.4%；Test-Stale最难（F1约36%）；智能体产生高度可执行的测试修改但表面形式与真实解差异大；轨迹分析揭示"执行-失败-修复"循环仅适用于Breaking测试。

Conclusion: 现有测试演化方法存在共享的性能上限，缺乏主动语义推理能力，无法处理Stale和Missing测试类型，需要新范式突破当前的性能天花板。

Abstract: As production code evolves, the test suite must co-evolve to remain effective. Existing benchmarks for test evolution operate at method-level granularity with pre-paired inputs, bypassing the task of locating affected tests from the full project and excluding the need for new tests entirely. We present TEBench, the first project-level benchmark for test evolution. Given a project repository and a code-changing commit, TEBench requires systems to autonomously identify tests requiring modification, determine where new tests are needed, and produce the corresponding test patch. We construct TEBench through a four-stage pipeline over Defects4J projects, curating 314 task instances from 10 projects with developer-written ground truth. Each instance is annotated with one or more of three evolution types: Test-Breaking (tests that fail), Test-Stale (tests that pass but no longer meaningfully validate updated behavior), and Test-Missing (new tests needed for introduced behavior). We evaluate seven configurations spanning three industrial agent frameworks (Claude Code, Codex CLI, OpenCode) and six base models, alongside a heuristic baseline. All seven configurations converge on an identification F1 of 45.7% to 49.4%, revealing a shared performance ceiling across both frameworks and base models. Test-Stale is the most challenging type, averaging F1 around 36%, since configurations rely on execution failure signals and lack proactive semantic reasoning. On the update task, configurations produce highly executable test modifications whose surface form diverges substantially from ground truth. Trajectory analysis reveals a reactive "execute-fail-fix" loop that succeeds for breaking tests but structurally cannot address stale or missing tests. TEBench is available at https://github.com/iSEngLab/TEBench with a leaderboard at https://tebench-leadership.vercel.app.

</details>


### [12] [BUILD-AND-FIND: An Effort-Aware Protocol for Evaluating Agent-Managed Codebases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06136)
*Jhen-Ke Lin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了BUILD-AND-FIND协议，用于评估代码生成仓库在下游智能体恢复设计意图时的可理解性，通过分离行为正确性与工件侧恢复效果，衡量恢复准确率、可重复性、实现覆盖率和检查成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着仓库级工程越来越多地由智能体管理（一个智能体写仓库，后续智能体检查、审计或扩展），生成的仓库不仅是任务的答案，更是未来工作的沟通载体。行为正确的仓库在意图暴露清晰度上可能存在差异，现有基准仅关注代码行为正确性，忽略了仓库作为沟通工件的质量。

Method: BUILD-AND-FIND协议：构建者根据隐藏的仓库规约创建代码库；查找者仅通过代码库和基于规约的多选题库来恢复设计意图。协议分离行为正确性与工件侧恢复，报告恢复准确率、可重复性、实现覆盖率和检查成本。包含仅问题/仅规约的对照实验，以及审计机制区分遗漏主张与查找者失败。

Result: 在发布的先验知识丰富的任务包中，恢复准确率接近饱和，因此检查成本和查找者特定效应成为主要的局部比较指标。

Conclusion: BUILD-AND-FIND提供了一种评估生成仓库作为沟通工件的有效方法，当行为正确性趋于饱和时，恢复成本和查找者差异成为区分不同仓库质量的关键维度。

Abstract: Most coding-agent benchmarks ask whether generated code behaves correctly. That remains essential, but repository-level engineering is increasingly agent-managed: one agent writes a repository, and later agents inspect, audit, or extend it as working context. In that setting, a generated repository is not only an answer to a task but also a communication artifact for future work. Even when strong agents nearly satisfy the visible behavioral objective, repositories can differ in how clearly they expose the intended behavior and design choices behind that behavior. We introduce BUILD-AND-FIND, a protocol for evaluating whether downstream agents can recover those intended choices from generated repositories, and how much inspection that recovery requires. For each task, a builder sees a hidden repository specification and creates a codebase; a finder sees only the codebase and a specification-traced multiple-choice question bank. The protocol separates behavioral correctness from artifact-side recovery and reports recovery accuracy, repeatability, implementation coverage, and inspection effort. Accuracy and stability act as gates: effort is interpreted only when recovery succeeds reliably. Among artifacts from which the same intent can be recovered, lower effort by the same finder suggests that the artifact makes that intent easier to locate. Question-only and spec-only controls quantify generic priors and specification access, while audits separate omitted claims from finder failures and check whether correct answers cite artifact evidence. In the released high-prior task pack, recovery accuracy is near saturation, so inspection effort and finder-specific effects provide the main panel-local comparison.

</details>


### [13] [SiblingRepair: Sibling-Based Multi-Hunk Repair with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06209)
*Xinyu Liu,Jiayu Ren,Yusen Wang,Qi Xin,Xiaoyuan Xie,Jifeng Xuan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SiblingRepair是一种基于LLM的多补丁自动程序修复技术，专用于修复代码中相似位置（siblings）的同类错误。它通过token和嵌入匹配搜索语义相关的sibling候选，利用LLM识别与失败相关的sibling，并通过同时修复和迭代修复两种策略生成一致补丁。在Defects4J和GHRB基准上显著优于包括Hercules在内的SOTA多补丁修复技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者常常在实现相关功能的代码位置（称为siblings）犯相似错误，这些位置需要相似的修复。现有SOTA技术Hercules在sibling修复上存在局限：对sibling位置和提交历史有强假设、基于AST的sibling匹配过于僵化、基于模板的补丁生成不够灵活。

Method: 从频谱故障定位的怀疑位置出发，使用token匹配和嵌入匹配搜索语义相关的sibling候选（不限于失败测试覆盖或提交历史）；利用LLM识别与失败相关的sibling；通过同时修复（联合修复sibling）和迭代修复（逐步分析候选构建补丁）两种互补策略生成一致补丁；保留早期怀疑位置生成的候选补丁，组合成广义多补丁补丁。

Result: 在Defects4J和GHRB基准上，SiblingRepair显著优于包括Hercules在内的SOTA多补丁修复技术。评估还展示了其修复效率、sibling检测和修复组件的有效性，以及LLM数据泄露对结果影响有限。

Conclusion: SiblingRepair推进了自动化的sibling修复和广义多补丁修复技术，通过LLM和灵活匹配策略克服了现有方法的局限，取得了显著更好的修复效果。

Abstract: Developers often make similar mistakes across code locations implementing related functionalities. These locations, called siblings, share similar issues and require similar fixes. Accurately identifying siblings and consistently repairing them are crucial for automated program repair. Hercules is a SOTA technique designed for sibling repair. However, it is limited by strong assumptions about sibling locations and commit-history availability, rigid AST-based sibling matching, and inflexible template-based patch generation. To address these limitations, we present SiblingRepair, a new LLM-based multi-hunk APR technique specialized for sibling repair. Starting from a suspicious location identified by spectrum-based fault localization, SiblingRepair searches for semantically related sibling candidates using token- and embedding-based code matching, without restricting discovery to failing-test coverage or commit history. It then uses an LLM to identify failure-relevant siblings and generate consistent patches through two complementary strategies: simultaneous repair, which jointly repairs siblings, and iterative repair, which progressively analyzes candidates for patch construction. SiblingRepair further preserves promising patches generated from earlier suspicious locations and combines them into generalized multi-hunk patches. We evaluate SiblingRepair on the Defects4J and GHRB benchmarks. The results show that SiblingRepair substantially outperforms SOTA multi-hunk repair techniques including Hercules. Our evaluation further demonstrates its repair efficiency, the effectiveness of its sibling detection and repair components, and limited impact of the LLM data leakage on the results. Overall, SiblingRepair advances automated sibling and general multi-hunk repair.

</details>


### [14] [Correct Code, Vulnerable Dependencies: A Large Scale Measurement Study of LLM-Specified Library Versions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06279)
*Chengjie Wang,Jingzheng Wu,Xiang Ling,Tianyue Luo,Chen Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 首次大规模测量研究LLM生成Python代码中第三方库版本选择的安全与兼容性风险，发现LLM倾向于指定存在已知CVE的版本（36.70%-55.70%的任务含至少一个CVE），且存在系统性偏差，版本约束可显著降低风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的代码广泛使用第三方库并指定版本标识符，但这些版本选择带来的安全与兼容性风险尚未被系统研究。

Method: 构建PinTrace基准（1000个Stack Overflow编程任务），评估10个LLM的版本指定行为，分析CVE暴露、静态兼容性（安装失败率）、动态兼容性（测试通过率），并通过外部版本锚定实验验证风险缓解效果。

Result: LLM指定版本率26.83%-95.18%（直接提示）和6.45%-59.19%（清单文件）；36.70%-55.70%任务含已知CVE，其中62.75%-74.51%为Critical/High严重性；72.27%-91.37%的CVE公开时间早于模型知识截止日期；静态兼容率19.70%-63.20%，动态测试通过率6.49%-48.62%。所有模型收敛于相同的少数高风险版本。

Conclusion: LLM版本选择是一个此前被忽视的一类风险表面，存在系统性偏差而非孤立错误；外部版本约束可显著降低漏洞暴露和兼容性失败。已向模型社区披露发现，多项已被确认。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are now largely involved in software development workflows, and the code they generate routinely includes third-party library (TPL) imports annotated with specific version identifiers. These version choices can carry security and compatibility risks, yet they have not been systematically studied.
  We present the first large-scale measurement study of version-level risk in LLM-generated Python code, evaluating 10 LLMs on PinTrace, a curated benchmark of 1,000 Stack Overflow programming tasks. LLMs tend to specify version identifiers when directly prompted at 26.83%-95.18%, while down to 6.45%-59.19% in creating a manifest file directly. Among the specified versions, 36.70%-55.70% of tasks contain at least one known CVE, and 62.75%-74.51% of them carry Critical or High severity ratings. In 72.27%-91.37% of cases, the associated CVEs were publicly disclosed before the model's knowledge cutoff. The statistics show all models converge on the same small set of risky release versions, indicating a systemic bias rather than isolated model error. Static compatibility rates range from 19.70% to 63.20%, with installation failure as the dominant cause. The dynamic test cases confirm the pattern by 6.49%-48.62% pass rates. Further experiments confirm that these failures are attributable to version selection rather than code quality, and that externally anchored version constraints substantially reduce both vulnerability exposure and compatibility failures. Our findings reveal LLM version selection as a first-class, previously overlooked risk surface in LLM-based development. We disclosed these findings to the community of the evaluated models, and several confirmed the issue. All the code and dataset have been released for open science at https://github.com/dw763j/PinTrace.

</details>


### [15] [Constraint Decay: The Fragility of LLM Agents in Backend Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06445)
*Francesco Dente,Dario Satriani,Paolo Papotti*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了LLM智能体在多文件后端代码生成中遵循结构约束的能力，发现存在“约束衰减”现象：随着结构需求积累，智能体性能显著下降（平均损失30个断言通过率百分点），在惯例密集型框架（如FastAPI、Django）中表现更差，数据层缺陷是主要错误根源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试常忽略非功能需求（如架构模式、数据库、ORM映射等结构约束），奖励功能正确但结构随意的解决方案。生产级代码需要严格遵守结构约束，因此有必要系统评估智能体处理结构约束的能力。

Method: 在80个绿字段生成任务和20个功能实现任务上固定统一的API契约，跨越8个Web框架，通过端到端行为测试和静态验证器进行双重评估，隔离结构复杂度的影响。

Result: （1）存在“约束衰减”现象：结构需求累积时性能显著下降，强配置平均损失30个断言通过率百分点，弱配置接近归零；（2）框架敏感性分析显示，在最小显式框架（如Flask）中表现好，在惯例密集型环境（如FastAPI、Django）中平均表现差；（3）错误分析发现数据层缺陷（查询组合错误、ORM运行时违规）是首要根因。

Conclusion: 同时满足功能需求和结构约束仍然是编码智能体面临的关键开放挑战。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents demonstrate strong performance in autonomous code generation under loose specifications. However, production-grade software requires strict adherence to structural constraints, such as architectural patterns, databases, and object-relational mappings. Existing benchmarks often overlook these non-functional requirements, rewarding functionally correct but structurally arbitrary solutions. We present a systematic study evaluating how well agents handle structural constraints in multi-file backend generation. By fixing a unified API contract across 80 greenfield generation tasks and 20 feature-implementation tasks spanning eight web frameworks, we isolate the effect of structural complexity using a dual evaluation with end-to-end behavioral tests and static verifiers. Our findings reveal a phenomenon of constraint decay: as structural requirements accumulate, agent performance exhibits a substantial decline. Capable configurations lose 30 points on average in assertion pass rates from baseline to fully specified tasks, while some weaker configurations approach zero. Framework sensitivity analysis exposes significant performance disparities: agents succeed in minimal, explicit frameworks (e.g., Flask) but perform substantially worse on average in convention-heavy environments (e.g., FastAPI, Django). Finally, error analysis identifies data-layer defects (e.g., incorrect query composition and ORM runtime violations) as the leading root causes. This work highlights that jointly satisfying functional and structural requirements remains a key open challenge for coding agents.

</details>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [16] [Direct From Darwin: Deriving Advanced Optimizers From Evolutionary First Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05284)
*Daniel Grimmer*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文从进化第一原理出发，推导出多种先进梯度优化算法（如SGD、自然梯度下降、阻尼牛顿法、Adam）与达尔文进化理论的等价性。通过引入达尔文谱系模拟（DLS）噪声关系来统一Fisher确定性进化与Wright随机遗传漂变两种对立观点，证明这些优化算法在添加DLS噪声后可以成为进化论意义上忠实的计算机仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进化计算领域长期存在两大目标矛盾：高性能优化与忠实科学仿真。现代算法大多依赖物理启发式或浅层生物学隐喻，背离了进化第一原理。作者试图从数学上统一Fisher与Wright两种历史上对立的进化观点，并证明主流优化算法本质上与进化动力学兼容。

Method: 提出达尔文谱系模拟（DLS）框架，通过将Fisher确定性进化的总群体划分为Wright随机漂移的亚群体，引入DLS噪声关系作为结构化噪声。利用这种表示自由度，证明SGD、自然梯度下降、阻尼牛顿法、Adam等优化算法在添加DLS噪声后即成为进化上忠实的仿真。

Result: 证明了Fisher与Wright的进化观在无性生殖语境下形式等价；证明了满足DLS噪声关系的任何记账选择都会产生忠实的进化仿真；揭示了SGD、自然梯度下降、阻尼牛顿法、Adam等优化算法可通过添加DLS噪声纳入进化动力学框架。

Conclusion: 进化计算有望同时实现高性能优化和科学真实的进化仿真，而无需在两者之间做出取舍。通过将优化算法与DLS噪声结合，可以弥合工程优化与理论进化生物学之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Evolutionary computation has long promised to deliver both high-performance optimization tools as well as rigorous scientific simulations of Darwinian evolution. However, modern algorithms frequently abandon evolutionary fidelity for physics-inspired heuristics or superficial biological metaphors. This paper derives a suite of advanced gradient-based optimization algorithms directly from evolutionary first principles. We introduce Darwinian Lineage Simulations (DLS) to prove that, in an asexual context, Fisher's and Wright's historically opposed views of evolution are actually formally equivalent. This unification requires carefully partitioning Fisher's deterministically-evolving total population into Wright's randomly-drifting sub-populations. We prove that proper bookkeeping requires introducing a specific kind of structured noise (the DLS noise relation). Crucially, however, any bookkeeping choices which satisfy this relation will result in a faithful simulation of evolution. Using this vast representational freedom, we prove that a broad family of battle-tested optimization algorithms are already perfectly compatible with evolutionary dynamics. These include: Stochastic Gradient Descent, Natural Gradient Descent, and the Damped Newton's method among many others. By simply adding DLS noise (i.e., evolutionarily faithful genetic drift), these algorithms become scientifically valid in silico simulations of Darwinian evolution. Finally, we demonstrate that even the state-of-the-art Adam optimizer can be brought into evolutionary compliance through a minor mathematical surgery.

</details>


### [17] [CoupleEvo: Evolving Heuristics for Coupled Optimization Problems Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06341)
*Thomas Bömer,Bastian Amberg,Max Disselnmeyer,Anne Meyer*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: CoupleEvo提出三种进化协调策略（顺序、迭代、集成），用于大语言模型驱动的耦合优化问题启发式自动设计。实验表明，基于分解的策略（顺序和迭代）比集成策略具有更稳定的收敛性和更高质量的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型驱动的自动化启发式设计方法仅限于单问题场景，无法处理现实世界中多子问题紧密耦合的优化问题，亟需能够协调多个相互依赖子问题的启发式进化方法。

Method: 提出CoupleEvo框架，包含三种进化协调策略：顺序策略（逐个进化子问题启发式）、迭代策略（跨代交替进化不同子问题启发式）和集成策略（同时进化所有子问题启发式）。

Result: 在两个代表性耦合优化问题上的实验表明，基于分解的策略（顺序和迭代）收敛更稳定、解质量更高；集成策略面临搜索复杂性增加和结果变异性大的问题。

Conclusion: 协调跨相互依赖子问题的进化搜索至关重要，LLM驱动的启发式设计在复杂耦合优化问题上具有潜力。

Abstract: Many real-world optimization problems consist of multiple tightly coupled subproblems whose solutions must be coordinated to achieve high overall performance. However, existing large language model driven automated heuristic design approaches are limited to single-problem settings. In this paper, we propose CoupleEvo. CoupleEvo proposes three evolutionary coordination strategies to evolve heuristics for coupled optimization problems: the sequential strategy evolves heuristics for one subproblem after the other; the iterative strategy alternates the evolution of heuristics for different subproblems over successive generations; and the integrated strategy evolves heuristics for all problems simultaneously. The approach is evaluated on two representative coupled optimization problems. Experimental results show that decomposition-based strategies (sequential and iterative) provide more stable convergence and higher solution quality, while the integrated evolution strategy suffers from increased search complexity and variability. These findings highlight the importance of coordinating evolutionary search across interdependent subproblems and demonstrate the potential of LLM-driven heuristic design for complex coupled optimization problems. The code is available: https://github.com/tb-git-kit-research/CoupleEvo.

</details>

### [18] [Making AI Drafts Count: A Quality Threshold in Audio Description Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05348)
*Lana Do,Shasta Ihorn,Charity M. Pitcher-Cooper,Sanjay Mirani,Gio Jung,Hyunjoo Shim,Zhenzhen Qin,Kien T. Nguyen,Vassilis Athitsos,Ilmi Yoon*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过GenAD（基于无障碍指南和视频上下文的AD生成管道）和RefineAD（人工修订界面），研究了AI草稿质量对音频描述（AD）创作过程的影响。实验表明，高质量AI草稿（GenAD）能将完成时间缩短一半以上并显著降低认知负荷，而低质量基线草稿仅提供有限帮助。研究提出"质量阈值"设计原则：AI辅助需达到与目标内容复杂度相匹配的质量门槛才能有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已表明为新手描述员提供AI生成的草稿有助于提升AD质量并降低入门门槛，但草稿质量如何影响后续人工编辑过程仍是未解问题。

Method: 构建GenAD（融合无障碍指南和视频上下文的AD生成管道）和RefineAD（支持文本、时序、交付三方面修订的编辑界面）。通过被试内实验，比较从零创作、编辑低质量基线AI草稿、编辑高质量GenAD草稿三种条件的差异。

Result: GenAD草稿使完成时间缩短一半以上，显著降低认知负荷；基线草稿仅带来微小的效率提升。定性分析表明质量阈值与视觉复杂度正相关。

Conclusion: 有效AI辅助需达到与目标内容相匹配的质量阈值，而非简单提供AI参与即可。设计原则：AI草稿质量应随视觉复杂度提升而提高。

Abstract: Audio description (AD) narrates visual elements in video for blind and low-vision audiences. Recent work has shown that giving novice describers an AI-generated draft to start from helps produce higher-quality AD and lowers the barrier to entry. What remains an open question is how draft quality shapes the editing process. We investigate this through GenAD, an AD generation pipeline that incorporates accessibility guidelines and contextual video information, and RefineAD, an editing interface for human revisions. Human-AI contributions are measured across text, timing, and delivery. In a within-subjects study, we compared authoring from scratch against editing AI drafts of varying quality. GenAD drafts cut completion time by more than half and significantly reduced cognitive load. In contrast, baseline drafts generated from simple, unguided prompts offered only modest benefits, pointing to a minimum quality threshold for effectiveness. Qualitative findings suggest this threshold is content-dependent; as visual complexity increases, so does the quality needed from AI drafts. We propose this as a design principle: effective AI assistance should clear a quality threshold suited to the target content, rather than simply be present.

</details>


### [19] [Why Someone Asked "Why": Foil Inference in Human and LLM Question Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05401)
*Britt Besch,Tobias Gerstenberg*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文研究了人们在理解"为什么"问题时如何推断隐含的对比项（foil）。实验发现，人们主要通过事后期望（对已发生结果的惊讶程度）来选择对比项，而大型语言模型在这一任务上的表现不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释本质上是对比性的——人们需要理解"为什么是E而不是E'发生"。但这些对比项（foil）很少被明确提及，需要根据上下文推断。研究旨在揭示人类如何选择问句中的隐含对比项。

Method: 参与者阅读场景故事，对每个可能的对比项进行三种判断：先验期望（接下来会发生什么）、接近度（与已发生结果最相似的是什么）、事后期望（本可以发生什么）。同时判断提问者心中想的是哪个对比项。此外，将相同任务交给大语言模型进行测试。

Result: 人类的对比项选择最好由事后期望判断来预测。大语言模型在执行相同任务时，其显式期望判断与对比项选择之间的耦合不一致。

Conclusion: 人们通过考虑提问者在结果发生后感到惊讶的内容来推断对比项。正确的对比项选择不仅在人人交互中重要，在人与大语言模型的对话中也日益重要。

Abstract: Explanations are inherently contrastive: E happened rather than E' because of C rather than C'. However, these contrasts, or "foils", are rarely mentioned explicitly but have to be inferred in context. Here, we investigate how people select the intended foil E' of a why-question. Participants read vignettes and judged, for each foil, their prior expectation (what will happen next), closeness (what is most similar to what happened), and hindsight expectation (what could have happened instead), as well as which foil they thought the question asker had in mind when they asked the why-question. We found that foil selections were best predicted by hindsight expectation judgments. This suggests that people infer the foil by considering what a question asker finds surprising after the outcome occurred. Since correct foil selection is relevant not only in human-human interaction but also increasingly in dialogues with large language models, we investigated their performance on the same task. The coupling between LLMs' explicit expectation judgments and their foil selections is inconsistent.

</details>


### [20] [The Ambivalent Experience of Eye Contact for People with Visual Impairments: Mechanisms and Design Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05437)
*Markus Wieland,Phillip Koch,Michael Sedlmair*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过对17名视障人士的访谈，揭示了混合能力协作中眼神接触作为注意力与话轮转换线索的不可及性，识别出三个因果机制（指名道姓、注意力分裂与疲劳、参与度偏见），并提出用可配置交互契约替代单纯可视化眼神的设计挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技术方案强调解决眼神可视化的技术手段，但缺乏对视障者与明眼人协作中体验的机制层面的解释，尤其忽视了眼神接触作为默认社交线索对视障者的排斥性。

Method: 采用批判实在论视角，对17名视障人士在工作、教育、社交场景中的日常互动进行半结构化访谈，通过事件-因果机制分析提取重复出现的模式。

Result: 识别出三个机制：1）眼神无法分配话语权时，需依靠明确点名；2）模糊的言语进入线索与持续的接入工作导致注意力分裂和疲劳，甚至退出；3）眼神接触规范会扭曲对参与度的判断，迫使视障者主动管理可见性。

Conclusion: 将三个机制转化为五个设计挑战，主张可访问的眼神接触应支持可配置的交互契约（configurable interaction contracts），而非仅使眼神可见。

Abstract: In mixed-ability collaboration, eye contact is often treated as a default cue for attention and turn-taking. As these signals are primarily visual, they are not reliably accessible to people with visual impairments. While prior work emphasized technical solutions, mechanism-level explanations of their experiences with sighted partners remain scarce. We interviewed 17 people with visual impairments about everyday interactions across work, education, and social settings. Using a critical-realist lens, we link events to plausible causal mechanisms and identify three recurring mechanisms: First, when gaze cannot allocate the floor, addressability hinges on explicit naming. Second, unclear speech entry cues and ongoing access work split attention and build fatigue, sometimes leading to withdrawal. Third, eye-contact norms can skew judgments of participation, prompting active management of visibility. We translate these mechanisms into five design challenges that reframe accessible eye contact as supporting configurable interaction contracts rather than merely making gaze visible.

</details>


### [21] [Cross-individual generalizability of machine learning models for ball speed prediction in baseball pitching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05487)
*Ryota Takamido,Chiharu Suzuki,Hiroki Nakamoto*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究评估了机器学习模型在棒球投掷球速预测中的跨个体泛化能力，发现跨个体预测性能（R²=0.38）远低于个体内预测（R²=0.91），模型对中级投手存在高估偏差，躯干和 pivot腿表现出较好的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于ML的运动表现预测研究中，跨个体泛化能力（模型能否适用于未见过的个体）未得到充分关注，限制了模型在实际体育场景中的应用价值。

Method: 使用50名不同水平投手的数据集，采用留一被试交叉验证评估跨个体泛化性，并考察专业水平限制和时空运动信息约束对泛化性能的影响。

Result: (1) 跨个体R²=0.38，远低于个体内R²=0.91；(2) 模型对中级投手的预测误差显著高于专家投手（p<.05）；(3) 躯干和pivot腿在重量转移起始阶段仍保持R²>0.25的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 跨个体评估对提升ML在体育领域的实践应用至关重要，研究结果有助于深入理解影响目标运动的生物力学因素。

Abstract: Although machine learning (ML)-based performance outcome prediction is an important topic in contemporary sports science, one important issue is the limited understanding of the cross-individual generalizability of ML models in sports contexts. To address this issue, this study aimed to evaluate the cross-individual generalizability of ML models for predicting ball speed in baseball pitching. A dataset comprising 50 pitchers from various competitive levels was analyzed. Cross-individual generalizability was assessed using leave-one-subject-out cross-validation. Specifically, the effects of expertise level and restrictions on spatiotemporal motion information were examined to identify factors influencing model generalizability. The results revealed that, under cross-individual evaluation, (1) predictive performance was markedly lower than under within-individual evaluation, with R-squared value decreasing from 0.91 to 0.38; (2) the model tended to overestimate the performance of Intermediate pitchers relative to Expert pitchers, with a significant group difference in signed prediction error (p < .05); and (3) the trunk and pivot leg demonstrated relatively high generalization performance, with the pivot leg showing notable generalizability even during the weight-shift initiation phase (R-squared value > 0.25). These findings underscore the importance of cross-individual evaluation in enhancing the practical applicability of ML in sports settings and contribute to a deeper understanding of the biomechanical factors underlying the target movement.

</details>


### [22] [Designing with Tensions: Older Adults' Emotional Support-Seeking Under System-Level Constraints in Conversational AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05552)
*Mengqi Shi,Tianqi Song,Zicheng Zhu,Yi-Chieh Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过对18位老年人使用对话AI进行情感支持的访谈，研究发现老年人常在社交支持不可及时转向AI，但当前安全干预措施会打断情感投入、削弱用户控制感，甚至导致情感困扰。研究提出了在老年用户社会情境中实施安全干预、匹配情感节奏、维护用户自主性的设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 老年人越来越多地使用对话AI获取情感支持，但现有研究缺乏对日常使用中情感支持互动体验的了解，尤其是当AI系统限制、重定向或干预这些互动时的用户体验。

Method: 对18位老年人进行访谈，了解他们使用对话AI获取情感支持的体验，包括何时转向AI、如何在情感脆弱时刻互动，以及当支持感被打断时的反应。

Result: 老年人常在其他社交支持不可及时依赖AI；当前安全干预措施会以打断情感投入或转移控制权的方式重定向互动；这种中断会削弱老年人情感投入能力，甚至导致情感困扰。

Conclusion: 情感支持型对话AI的设计应将安全干预措施置于老年用户的社会情境中实施，与用户的情感节奏保持一致，并维护用户的自主感。

Abstract: Older adults have increasingly turned to conversational AI as a source of emotional support. However, little is known about how emotionally supportive interactions are experienced in everyday use, particularly when AI systems limit, redirect, or intervene during these interactions. We interviewed 18 older adults about their experiences using conversational AI for emotional support, examining when they turn to AI, how they engage during emotionally vulnerable moments, and how they respond when support feels disrupted. Our findings show that older adults often rely on AI when other forms of social support feel inaccessible. However, current safety-related interventions can redirect interactions in ways that participants experience as interruptions to emotional engagement or as shifts in control away from them. Such disruptions can undermine older adults' ability to remain emotionally engaged and, in some cases, contribute to emotional distress. We discussed design implications for emotionally supportive conversational AI, emphasizing the need for safety interventions that are enacted within older adults' social contexts, align with users' emotional pacing, and preserve their sense of agency.

</details>


### [23] [The Capacity to Care: Designing Social Technology for Sustained Engagement With Societal Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05651)
*JaeWon Kim,Lindsay Popowski,Louisa Conwill,Elizabeth `Lizzie' Li,Meryl Ye,Jiaying `Lizzy' Liu,Jose A. Guridi,Theia Henderson,Bingxu Han,Dennis Wang,Angel Hsing-Chi Hwang,Susan Wyche,Yasmine Kotturi,Gillian R. Hayes,Angela D. R. Smith*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文探讨社交媒体架构如何影响人们对大型社会问题的持续参与能力，基于Tronto的关怀伦理框架分析为何规模化关怀如此困难，并提出"可持续关怀"的设计方向，以解决用户因心理成本过高而产生的痛苦、 overwhelm和疏离问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人们并非缺乏对气候变化、不公正和人道主义危机的关注，而是面临能力瓶颈：社交媒体架构放大了问题意识却未提供有意义的行动路径，导致心理负担加重、 engagement下降，尤其是年轻群体。需要理解为何规模化关怀如此困难，以及技术设计如何改善这一困境。

Method: 采用Tronto的关怀伦理学框架（关怀需要意识、责任、能力和共同体四个要素），结合道德心理学和平台研究，分析主流社交媒体架构如何阻碍关怀过程（停滞在最初的意识阶段），并邀请研究者和设计师共同识别促进或削弱关怀能力的平台设计特征。

Result: 指出主流社交媒体架构将关怀过程阻滞在最早期阶段（仅停留于意识层面），未能支持后续的责任、能力和共同体环节；提出了"可持续关怀"的概念——一种用户可以长期维持而不致倦怠的参与模式。

Conclusion: 需要重新设计社交媒体平台，使其从单纯的注意力捕获机制转向支持持续、可负担的社会参与，从而实现真正意义上的"规模化关怀"。

Abstract: People care about climate change, injustice, and humanitarian crises. The challenge is not apathy but capacity: sustained engagement with large-scale problems is psychologically costly, and social media architecture often amplifies awareness while providing few pathways to meaningful action. The result is rising distress, overwhelm, and disengagement -- particularly among young people who encounter global suffering through platforms designed for attention capture rather than constructive response. This workshop examines how social technology design shapes the conditions for sustained engagement with societal challenges. Drawing on Tronto's care ethics framework and research in moral psychology and platform studies, we ask why caring at scale is difficult and how social media can both exacerbate and potentially mitigate this difficulty. Tronto's framework shows that good care requires more than awareness: it demands responsibility, competence, and community. Dominant social media architectures stall the caring process at its earliest phase. We invite researchers and designers to identify platform designs that deplete or support the capacity to care, and to develop design directions for \textit{sustainable care}: engagement that people can maintain over time without burning out.

</details>


### [24] [PersonaTeaming: Supporting Persona-Driven Red-Teaming for Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05682)
*Wesley Hanwen Deng,Mingxi Yan,Sunnie S. Y. Kim,Akshita Jha,Lauren Wilcox,Kenneth Holstein,Motahhare Eslami,Leon A. Gatys*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出PersonaTeaming方法，通过引入人格角色（persona）到自动化红队测试中，既提升了自动化攻击的成功率和提示多样性，又通过用户界面支持人类红队成员与AI协作，在11位行业从业者的用户研究中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化红队测试方法缺乏对人类身份和视角的考虑，无法模拟不同背景红队成员的策略差异；而人类红队测试虽能提供多样化视角，但规模受限。本文旨在弥合这一差距，通过人格驱动的方法同时推进自动化红队测试和人机协作。

Method: 1) 提出PersonaTeaming Workflow，将人格角色融入对抗性提示生成过程，探索更广泛的对抗策略空间；2) 将该工作流实例化为PersonaTeaming Playground，一个面向用户的交互界面，让红队成员可以自定义人格角色并与AI协作进行提示细化和变异；3) 通过11位行业从业者的用户研究评估系统效果。

Result: 与SOTA方法RainbowPlus相比，PersonaTeaming Workflow在保持提示多样性的同时实现了更高的攻击成功率；用户研究表明PersonaTeaming Playground支持多样化的红队策略，从业者认为其输出有用，且AI生成的建议即使未被严格遵循也能激发创新思维。

Conclusion: 本文推进了自动化与人在回路两种红队测试方法，为人机协作在生成式AI红队测试中的交互模式与设计提供了重要洞见。

Abstract: Recent developments in AI safety research have called for red-teaming methods that effectively surface potential risks posed by generative AI models, with growing emphasis on how red-teamers' backgrounds and perspectives shape their strategies and the risks they uncover. While automated red-teaming approaches promise to complement human red-teaming through larger-scale exploration, existing automated approaches do not account for human identities and rarely incorporate human inputs. In this work, we explore persona-driven red-teaming to advance both automated red-teaming and human-AI collaboration. We first develop PersonaTeaming Workflow, which incorporates personas into the adversarial prompt generation process to explore a wider spectrum of adversarial strategies. Compared to RainbowPlus, a state-of-the-art automated red-teaming method, PersonaTeaming Workflow achieves higher attack success rates while maintaining prompt diversity. However, since automated personas only approximate real human perspectives, we further instantiate PersonaTeaming Workflow as PersonaTeaming Playground, a user-facing interface that enables red-teamers to author their own personas and collaborate with AI to mutate and refine prompts. In a user study with 11 industry practitioners, we found that PersonaTeaming Playground enabled diverse red-teaming strategies and outputs that practitioners perceived as useful, and that AI-generated suggestions in the PersonaTeaming Playground encouraged out-of-the-box thinking even when practitioners did not follow them strictly. Together, our work advances both automated and human-in-the-loop approaches to red-teaming, while shedding light on interaction patterns and design insights for supporting human-AI collaboration in generative AI red-teaming.

</details>


### [25] [Priming, Path-dependence, and Plasticity: Understanding the molding of user-LLM interaction and its implications from (many) chat logs in the wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05767)
*Shengqi Zhu,Jeffrey M. Rzeszotarski,David Mimno*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过对7955名全球用户14万次聊天会话的分析，研究发现LLM用户并非从零开始或持续适应，而是通过早期交互轨迹快速形成稳定模式；早期探索行为与长期结果（如文本模式重复率、留存率）强相关；存在任务类型和模型版本更新驱动的并行动态。研究揭示了“能动性悖论”：尽管LLM输入空间无约束且由用户驱动，但用户探索行为反而更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验室研究无法反映真实世界中用户与LLM交互的先前经验和个体探索等外部因素，因此需要一种通过大规模野外聊天日志来研究真实用户-LLM交互的新方法。

Method: 对7955名匿名全球用户的14万次聊天会话进行纵向分析，通过用户表达模式、早期轨迹、文本模式重复率和留存率等指标进行量化研究。

Result: (1) 用户交互模式通过早期轨迹快速形成并稳定；(2) 早期探索与长期结果（重复文本模式、留存率）强相关；(3) 存在按任务类型（如情感支持）和模型版本更新的并行动态。揭示了“能动性悖论”。

Conclusion: 用户的实际探索行为少于预期，呼吁未来研究关注用户交互模式的“塑造程序”及其设计考量。

Abstract: User interactions with LLMs are shaped by prior experiences and individual exploration, but in-lab studies do not provide system designers with visibility into these in-the-wild factors. This work explores a new approach to studying real-world user-LLM interactions through large-scale chat logs from the wild. Through analysis of 140K chatbot sessions from 7,955 anonymized global users over time, we demonstrate key patterns in user expressions despite varied tasks: (1) LLM users are not tabula rasa, nor are they constantly adapting; rather, interaction patterns form and stabilize rapidly through individual early trajectories; (2) Longitudinal outcomes, such as recurring text patterns and retention rates, are strongly correlated with early exploration; (3) Parallel dynamics are present, including organizing expressions by task types such as emotional support, or in response to model-version updates. These results present an ``agency paradox'': despite LLM input spaces being unconstrained and user-driven, we in fact see less user exploration. We call for design consideration surrounding the molding procedure and its incorporation in future research.

</details>


### [26] [GazeMind: A Gaze-Guided LLM Agent for Personalized Cognitive Load Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05790)
*Bin Wang,Yue Liu,Benjamin Newman,Ajoy S. Fernandes,Zhiyuan Wang,Robert Cavin,Michele A. Cox,Vijay Rajanna,Takumi Bolte,Melissa Hunfalvay,Ulas Bagci,Michael J. Proulx*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出GazeMind框架，利用LLM结合眼动追踪数据评估智能眼镜用户的认知负荷，无需微调即可跨场景泛化；同时构建了最大的基于眼动的认知负荷数据集CogLoad-Bench（152人，40+小时，1万+标注），性能超过基线20%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能眼镜AI助手缺乏对用户内部认知状态的感知，无法主动预判用户需求；而当前认知负荷评估方法要么依赖不适合轻量眼镜的传感器，要么基于眼动的模型可解释性差、需任务微调且难以跨个体泛化。

Method: 提出GazeMind框架：将眼动追踪数据编码为结构化表示供LLM推理；设计任务引导推理（task-guidance reasoning）实现无需微调的跨场景泛化；通过融入用户特征和历史参考实现个性化适应。

Result: 在CogLoad-Bench数据集上，GazeMind取得最优性能，在所有指标上超过基线方法20%以上。

Conclusion: GazeMind通过LLM驱动的眼动分析，实现了可解释、可泛化、个性化的认知负荷评估，有望提升智能眼镜AI助手的主动感知能力。

Abstract: Smart glasses with AI assistants are increasingly used in daily life. However, current systems lack awareness of the user's internal cognitive state, leaving them unable to proactively anticipate users' needs without access to cognitive load. Existing methods for assessing cognitive load either rely on impractical sensors for lightweight eyewear or utilize eye gaze-based models that suffer from poor interpretability, and require task-specific fine-tuning, often failing to generalize across individuals. We propose GazeMind, a gaze-guided LLM agent framework for cognitive load assessment on smart glasses. It encodes eye-tracking data into structured representations for LLM-based reasoning and provides interpretable cognitive load predictions. Importantly, GazeMind generalizes across scenarios without LLM fine-tuning through a novel task-guidance reasoning approach and achieves personalized adaptation by incorporating user-specific characteristics and historical references. To support evaluation, we introduce CogLoad-Bench, the largest gaze-based cognitive load dataset with 152 participants, 40+ hours of multimodal data, and 10K+ real-time annotations across controlled and real-world tasks. Experiments show that GazeMind achieves state-of-the-art performance, outperforming baselines by over 20% across all metrics.

</details>


### [27] [Can providing feedback on gaze and mental-effort synchrony improve pair programming performance?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05836)
*Anahita Golrang,Kshitij Sharma*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过双人眼动追踪实验，研究基于联合视觉注意和联合心理努力的AI反馈（反应性vs.前瞻性）对结对编程协作表现的影响。结果表明，多模态反馈显著提升调试成功率和效率，其中前瞻性反馈更优，能减少时间开销、提高反馈采纳率并保护学习者的自主性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结对编程作为一种广泛使用的协作学习实践，其效果因协调、注意力和认知调节的断裂而差异显著。现有研究缺乏利用实时多模态信号（视觉注意和心理努力）驱动AI反馈来改善协作编程的系统性探索。

Method: 开展两项双人眼动追踪实验。研究1采用反应性反馈（当联合视觉注意或联合心理努力偏离预设阈值时干预），研究2采用前瞻性反馈（利用机器学习模型预测未来调节断裂并提前干预）。通过调试成功率、任务完成时间和反馈采纳率（代码变更）评估效果。

Result: 多模态反馈显著优于无反馈条件。反应性反馈中联合视觉注意与心理努力结合的反馈提升最大；前瞻性反馈进一步提升了性能、缩短了任务时间、增加了建设性反馈采纳，同时减少了侵入性干预，尤其对高绩效组合更有效。

Conclusion: 凝视和心理努力同步性可作为AI支持协作学习的可靠触发信号。反馈时机、透明度和预期性调节对于支持有效的结对编程至关重要。

Abstract: Pair programming is a widely used collaborative learning practice in computer science education yet its effectiveness varies substantially due to breakdowns in coordination attention and cognitive regulation between partners. This paper investigates whether AI supported feedback grounded in joint visual attention and joint mental effort can improve collaborative programming performance and how feedback timing shapes learner AI interaction. Two experimental studies using dual eye tracking capture real time indicators of collaborative regulation during debugging tasks. Study 1 examines reactive feedback that intervenes when observed joint visual attention or joint mental effort deviates beyond predefined thresholds while Study 2 evaluates proactive feedback that forecasts future regulatory breakdowns using machine learning models and intervenes pre emptively. Across both studies feedback effectiveness is assessed through debugging success time on task and feedback uptake reflected in code changes. Multimodal feedback significantly improves collaborative performance compared to no feedback conditions. Reactive feedback yields strong gains in debugging success and efficiency particularly when joint visual attention and joint mental effort based feedback are combined. Proactive forecast based feedback further enhances performance reduces time on task and increases constructive feedback uptake while relying less on intrusive interventions. Proactive feedback better preserves learner agency by maintaining optimal collaboration states, particularly for high-performing pairs. These findings demonstrate that gaze and mental effort synchrony can serve as reliable actionable triggers for AI supported collaborative learning highlighting the importance of feedback timing transparency and anticipatory regulation in supporting effective pair programming.

</details>


### [28] [EventColumn: Integrating Event Sequences into Tabular Visualizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06065)
*Jakob Zethofer,Andreas Hinterreiter,Lukas Schiefermüller,Belgin Mutlu,Marc Streit*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: EventColumn是一种新型列类型，将事件序列数据与异构表格属性统一集成，支持分析师在实例和组级别同时比较事件序列与数值、类别、时间等属性，并提供压缩概览、热力图组摘要、事件类型对齐及相似历史项目箱线图等功能。该方案与钢铁行业合作开发，已集成至Taggle和Microsoft Power BI，在钢铁生产物流和公共电商数据集上得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常只能单独比较事件序列或表格数据，缺乏同时支持两者比较的工具。从钢铁行业实际需求出发，需要一种能统一处理事件序列与异构表格属性的可视化方案，以便分析师在生产事件分析和仓库物流等场景中进行多维度对比。

Method: 提出EventColumn新型列类型，将事件序列数据与数值、类别、时间等异构表格属性统一集成到单个表中。提供压缩概览、热力图组摘要、事件类型对齐、相似历史项目箱线图等多种可视化手段，支持实例级和组级的同时比较。

Result: 成功集成至Taggle和Microsoft Power BI，在钢铁生产物流数据和公共电商数据集上验证了有效性，证明该方案能泛化到多种带有额外表格属性的事件序列数据集。

Conclusion: EventColumn填补了同时比较事件序列与表格属性的工具空白，通过统一集成和多样化可视化手段提升了分析效率，并具有良好的泛化性和可集成性。

Abstract: We introduce EventColumn, a new column type that integrates event-sequence data with heterogeneous tabular attributes into a single unified table. EventColumn lets analysts compare event sequences alongside numerical, categorical, and temporal attributes at both instance and group levels, offering a compressed overview, heatmap group summaries, alignment by event types, and boxplots of similar historical items. We developed EventColumn together with collaborators from the steel industry to facilitate the analysis of production events and warehouse logistics, but the solution generalizes to a wide range of event sequence datasets with additional tabular attributes. Unlike most existing approaches that compare either event sequences or tables, EventColumn supports simultaneous comparison of both. We demonstrate its integration with Taggle and Microsoft Power BI on data from steel production logistics and on a public e-commerce dataset.

</details>


### [29] [AffectGPT-RL: Revealing Roles of Reinforcement Learning in Open-Vocabulary Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06126)
*Zheng Lian,Fan Zhang,Lan Chen,Yazhou Zhang,Rui Liu,Jinyang Wu,Haoyu Chen,Xiaobai Li,Xiaojiang Peng,Bin He,Jianhua Tao*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出AffectGPT-RL框架，通过强化学习优化开放词汇多模态情感识别（OV-MER）中不可微的评估指标，解决了传统token级损失与评估指标不匹配的问题，在OV-MER和基础情感识别任务上均取得显著性能提升，并在MER-UniBench上达到最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OV-MER方法使用token级损失进行训练，但这种目标函数与OV-MER中使用的评估指标（如情感轮度量）不一致，且这些指标无法通过梯度反向传播直接优化，导致训练与评估之间存在目标偏差。

Method: 提出AffectGPT-RL框架，将强化学习引入OV-MER任务，利用其能够优化不可微目标函数的特性，直接优化与评估指标对齐的奖励函数。

Result: 在OV-MER任务上取得显著性能提升；在基础情感识别任务上也获得卓越效果，在MER-UniBench上达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 这是首个探索强化学习在OV-MER中作用的工作，验证了推理过程的必要性、不同奖励函数的影响以及该方法对其他情感任务的泛化能力，为后续研究提供了有价值的指导。

Abstract: Open-Vocabulary Multimodal Emotion Recognition (OV-MER) aims to predict emotions without being constrained by predefined label spaces, thereby enabling fine-grained emotion understanding. Unlike traditional discriminative methods, OV-MER leverages generative models to capture the full spectrum of emotions and employs emotion wheels (EWs) for metric calculation. Previous approaches primarily rely on token-level loss during training. However, this objective is misaligned with the metrics used in OV-MER, and these metrics cannot be directly optimized via gradient backpropagation. To address this limitation, we turn our attention to reinforcement learning, as this strategy can optimize non-differentiable objectives. We term this framework AffectGPT-RL. Furthermore, we conduct extensive experiments to elucidate the role of reinforcement learning in this task, revealing the necessity of the reasoning process, the impact of different rewards, and the generalizability to other emotion tasks such as sentiment analysis and basic emotion recognition. Experimental results demonstrate that AffectGPT-RL yields significant performance improvements on OV-MER. Beyond this task, we also achieve remarkable performance gains on basic emotion recognition, attaining state-of-the-art results on MER-UniBench. To the best of our knowledge, this is the pioneering work exploring the role of reinforcement learning in OV-MER, providing valuable guidance for subsequent researchers. Our code is provided in the supplementary material and will be released to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [30] [LearnMate^2: Design and Evaluation of an LLM-powered Personalized and Adaptive Support System for Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06257)
*Xinyu Jessica Wang,Christine P. Lee,Bilge Mutlu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文探索了利用大语言模型（LLM）驱动的工具来支持在线学习中的个性化和自适应学习，设计并评估了一个名为\tool{}的系统，该系统提供个性化学习计划、实时情境帮助和自适应学习活动。通过初步研究（n=24）和对比评估（n=16），结果表明\tool{}相比现有在线学习平台和LLM支持工具，在提升学习成果和用户体验方面表现更优，推进了AI教育学的发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线学习虽然广泛可及、开放性高，但缺乏个性化指导，而个性化对于有效学习至关重要。现有的大语言模型技术为弥补这一差距提供了机会，因此作者探索如何设计LLM驱动的工具来支持个性化和自适应学习。

Method: 作者迭代设计了一个名为\tool{}的系统，提供个性化学习计划、实时情境帮助和自适应学习活动。首先进行初步研究（n=24）评估系统有效性和可用性并据此改进，随后在对比评估（n=16）中与最先进的在线学习平台和LLM学习支持工具组合进行比较。

Result: \tool{}在提升学习成果和用户体验方面均优于现有的在线学习平台和LLM支持工具，推进了AI教育学的发展。

Conclusion: 本文推进了对个性化AI驱动教育工具设计空间及其对用户体验潜在影响的理解。

Abstract: Personalization is crucial for effective learning, yet online learning, designed for widespread availability and open access, lacks personalized guidance. Recent advancements in large language models (LLMs) offer opportunities to bridge this gap. We explore how LLM-driven tools may be designed to support personalized and adaptive learning and examine how they shape user experience and learning outcomes. We iteratively designed \tool{} to support online learning by providing personalized study plans, real-time contextual assistance, and adaptive learning activities. A preliminary study ($n=24$) assessed the effectiveness and usability of \tool{} and informed refinements in our system, which we then evaluated ($n = 16$) against a combination of a state-of-the-art online learning platform and an LLM for learning support. Results indicate that \tool{} advances AI pedagogy by improving both learning outcomes and user experience compared to existing online learning and support tools. This work advances our understanding of the design space of personalized, AI-driven educational tools and their potential impact on user experience.

</details>


### [31] [LLM-Based Educational Simulation: Evaluating Temporal Student Persona Stability Across ADHD Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06307)
*Jana Gonnermann-Müller,Jennifer Haase,Nicolas Leins,Thomas Kosch,Sebastian Pokutta*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过双评估框架（自我报告+观察者评分）检验LLM模拟学生时的人格稳定性，发现高强度ADHD人格在自我报告上稳定，但非脚本对话中行为表现会出现漂移，而结构化脚本交互可消除此漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM模拟学生为教育研究和教师培训提供可扩展方案，但其有效性取决于模型能否在长时间交互中保持稳定的人格特征，目前对此前提条件的验证尚不充分。

Method: 采用双评估框架（自我报告特征+观察者评分行为表达），设计两个实验，包含4种临床ADHD人格条件、5种LLM和3种提示设计，量化对话间稳定性（N=4,968）和对话内稳定性（N=3,952，9轮次）。

Result: 高强度ADHD自我报告特征稳定；观察者评分中，高/中度ADHD在非脚本对话中出现对话内漂移，脚本化交互（显式任务提示）可完全消除漂移。

Conclusion: 稳定的、人格一致的学习者模拟需要结构化交互设计来维持行为连贯性，这对教师培训、自适应辅导等需要持续路径依赖型学习者交互的应用具有重要启示。

Abstract: Student simulation with Large language models (LLMs) offers a scalable alternative for educational research and teacher training. Yet, its validity depends on whether models maintain stable personas across extended interactions. We test this prerequisite using a dual-assessment framework measuring self-reported characteristics and observer-rated behavioral expressions. Across two experiments testing four clinically-grounded ADHD persona conditions, five LLMs, and three prompt designs, we quantify between-conversation stability (N=4,968) and within-conversation stability (N=3,952 across 9 turns). Self-reported characteristics remain stable for high intensities, constituting a necessary prerequisite for valid behavioral simulation. Observer-rated behavioral expression reveals selective instability: within-conversation drift occurs in unscripted dialog for high and moderate ADHD personas. Scripted interactions with explicit task prompts eliminate this drift entirely. Stable, persona-aligned simulated learners benefit from a structured interaction design to maintain behavioral coherence, which holds significant implications for teacher training, adaptive tutoring, and any application requiring sustained, path-dependent learner interactions.

</details>


### [32] [Human-AI Co-Evolution and Epistemic Collapse: A Dynamical Systems Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06347)
*Xuening Wu,Yanlan Kang,Qianya Xu,Kexuan Xie,Jiaqi Mi,Honggang Wang,Yubin Liu,Zeping Chen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文从耦合动力系统视角，提出人类与语言模型通过使用-生成-再训练的反馈循环形成动态系统，并通过三变量最小模型（人类认知、数据质量、模型能力）识别出三种动态机制：协同进化增强、脆弱均衡和退化收敛，表明过度依赖AI会导致低多样性、次优均衡，从信息论角度对应信息瓶颈的出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前人工作分别研究人类认知卸载和递归训练中的模型崩溃，但缺乏统一视角。本文旨在提出一个统一框架，将人类与语言模型视为耦合动力系统，分析反馈循环如何影响系统演化轨迹。

Method: 建立包含三个变量（人类认知、数据质量、模型能力）的最小数学模型；通过仿真分析不同依赖程度下的动态机制；从信息论角度用熵减解释信息瓶颈的出现。

Result: 识别出三种动态机制：协同进化增强、脆弱均衡、退化收敛；增加对AI的依赖可诱发向低多样性、次优均衡的转变；该转变对应人类-AI回路中涌现出的信息瓶颈。

Conclusion: AI系统的演化轨迹不仅受模型设计影响，更受人类-AI协同进化动态的塑造；过度依赖AI可能导致系统退化到低多样性状态。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are reshaping how knowledge is produced, with increasing reliance on AI systems for generation, summarization, and reasoning. While prior work has studied cognitive offloading in humans and model collapse in recursive training, these effects are typically considered in isolation. We propose a unified perspective: humans and language models form a coupled dynamical system linked by a feedback loop of usage, generation, and retraining. We introduce a minimal model with three variables -- human cognition, data quality, and model capability -- and show that this feedback can give rise to distinct dynamical regimes. Our analysis identifies three regimes: co-evolutionary enhancement, fragile equilibrium, and degenerative convergence. Through a simple simulation, we demonstrate that increasing reliance on AI can induce a transition toward a low-diversity, suboptimal equilibrium. From an information-theoretic perspective, this transition corresponds to an emergent information bottleneck in the human-AI loop, where entropy reduction reflects loss of diversity and support under closed-loop feedback rather than beneficial compression. These results suggest that the trajectory of AI systems is shaped not only by model design, but by the dynamics of human-AI co-evolution.

</details>


### [33] [SIGMA-ASL: Sensor-Integrated Multimodal Dataset for Sign Language Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06351)
*Xiaofang Xiao,Guangchao Li,Guangrong Zhao,Qi Lin,Wen Ma,Hongkai Wen,Yanxiang Wang,Yiran Shen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了SIGMA-ASL，一个大规模多模态美国手语识别数据集，集成了RGB-D相机、毫米波雷达和IMU传感器，包含20名参与者执行160个ASL手势的93,545个同步多模态片段，旨在克服视觉数据集的局限性，推动隐私保护且鲁棒的手语识别研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SLR研究过度依赖视觉数据集，存在光照/遮挡敏感、隐私风险高、跨模态多样性不足等局限性，亟需一种融合互补传感模态的大规模多模态数据集来推动鲁棒且隐私保护的手语识别。

Method: 构建了SIGMA-ASL多模态数据集：采用Azure Kinect RGB-D相机、毫米波雷达和双腕部IMU捕捉视觉、射频反射和运动学信息；在受控工作室环境中采集20名参与者的160个ASL手势；通过统一传感框架实现毫秒级跨模态对齐；设计了标准化预处理流程和用户依赖/独立两种基准评测协议。

Result: 数据集包含93,545个时间同步的词级多模态片段，经过实验验证了数据集质量，展示了其在单模态和多模态SLR评测中的有效性。

Conclusion: SIGMA-ASL为开发鲁棒、隐私保护且普适的手语识别系统提供了宝贵的基础资源，有望推动多模态融合和跨模态学习在SLR领域的进一步发展。

Abstract: Automatic sign language recognition (SLR) has become a key enabler of inclusive human-computer interaction, fostering seamless communication between deaf individuals and hearing communities. Despite significant advances in multimodal learning, existing SLR research remains dominated by vision-based datasets, which are limited by sensitivity to lighting and occlusion, privacy concerns, and a lack of cross-modal diversity. To address these challenges, we introduce SIGMA-ASL, a large-scale multimodal dataset for SLR. The dataset integrates an Azure Kinect RGB-D camera, a millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar, and two wrist-worn inertial measurement units (IMUs) to capture complementary visual, radio-reflection, and kinematic information. Collected in a controlled studio environment with 20 participants performing 160 common American sign language (ASL) signs, SIGMA-ASL provides 93,545 temporally synchronized word-level multimodal clips. A unified sensing framework achieves millisecond-level alignment across modalities, enabling reliable sensor fusion and cross-modal learning. We further design standardized preprocessing pipelines and benchmarking protocols under both user-dependent and user-independent settings, offering a comprehensive foundation for evaluating single and multimodal SLR. Extensive experiments validate the dataset's quality and demonstrate its potential as a valuable resource for developing robust, privacy-preserving, and ubiquitous sign language recognition systems.

</details>

Findings demonstrate that PtMP IPoWDM significantly optimizes efficiency across diverse geotypes, slashing CAPEX by 92.0% and power by 99.2% compared to the traditional benchmark over a ten-year horizon.
