For any first-order attribution $φ(f)$ explaining a model $f$, we measure the directional influence of feature $j$ on the attribution of feature $i$, denoted as meta-attribution $\varphi_{j \to i}(f)$, by treating the attribution method itself as a cooperative game and computing its Shapley value. Theoretically, we prove that attributions hierarchically decompose into meta-attributions, and establish these as directional extensions of existing interaction indices. Empirically, we demonstrate that the metagame delivers insights across diverse interpretability applications: (i) quantifying token interactions in instruction-tuned language models, (ii) explaining cross-modal similarity in vision-language encoders, and (iii) interpreting text-to-image concepts in multimodal diffusion transformers.

</details>


### [145] [Molecules Meet Language: Confound-Aware Representation Learning and Chemical Property Steering in Transformer-VAE Latent Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06303)
*Zakaria Elabid,Jan Andrzejewski,Bartosz Brzoza,Attila Cangi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了无监督自回归Transformer-VAE（基于SELFIES表示）的分子潜空间中，化学属性调控的真实有效性。通过线性探针分析发现，潜空间编码了大量SELFIES序列伪影（如长度、分支/环标记、令牌熵）。为此引入基于残差化、混淆方向对齐分析和解码分子遍历的混淆感知评估框架。在该框架下，cLogP、FractionCSP3、HeavyAtomCount、TPSA、BertzCT和HBA六个属性展现出鲁棒的单调控方向；非线性探针进一步揭示部分属性存在稳定全局方向，另一些则更依赖局部潜变量梯度。结论表明：化学有意义的调控可从纠缠的分子潜空间中涌现，但必须通过解码分子验证并控制表示层混淆因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子生成模型常假设潜空间具有有意义的化学几何结构，但属性的可预测性可能源于序列层次的捷径（如SELFIES标记模式），而非真正的化学组织。作者旨在区分化学信号与SELFIES伪影，并验证潜空间中是否存在化学有意义的调控方向。

Method: 采用无监督自回归Transformer-VAE在SELFIES上训练；冻结模型后对RDKit描述符拟合线性探针，以探针权重作为候选全局调控方向；引入混淆感知评估框架（残差化、混淆方向对齐分析、解码分子遍历），控制SELFIES长度、分支/环标记和熵等混淆因素；使用非线性探针比较全局方向与局部梯度。

Result: 仅有6个属性（cLogP、FractionCSP3、HeavyAtomCount、TPSA、BertzCT、HBA）在混淆感知评估下表现出鲁棒的单调控方向；非线性探针显示部分属性有稳定全局方向，另一些仅局部有效。

Conclusion: 化学有意义的调控可从纠缠的分子潜空间中涌现，但必须通过解码分子验证并控制表示层混淆因素，否则伪影可能被误认为化学信号。

Abstract: Molecular generative models often assume meaningful latent geometry, but apparent property predictability can reflect sequence-level shortcuts rather than chemical organization. We study this issue in an unsupervised autoregressive Transformer-VAE trained on SELFIES. After training, we freeze the model, fit linear probes to RDKit descriptors, and use the probe weights as candidate global steering directions. To separate chemical signal from SELFIES artifacts, we introduce a confound-aware evaluation based on residualization, confound-direction alignment analysis, and decoded-molecule traversal. This is necessary because SELFIES length, branch tokens, ring tokens, and token entropy are strongly encoded in the latent space. Under this confound-aware evaluation, we find robust monotonic steering for cLogP, FractionCSP3, HeavyAtomCount, TPSA, BertzCT, and HBA. Nonlinear probes further show that some properties admit stable global directions, while others are better described by local latent gradients. Overall, our results show that chemically meaningful steering can emerge in entangled molecular latent spaces, but only when validated through decoded molecules and controlled for representation-level confounds.

</details>


### [146] [When Does $\ell_2$-Boosting Overfit Benignly? High-Dimensional Risk Asymptotics and the $\ell_1$ Implicit Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06314)
*Ye Su,Jian Li,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文理论分析了贪婪集成（ℓ2-Boosting）在高维线性模型中的ℓ1隐式偏差，发现在纯噪声及尖峰-各向同性设计下，良性过拟合在线性率上失效——方差仅以对数率衰减；提出了一种免调参的早停规则，在ℓ1路径有界条件下可恢复Lasso基本不等式并达到极小极大最优经验预测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ℓ2几何中良性过拟合已被充分刻画，但贪婪集成方法因ℓ1隐式偏差导致的非光滑坐标选择阈值非线性耦合，使得标准谱解析工具失效，亟需新的理论框架来理解该算法偏差下的高维风险行为。

Method: 将凸高斯极小极大定理与双截断高斯矩的渐近展开耦合，解析求解非光滑ℓ1插值解；并进一步分析Boosting流的非光滑次微分动力学，推导早停规则。

Result: 纯噪声模型下，贪婪选择将噪声局部化至稀疏活动集，过剩方差以Θ(σ²/log(p/n))的对数率衰减，而非线性率；尖峰-各向同性设计下，风险在r₂≫n时趋于零，但仅以Θ(σ²/log(r₂/n))的对数率收敛，慢于ℓ2几何下的线性衰减。

Conclusion: ℓ1隐式偏差下的贪婪集成在高维中良性过拟合失效（仅对数率），但通过早停规则可在ℓ1有界信号下达到Lasso级最优泛化性能。

Abstract: Benign overfitting is well-characterized in $\ell_2$ geometries, but its behavior under the $\ell_1$ implicit bias of greedy ensembles remains challenging. The analytical barrier stems from the non-linear coupling of coordinate selection thresholds, which invalidates standard spectral resolvent tools. To isolate this algorithmic bias, we characterize the high-dimensional risk of continuous-time $\ell_2$-Boosting over $p$ features and $n$ samples. By coupling the Convex Gaussian Minimax Theorem with delicate asymptotic expansions of double-sided truncated Gaussian moments, we analytically resolve the non-smooth $\ell_1$ interpolant. Under an isotropic pure-noise model, we prove that benign overfitting fails at the linear rate: greedy selection localizes noise into sparse active sets, and the excess variance decays at a logarithmic rate $Θ(σ^2/\log(p/n))$ for noise variance $σ^2$. We remark that while this localization mechanism should persist in the presence of signals, the exact signal-noise decomposition remains an open problem. For spiked-isotropic designs with $k^*$ head eigenvalues and $r_2 = p - k^*$ tail dimensions, the risk converges to zero when $r_{2} \gg n$, but only at a logarithmic rate $Θ(σ^2/\log(r_2/n))$, which is slower than the linear decay observed in $\ell_2$ geometries. To avoid this slow convergence, we analyze the non-smooth subdifferential dynamics of the boosting flow. This yields a tuning-free early stopping rule that, under a bounded $\ell_1$-path condition, recovers the Lasso basic inequality and attains the minimax-optimal empirical prediction rate for $\ell_1$-bounded signals.

</details>


### [147] [Pro-KLShampoo: Projected KL-Shampoo with Whitening Recovered by Orthogonalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06316)
*Ruotong Sun,Ermin Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Pro-KLShampoo方法，通过利用KL-Shampoo预条件子的"尖峰-平坦"谱结构（少量主导特征值+均匀尾部），在保留r维子空间完整谱结构的同时对剩余方向进行正交化，在四个预训练规模上相比KL-Shampoo提升了验证损失、GPU内存和运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于Kronecker分解的预条件优化器（如KL-Shampoo）与梯度动量正交化方法（如Muon）通常是独立发展的两条路线，缺乏对两者之间结构联系的深入分析。

Method: 观察到KL-Shampoo的Kronecker预条件子具有"尖峰-平坦"谱结构（在秩-ρ信号加噪声梯度模型下精确成立），据此提出限制其中一个Kronecker因子到参数化族：在追踪的r维子空间上保留完整谱结构，其余n-r个方向共享单一特征值并应用正交化。通过恒等式证明该正交化恢复了完整KL-Shampoo预条件子的代数形式。

Result: 在GPT-2 124M/350M、LLaMA 134M/450M四个预训练规模上，Pro-KLShampoo在验证损失、每GPU峰值内存和达到各损失水平的wall-clock时间上均一致优于KL-Shampoo。

Conclusion: 利用预条件子的谱结构可以桥接Kronecker预条件与正交化两条路线，在保持理论形式一致性的同时显著提升计算效率。

Abstract: Optimizers that exploit the matrix structure of gradients are central to modern LLM pre-training, with two distinct frontiers: explicit Kronecker-factored preconditioning -- most recently KL-Shampoo, which estimates the preconditioner via KL divergence minimization -- and orthogonalization of the gradient momentum, exemplified by Muon and analyzed as steepest descent under the spectral norm. The two routes are typically developed in isolation. We make a structural observation about KL-Shampoo's Kronecker preconditioners: their eigenvalue spectra exhibit a \emph{spike-and-flat} shape -- a few dominant eigenvalues followed by an approximately uniform tail -- across layers and training stages, holding exactly under a rank-$ρ$ signal-plus-noise gradient model. We exploit this structure by restricting one of KL-Shampoo's Kronecker factors to a parametric family aligned with the spike-and-flat shape: full spectral structure on a tracked $r$-dimensional subspace, single shared eigenvalue across the remaining $n-r$ directions. On these directions, we apply orthogonalization. An identity shows that this orthogonalization recovers the algebraic form of full KL-Shampoo's preconditioner. On four pre-training scales (GPT-2 124M / 350M, LLaMA 134M / 450M), Pro-KLShampoo consistently outperforms KL-Shampoo at every subspace rank we test in validation loss, peak per-GPU memory, and wallclock time to reach each loss level.

</details>


### [148] [LINC: Decoupling Local Consequence Scoring from Hidden Matching in Constructive Neural Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06332)
*Shaofeng Qin,Li Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LINC解码器架构，通过显式计算局部过渡结果（行程、等待、松弛、容量变化）并按其决策角色使用，显著提升CVRPTW等约束路由问题的求解质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有构造式神经路由求解器将下一步动作评分简单视为解码器上下文与候选嵌入的全局匹配，隐藏了行程、等待、松弛、容量变化等确定性一步过渡后果，导致隐藏状态需要重新发现这些转移算术，性能受限。

Method: 提出LINC（Local Inference via Normed Comparison）解码器侧候选决策架构：(1) 显式计算每一步的局部过渡结果（行程、等待、松弛、容量变化等）；(2) 中心化的相对后果通过共享线性局部评分器进行比较；(3) 可行集摘要用于调制解码器上下文，保留标准全局匹配机制。

Result: 在CVRPTW上，LINC将PolyNet在Solomon/Homberger基准上的差距从13.83%/38.15%降至7.26%/14.71%；在TSP和CVRP上也改进了外部基准差距。

Conclusion: 显式建模确定性过渡后果并按其决策角色分离处理，能有效提升神经路由求解器在复杂约束下的表现，且同一接口可扩展至CVRP和TSP等不同问题。

Abstract: Constructive neural routing solvers usually score the next action by matching a decoder context to candidate embeddings, hiding deterministic one-step consequences such as travel, waiting, slack, and capacity changes. We propose LINC (Local Inference via Normed Comparison), a decoder-side candidate decision architecture that computes these consequences explicitly. LINC uses them according to their decision role: centered relative consequences are compared by a shared linear local scorer, while feasible-set summaries modulate the decoder context. This preserves standard global matching and relieves the hidden state from rediscovering transition arithmetic. The Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem with Time Windows (CVRPTW) serves as the main constrained-routing stress test; the same interface extends to the Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem (CVRP) and Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP). In particular, for CVRPTW, LINC reduces PolyNet's Solomon/Homberger gaps from 13.83\%/38.15\% to 7.26\%/14.71\%; for TSP and CVRP, it also improves external-benchmark gaps.

</details>


### [149] [Eliciting associations between clinical variables from LLMs via comparison questions across populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06335)
*Fabian Kabus,Kian Kordtomeikel,Thomas Brox,Heinz Wiendl,Daiana Stolz,Harald Binder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于结构化比较问题（患者比较三元组问题）的方法，从大语言模型中恢复患者特征间的相关性和因果联系，结合统计模型和不变因果预测（ICP）来获取保守的候选因果父节点链接，并在COPD和MS两个临床领域验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的训练数据包含大量生物医学文献，反映了不同患者群体的数据。研究者希望从LLM中恢复患者特征之间的相关性和因果联系，作为医疗决策的关键构建模块，同时避免直接询问带来的陷阱。

Method: 提出基于结构化比较问题（患者比较三元组问题）的方法，结合LLM表示的统计模型来估计相关性（无需访问激活值或模型内部）。通过提示级别的环境变换获得不同子群体的相关性估计，进而采用不变因果预测（ICP）方法获得保守的候选父节点因果链接。

Result: 在COPD和MS两个临床领域，通过提示环境变换得到的相关性平滑、稳定且具有临床可解释性，统计上显著的变化支持了下游的不变性检验，ICP提供了少量候选的不变父节点因果链接。

Conclusion: 通过三元组比较的间接询问方法可以从LLM中恢复有意义的关联结构，并提供了一条从隐式相关到与LLM回答模式一致的因果陈述的谨慎路径。

Abstract: The training data of large language models (LLMs) comprises a wide range of biomedical literature, reflecting data from many different patient populations. We investigate how it might be possible to recover information on correlation and causal links between patient characteristics, as a key building block for medical decision making. To avoid the pitfalls of direct elicitation, we propose an approach based on structured comparison questions, specifically patient comparison triplet questions. This is combined with a statistical model for the LLM representation that provides estimates of correlations without access to activations or model internals. Intuitively, we consider how similarity decisions of LLMs based on a first variable are affected by providing information on a second variable for one of the patients being assessed. We then induce prompt-level environment shifts to obtain correlation estimates for different subpopulations, which enables an invariant causal prediction (ICP) approach to obtain conservative candidate parent links. We demonstrate the method in two clinical domains, chronic obstructive pulmonary disease (COPD) and multiple sclerosis (MS). Across prompted environments, the elicited correlations are smooth, stable, and clinically interpretable, yet vary in a statistically significant way that supports downstream invariance testing, such that ICP provides a small set of candidate invariant parent links. These results show that indirect elicitation via triplet comparisons can recover meaningful association structure from LLMs and offer a cautious route from implicit correlations to causal statements that are congruent with LLM answering patterns.

</details>


### [150] [Is Escalation Worth It? A Decision-Theoretic Characterization of LLM Cascades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06350)
*Dylan Bouchard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于约束优化和对偶理论的决策框架来分析模型级联（cascade）的成本-质量边界。研究发现：两级级联的成本-质量边界具有分段凹性；k模型级联中，确定性的两级阈值级联的最优边界是成对级联的点态包络；完整固定链的性能不如成对包络，而轻量级预生成路由策略在多数数据集上优于最佳级联策略，原因在于它避免了将查询发送给大模型前先支付小模型生成成本的结构性开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型级联方法将延迟阈值视为经验超参数，缺乏对成本-质量边界几何形状的理论指导。作者希望建立一个严格的理论框架来理解级联策略的优化本质。

Method: 基于约束优化和对偶理论建立决策框架；分析两级和k级级联的成本-质量边界性质；在五个基准上使用八个模型进行实证验证；比较不同级联策略（固定链、成对包络、优化子序列级联、预生成路由）。

Result: 两级级联边界具有分段凹性，影子价格倒数关联预算和质量约束；k模型级联的最优确定性两级阈值策略是成对级联的点态包络；完整固定链不如成对包络；轻量级预生成路由在4/5数据集上优于最佳级联，主要因为它避免了小模型的生成成本。

Conclusion: 级联性能的主要限制来自结构性成本（发送给大模型前已支付小模型成本），而非中间阶段不足；预生成路由通过消除这种结构性成本获得优势。

Abstract: Model cascades, in which a cheap LLM defers to an expensive one on low-confidence queries, are widely used to navigate the cost-quality tradeoff at deployment. Existing approaches largely treat the deferral threshold as an empirical hyperparameter, with limited guidance on the geometry of the resulting cost-quality frontier over a model pool. We develop a decision-theoretic framework grounded in constrained optimization and duality. For a two-model cascade, we establish piecewise concavity of the cost-quality frontier on decreasing-benefit regions of the confidence support, with reciprocal shadow prices linking the budget- and quality-constrained formulations. Given a pool of $k$ models, we characterize the frontier achievable by deterministic two-model threshold cascades as the pointwise envelope over $\binom{k}{2}$ pairwise cascades, with switching points where the optimal pair changes. For $k$-model cascades, we derive first-order conditions in which a single shadow price equalizes marginal quality-per-cost across stage boundaries. We validate the framework on five benchmarks (MATH, MMLU, TriviaQA, SimpleQA, LiveCodeBench) across eight models from five providers. Within the deterministic threshold-cascade class, full fixed chains underperform the pairwise envelope, and optimized subsequence cascades do not deliver practically meaningful held-out gains over it. A lightweight pre-generation router exceeds the best cascade policy on four of five datasets, mainly because it avoids the cheap model's generation cost on queries sent directly to a larger model rather than because of a stronger routing signal. These results suggest that cascade performance is limited primarily by structural cost, since cascades pay the cheap model before any escalation decision, rather than by a shortage of intermediate stages.

</details>


### [151] [Order-Agnostic Autoregressive Modelling with Missing Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06355)
*Ignacio Peis,Pablo M. Olmos,Jes Frellsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新解读顺序无关自回归模型（OARM）在缺失数据场景下的应用，提出缺失感知的顺序无关自回归模型（MO-ARM），证明其在完全数据上训练隐含了完全随机缺失（MCAR）机制下的插补，并首次提出在不完整数据上训练该模型的框架，以及利用其进行主动信息获取。在多个真实基准上，MO-ARM优于已有插补方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序无关自回归模型在深度生成建模中表现优异，但在不完整数据场景下的应用尚未被充分探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，重新审视OARM在缺失数据下的行为，并开发一个能直接处理不完整数据的统一框架。

Method: 1) 理论分析：证明OARM在完全数据上的标准训练隐式执行了MCAR机制下的插补；2) 提出MO-ARM框架：首个在一般缺失机制下直接训练不完整数据的原理性框架；3) 利用OARM的摊销条件密度估计进行主动信息获取，即顺序选择最有信息量的缺失变量用于下游预测或推断。

Result: 在多个真实世界基准数据集上，MO-ARM在插补性能上持续优于已有的插补基线方法。

Conclusion: OARM在缺失数据场景下具有天然优势，MO-ARM通过理论分析和框架设计将其扩展至一般缺失机制和主动信息获取任务，验证了其有效性和优越性。

Abstract: Order-Agnostic autoregressive models have demonstrated strong performance in deep generative modeling, yet their use in settings with incomplete data remains largely unexplored. In this work, we reinterpret them through the lens of missing data. First, we show that their standard training procedure on fully observed data implicitly performs imputation under a missing completely at random mechanism, resulting in robust out-of-sample imputation performance in settings with high missingness. Second, we introduce the first principled framework for training them directly on incomplete datasets under general missingness mechanisms. Third, we leverage their amortized conditional density estimation to perform active information acquisition, i.e., sequentially selecting the most informative missing variables for downstream prediction or inference. Across a suite of real-world benchmarks, our Missingness-Aware Order-Agnostic Autoregressive Model (MO-ARM) consistently outperforms established imputation baselines.

</details>


### [152] [Memory Efficient Full-gradient Attacks (MEFA) Framework for Adversarial Defense Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06357)
*Yuan Du,Mitchel Hill,HanQin Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于梯度检查点的内存高效全梯度评估框架，用于对迭代随机净化防御进行白盒攻击，发现全梯度攻击能揭示近似梯度评估遗漏的漏洞，在ℓ∞和ℓ₂白盒攻击上达到SOTA效果，证明精确梯度评估对可靠基准测试至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对扩散和朗之万净化防御的评估因内存限制采用近似反向传播，弱化了攻击信号，可能导致鲁棒性高估；同时迭代净化中的随机性未得到充分控制，不同轨迹会显著影响鲁棒性指标。

Method: 利用梯度检查点技术通过额外重计算换取更低内存使用，实现长净化轨迹的精确端到端梯度计算；结合控制随机变异性的评估协议，构建内存高效的全梯度评估框架。

Result: 全梯度攻击在ℓ∞和ℓ₂白盒攻击上达到SOTA，发现了近似梯度方法遗漏的漏洞；该方法还支持对分布外鲁棒性的探测。

Conclusion: 精确梯度评估对于迭代随机防御的可靠基准测试是不可或缺的，近似方法可能显著高估模型鲁棒性。

Abstract: This work studies the robust evaluation of iterative stochastic purification defenses under white-box adversarial attacks. Our key technical insight is that gradient checkpointing makes exact end-to-end gradient computation through long purification trajectories practical by trading additional recomputation for substantially lower memory usage. This enables full-gradient adaptive attacks against diffusion- and Langevin-based purification defenses, where prior evaluations often resort to approximate backpropagation due to memory constraints. These approximations can weaken the attack signal and risk overestimating robustness. In parallel, stochasticity in iterative purification is frequently under-controlled, even though different purification trajectories can substantially change reported robustness metrics. Building on this insight, we introduce a memory-efficient full-gradient evaluation framework for stochastic purification defenses. The framework combines checkpointed backpropagation with evaluation protocols that control stochastic variability, thereby reducing memory bottlenecks while preserving exact gradients. We evaluate diffusion-based purification and Langevin sampling with Energy-Based Models (EBMs), demonstrating that full-gradient attacks uncover vulnerabilities missed by approximate-gradient evaluations. Our framework yields stronger state-of-the-art $\ell_{\infty}$ and $\ell_{2}$ white-box attacks and further supports probing out-of-distribution robustness. Overall, our results show that exact-gradient evaluation is essential for reliable benchmarking of iterative stochastic defenses.

</details>


### [153] [Preliminary Insights in Chronos Frequency Data Understanding and Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06361)
*Alessandro Pagani,Marco Cominelli,Liying Han,Gaofeng Dong,Sergio Benini,Francesco Gringoli,Mattia Savardi,Mani B. Srivastava,Trevor Bihl,Erik P. Blasch,Daniel O. Brigham,Kara Combs,Lance M. Kaplan,Federico Cerutti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文初步分析了Chronos基础模型处理及内部表示频域信息的能力。通过对简单正弦信号进行受控实验，并利用轻量级在线最小描述长度探针分析解码器架构，检测模型内部表示中频率信息的存在性与可分离性。结果揭示了频谱上频率信息的捕获方式，指出了表示质量可能下降的区域，为信号处理与信息融合领域使用Chronos提供了实用指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管处理时间序列数据的基础模型已被广泛采用，但这类模型在多大程度上编码了基本的信号属性（如频率信息）仍缺乏充分表征。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在可控条件下以固定频率的离散正弦信号作为最简单信号类，对Chronos模型进行测试；使用轻量级在线最小描述长度（MDL）探针应用于解码器架构，检测内部表示中频率信息的存在性和可分离性。

Result: 揭示了频域内容在频谱上的捕获方式，并指出了表示质量可能退化或需要特别关注的区域。

Conclusion: 为Chronos用户在信号处理和信息融合场景中提供了实用指导，并有助于推动时间数据基础模型可解释性与评估的持续改进。

Abstract: This paper presents a preliminary analysis of the ability of Chronos foundation model to process and internally represent frequency domain information. Foundation models that process time-series data offer practitioners a unified architecture capable of learning generic temporal representations across diverse tasks and domains, reducing the need for task-specific feature engineering and enabling transfer across signal modalities. Despite their growing adoption, the extent to which such models encode fundamental signal properties remains insufficiently characterised. We address this gap by analysing Chronos under controlled conditions, starting from the simplest class of signals: discrete sinusoids generated at fixed frequencies. Using lightweight online minimum description length probes applied to the decoder architecture, we test for the presence and separability of frequency information in the model's internal representations. The results provide insight into how frequential content is captured across the frequency spectrum and highlight regimes in which representation quality may degrade or require particular care. These findings offer practical guidance for users of Chronos in signal processing and information fusion contexts, and contribute to ongoing efforts to improve the interpretability and evaluation of foundation models for temporal data.

</details>


### [154] [Flow Matching with Arbitrary Auxiliary Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06364)
*Xin Peng,Ang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AuxPath-FM框架，通过引入任意分布的辅助变量η，将条件流匹配推广到更一般的概率路径设计，支持多种先验分布和标签引导生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件流匹配方法仅支持高斯噪声作为辅助变量，限制了概率路径设计的灵活性和多样性，无法充分利用不同分布带来的几何特性及语义信息。

Method: 在条件流匹配中引入服从任意分布的辅助变量η，构造形如X_t = a(t)X_1 + b(t)X_0 + c(t)η的轨迹，理论上证明了连续性方程和训练目标的一致性。

Result: 支持高斯、均匀、拉普拉斯、离散Rademacher等多种先验分布的概率路径设计，并实现标签引导生成等任务。

Conclusion: AuxPath-FM为概率路径设计提供了原则性且通用的理论基础，兼具理论一般性和实践灵活性。

Abstract: We introduce a new generative modeling framework, \textbf{Flow Matching with Arbitrary Auxiliary Paths (AuxPath-FM)}, which generalizes conditional flow matching by incorporating an auxiliary variable drawn from an arbitrary distribution into the probability path. Unlike prior methods that restrict auxiliary components to Gaussian noise, AuxPath-FM allows the variable $η$ to follow any distribution, producing trajectories of the form $X_t = a(t)X_1 + b(t)X_0 + c(t)η$. We theoretically demonstrate that this construction preserves the continuity equation and maintains a training objective consistent with the marginal formulation. This flexibility enables the design of diverse probability paths using various priors, including Gaussian, Uniform, Laplace, and discrete Rademacher distributions, each offering unique geometric properties for generative flows. Furthermore, our framework allows for specialized tasks such as label-guided generation by encoding structured semantic information into the auxiliary distribution. Overall, AuxPath-FM provides a principled and general foundation for probability path design, offering both theoretical generality and practical flexibility for diverse generative modeling tasks.

</details>


### [155] [Data-Driven Covariate Selection for Nonparametric and Cycle-Agnostic Causal Effect Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06385)
*Ana Leticia Garcez Vicente,Gijs van Seeventer,Saber Salehkaleybar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了基于条件独立性的局部协变量选择方法在循环因果模型中同样有效，建立了适用于循环与非循环情景的统一协变量选择视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的因果效应估计方法通常假设无环性（acyclicity）或依赖全局因果结构学习，这在存在潜在混淆和反馈循环的现实场景中计算效率低、适用性有限。需要研究局部、数据驱动的协变量选择方法在循环因果模型中的有效性。

Method: 研究基于条件独立性信息的局部协变量选择方法，利用σ-无环化（σ-acyclification）下条件独立性断言的不变性，将已有的无环因果模型中的完备性保证推广到循环因果模型。

Result: 证明了该局部协变量选择方法在循环因果模型中同样保持可靠性和完备性，并在大规模合成数据上进行了实证验证，展示了在循环因果模型中的可靠性能。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了统一的、对循环结构无感知（cycle-agnostic）的协变量选择和因果效应估计视角，表明该方法无需修改即可同时适用于循环和非循环场景，具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Estimating causal effects from observational data requires identifying valid adjustment sets. This task is especially challenging in realistic settings where latent confounding and feedback loops are present. Existing approaches typically assume acyclicity or rely on global causal structure learning, limiting applicability and computational efficiency. In this work, we study a local, data-driven method for covariate selection based on conditional independence information. While this method is known to be sound and complete in acyclic causal models, its validity in the presence of cycles has remained unclear. Our main contribution is to show that these guarantees extend to cyclic causal models. In particular, our result relies on the invariance of conditional independence assertions under $σ$-acyclification. These findings establish a unified, cycle-agnostic perspective on covariate selection and causal effect estimation, showing that the method applies across cyclic and acyclic settings without modification. Empirically, we validate this on extensive synthetic data, showing reliable performance in cyclic causal models.

</details>


### [156] [Asymmetric On-Policy Distillation: Bridging Exploitation and Imitation at the Token Level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06387)
*Nan Jia,Haojin Yang,Xing Ma,Jiesong Lian,Shuailiang Zhang,Weipeng Zhang,Ke Zeng,Xunliang Cai,Zequn Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出非对称在线策略蒸馏（AOPD），通过用局部散度最小化替代非正优势区域的无效负强化，解决了标准在线策略蒸馏（OPD）在优势加权策略梯度中的高方差、零优势区域梯度消失和探索瓶颈问题，在数学推理任务上平均提升4.09/8.34（强/弱初始化）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准在线策略蒸馏（OPD）虽优于离线蒸馏和标准强化学习，但其优势加权策略梯度存在三个结构性缺陷：高方差更新、零优势区域梯度消失、以及纠正信号不足时的探索瓶颈，亟需改进。

Method: 提出非对称在线策略蒸馏（AOPD），在非正优势区域用局部散度最小化替代无效的负强化，同时保留正优势区域的强化学习。

Result: 在数学推理基准上，AOPD一致优于标准OPD，强/弱初始化下平均提升4.09/8.34；训练期间保持更高策略熵，并在顺序工具使用适应中保持更好的能力保留。

Conclusion: AOPD通过非对称处理正负优势区域，有效克服了OPD的结构性缺陷，提升了算法稳定性、探索能力和性能。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own trajectories with token-level teacher feedback and often outperforms off-policy distillation and standard reinforcement learning. However, we find that its standard advantage weighted policy gradient suffers from three structural weaknesses, including high variance updates, vanishing gradients in zero-advantage regions, and exploration bottlenecks when corrective signals are insufficient.We therefore propose Asymmetric On-Policy Distillation (AOPD), which replaces ineffective negative reinforcement with localized divergence minimization in non-positive advantage regions while preserving positive reinforcement learning. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that AOPD consistently outperforms standard OPD, with average gains of 4.09 / 8.34 under strong / weak initialization, respectively. AOPD also maintains higher policy entropy during training and better capability retention during sequential tool-use adaptation.

</details>


### [157] [SparseForge: Efficient Semi-Structured LLM Sparsification via Annealing of Hessian-Guided Soft-Mask](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06402)
*Liu Hanzuo,Chaofan Lin,Weixuan Sun,Yulong Wang,Key,Rayying,Mingyu Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SparseForge提出了一种面向半结构化稀疏的后训练框架，通过直接优化稀疏掩码（结合Hessian感知重要性估计和软掩码渐进退火），在仅使用5B重训练令牌的情况下，使LLaMA-2-7B在2:4稀疏度下零样本准确率达57.27%，超越稠密模型（56.43%）并接近使用40B令牌的SOTA方法（57.52%），显著提升了精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半结构化稀疏虽能利用硬件加速LLM，但后训练剪枝因结构耦合导致精度严重下降，现有方法依赖大规模稀疏重训练恢复精度，计算成本高昂。

Method: 提出SparseForge框架，直接优化稀疏掩码而非增加重训练令牌量；结合Hessian感知的重要性估计与软掩码渐进退火，使其收敛为硬件可执行的半结构化稀疏模式。

Result: 在LLaMA-2-7B的2:4稀疏度下，SparseForge使用5B令牌达到57.27%零样本准确率，超越稠密模型（56.43%），接近使用40B令牌的SOTA方法（57.52%）。该精度-效率优势在多个模型家族中一致。

Conclusion: SparseForge通过优化掩码而非扩大数据量，实现了高效的后训练稀疏恢复，在半结构化稀疏场景下显著改善了准确率与计算开销之间的权衡。

Abstract: Semi-structured sparsity provides a practical path to accelerate large language models (LLMs) with native hardware support, but post-training semi-structured pruning often suffers from substantial quality degradation due to strong structural coupling. Existing methods rely on large-scale sparse retraining to recover accuracy, resulting in high computational cost.
  We propose SparseForge, a post-training framework that improves recovery efficiency by directly optimizing the sparsity mask rather than scaling up retraining tokens. SparseForge combines Hessian-aware importance estimation with progressive annealing of soft masks into hardware-executable structured sparsity, enabling stable and efficient sparse recovery. On LLaMA-2-7B under 2:4 sparsity, SparseForge achieves 57.27% average zero-shot accuracy with only $\textbf{5B}$ retraining tokens, surpassing the dense model's 56.43% accuracy and approaching the 57.52% result of a state-of-the-art method using $\textbf{40B}$ tokens. Such improvements on the accuracy-efficiency trade-off from SparseForge are shown to be consistent across model families.

</details>


### [158] [FRInGe: Distribution-Space Integrated Gradients with Fisher--Rao Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06404)
*Gabriele Martino,Sebastian Tschiatschek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对积分梯度（IG）在基线选择、积分路径离散化和饱和效应上的脆弱性，提出Fisher-Rao积分梯度（FRInGe）方法，在预测分布空间定义参考点和插值调度，通过Fisher-Rao测地线生成输入空间轨迹，并结合KL和欧几里得信任域稳定归因，在六个ImageNet架构上提升了校准导向的归因指标（尤其是MAS分数），同时在扰动AUC和infidelity上保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度归因方法虽具模型忠实性和可扩展性，但IG因启发式基线、直线路径、离散化和饱和效应而表现脆弱，亟需更鲁棒的解释方法。

Method: 在预测分布空间中定义最大熵参考点，沿Fisher-Rao测地线（概率单纯形上）进行插值；通过拉回Fisher度量得到对应输入空间轨迹，并利用KL散度和欧几里得信任域稳定归因；最终沿该轨迹积分输入梯度获得归因值。

Result: 在六个ImageNet架构上，FRInGe在MAS等校准导向归因指标上提升最显著，同时在扰动AUC和infidelity指标上保持与现有方法相当的性能。

Conclusion: 将归因路径从输入空间迁移到预测分布空间，并采用信息几何中的Fisher-Rao测地线，是提升归因方法鲁棒性和校准性的有效途径。

Abstract: Gradient-based attribution methods are model-faithful and scalable, but Integrated Gradients (IG) can be brittle because explanations depend on heuristic baselines, straight-line paths, discretization, and saturation. We propose Fisher--Rao Integrated Gradients (FRInGe), which defines both the reference and interpolation schedule in predictive distribution space. FRInGe replaces input baselines with a maximum-entropy predictive reference and follows a Fisher-Rao geodesic on the probability simplex. The corresponding input-space trajectory is realized through the pullback Fisher metric and stabilized by KL and Euclidean trust regions; attributions are obtained by integrating input gradients along this trajectory. Across six ImageNet architectures, FRInGe most clearly improves calibration-oriented attribution metrics, especially MAS scores, while remaining competitive on perturbation AUC and infidelity.

</details>


### [159] [E = T*H/(O+B): A Dimensionless Control Parameter for Mixture-of-Experts Ecology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06415)
*Qingjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出无量纲控制参数 E = T*H/(O+B)，用于预测混合专家（MoE）模型是否会形成健康的专家生态或出现死亡专家问题。通过12组受控实验（8个视觉任务、4个语言任务）共计11,000+训练轮次，证明E ≥ 0.5足以保证零死亡专家，无需人工设计的负载均衡辅助损失。此外还发现死专家可复活、正交毒性依赖数据集、任务复杂度改变E临界阈值等六个重要结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型中专家崩溃（死亡专家）是常见问题，现有解决方案依赖手工设计的负载均衡损失函数，缺乏统一的理论指导。作者希望提出一个无量纲控制参数，统一描述和预测MoE训练的专家生态健康状况，消除对人工辅助损失的需求。

Method: 提出控制参数 E = T*H/(O+B)，其中T为路由温度，H为路由熵权重，O为oracle权重，B为平衡权重。在8个视觉任务（CIFAR-10/100, TinyImageNet-200）和4个语言任务（WikiText-2/103）上进行12组受控实验，总计超11,000训练轮次，跨模态验证E与专家生态健康的关系。

Result: E ≥ 0.5足以保证零死亡专家，无需手工负载均衡辅助损失。六项额外发现：(1)死专家可被重新激活；(2)正交毒性具有数据集依赖性；(3)任务复杂度影响E临界阈值；(4)过拟合与专家生态健康解耦；(5)三层MoE自发坍缩为两层功能结构；(6)专家生态结构在50倍温度范围内保持不变。

Conclusion: E可作为MoE训练的统一诊断指标，其作用类似于流体力学中的雷诺数。该参数简化了MoE训练的超参数调优过程，并为理解专家生态动力学提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We introduce E = T*H/(O+B), a dimensionless control parameter that predicts whether Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models will develop a healthy expert ecology or collapse into dead experts. E combines four hyperparameters -- routing temperature T, routing entropy weight H, oracle weight O, and balance weight B -- into a single quantity. Through 12 controlled experiments (8 vision, 4 language) totaling over 11,000 training epochs, we establish that E >= 0.5 alone is sufficient to guarantee zero dead experts, removing the necessity for handcrafted load-balancing auxiliary losses. We validate this cross-modally on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, TinyImageNet-200, WikiText-2, and WikiText-103. Six additional findings emerge: (1) dead experts can resuscitate -- triggered by balance loss driving router re-exploration; (2) ortho toxicity is dataset-dependent, not universal; (3) task complexity shifts the critical E threshold; (4) model overfitting is decoupled from expert ecological health; (5) three-tier MoE spontaneously collapses into a two-tier functional structure; (6) ecological structure is temperature-invariant across a 50x range. We propose that E serves as a unified diagnostic for MoE training, analogous to the Reynolds number in fluid dynamics.

</details>


### [160] [Federated Cross-Client Subgraph Pattern Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06433)
*Selin Ceydeli,Rui Wang,Kubilay Atasu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对分布式图场景下GNN子图检测的表示等价差距问题，本文提出了一种逐层嵌入交换框架，通过在前向传播每层同步中间节点表示，恢复与集中式GNN相同的表示能力，实验表明嵌入交换与联邦参数聚合互补而非可互换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GNN的子图模式检测方法假设图可集中访问，但实际中图常分布在多方。客户端本地GNN计算与集中式模型存在表示等价差距，导致跨分区边界的子图模式局部不可识别。

Method: 形式化为结构可观测性问题，提出逐层嵌入交换框架：客户端在前向传播每层同步中间节点表示，不暴露原始特征或标签，在扩展子图假设和共享模型参数下恢复集中式GNN表示。

Result: 在合成有向多重图（含环、双团、散聚模式）上实验表明，嵌入交换与联邦参数聚合互补，且仅在每步新鲜交换而非每轮陈旧交换时才能恢复大部分表示差距。

Conclusion: 逐层嵌入交换能有效桥接分布式图GNN的表示等价差距，且与联邦学习中的参数聚合机制互补。

Abstract: Subgraph pattern detection aims to uncover complex interaction structures in graphs. However, state-of-the-art graph neural network (GNN)-based solutions assume centralized access to the entire graph. When graphs are instead distributed across multiple parties, client-local GNN computations diverge from those of a centralized model, resulting in a representation-equivalence gap. We formalize this as a structural observability problem, where subgraph patterns crossing partition boundaries become locally unidentifiable. To bridge this gap, we propose a per-step, layer-wise embedding exchange framework in which clients synchronize intermediate node representations at each layer of the forward pass, without exposing raw features or labels. Under an extended-subgraph assumption and shared model parameters across clients, this framework recovers the same node representations as a centralized GNN over the full graph. Experiments on synthetic directed multigraphs with cycles, bicliques, and scatter-gather patterns show that embedding exchange and federated parameter aggregation are complementary rather than interchangeable: their combination recovers most of the representation gap, provided exchanged embeddings are fresh per-step rather than stale per-epoch.

</details>


### [161] [Hyperbolic Concept Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06440)
*Daniel Uyterlinde,Swasti Shreya Mishra,Pascal Mettes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出双曲概念瓶颈模型(HypCBM)，将概念嵌入从欧氏空间迁移到双曲空间，利用蕴含锥的几何包含关系实现层级感知的稀疏激活，无需额外监督即可实现层级一致的概念推理与干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念瓶颈模型(CBM)将概念嵌入在平坦欧氏空间中，视其为独立正交维度，忽略了概念本身具有的语义层级结构（如"狗"包含"柯基"），导致模型表示与人类认知结构不匹配。

Method: 1) 将概念激活重新形式化为双曲空间中蕴含锥内的非对称几何包含关系；2) 利用推理阶段概念蕴含锥的包含边际作为稀疏、层级感知的激活信号，无需额外监督或学习模块；3) 提出自适应缩放律，实现层级一致的用户干预传播。

Result: 在稀疏场景下，HypCBM仅需欧氏后验模型1/20的训练数据即可达到可比性能；层级一致性更强；对输入损坏的鲁棒性更好。

Conclusion: 将概念瓶颈嵌入双曲空间能够更自然地建模概念的层级结构，在保持可解释性的同时提升数据效率和鲁棒性，为概念瓶颈模型的设计提供了几何化的新范式。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) have become a popular approach to enable interpretability in neural networks by constraining classifier inputs to a set of human-understandable concepts. While effective, current models embed concepts in flat Euclidean space, treating them as independent, orthogonal dimensions. Concepts, however, are highly structured and organized in semantic hierarchies. To resolve this mismatch, we propose Hyperbolic Concept Bottleneck Models (HypCBM), a post-hoc framework that grounds the bottleneck in this structure by reformulating concept activation as asymmetric geometric containment in hyperbolic space. Rather than treating entailment cones as a pre-training penalty, we show they encode a natural test-time activation signal: the margin of inclusion within a concept's entailment cone yields sparse, hierarchy-aware activations without any additional supervision or learned modules. We further introduce an adaptive scaling law for hierarchically faithful interventions, propagating user corrections coherently through the concept tree. Empirically, HypCBM rivals post-hoc Euclidean models trained on 20$\times$ more data in sparse regimes required for human interpretability, with stronger hierarchical consistency and improved robustness to input corruptions.

</details>


### [162] [FedFrozen: Two-Stage Federated Optimization via Attention Kernel Freezing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06446)
*Junye Du,Zhenghao Li,Yushi Feng,Long Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedFrozen两阶段联邦优化框架：在全模型预热训练后冻结注意力机制的query/key模块，仅优化value模块。理论分析表明该策略能在异质性联邦学习中减少客户端漂移，实验验证了其提升Transformer模型训练稳定性与效果的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦优化方法主要通过目标正则化或更新校正机制处理客户端漂移问题，但观察到Transformer架构在异质联邦训练中天然更鲁棒。据此动机，作者探究注意力机制中不同参数组件对联邦优化的影响，旨在设计更高效的异质联邦学习方法。

Method: 将注意力模块分解为query/key块（决定注意力核）和value块（在诱导核下执行语义变换）。提出FedFrozen两阶段框架：阶段一进行全模型预热训练；阶段二冻结query/key块，仅优化value块。在线性注意力设定下，将预热阶段解释为正则化核轮廓目标上的非精确下降过程，冻结阶段简化为固定注意力核下的受限value块优化问题。

Result: 理论分析揭示了控制预热长度的显式权衡关系。仿真实验验证了预测的偏差漂移行为，真实数据实验表明FedFrozen在异质联邦学习中提升了Transformer模型的训练稳定性和效果。

Conclusion: 通过解耦注意力机制中不同参数组件的联邦学习特性，FedFrozen以两阶段冻结策略有效缓解了客户端漂移问题，为异质性联邦学习中的Transformer优化提供了新的理论视角和实用方法。

Abstract: Federated learning with heterogeneous clients remains a significant challenge for deep learning, primarily due to client drift arising from inconsistent local updates. Existing federated optimization methods typically address this issue through objective-level regularization or update-correction mechanisms. Recent studies, however, suggest that Transformer-based architectures may be inherently more robust than conventional models under heterogeneous federated training. Motivated by this observation, we investigate how different parameter components within the attention mechanism influence federated optimization. Specifically, we decompose the attention module into a query/key block, which determines the attention kernel, and a value block, which performs semantic transformation under the induced kernel. Based on this perspective, we propose FedFrozen, a two-stage federated optimization framework that first performs full-model warm-up training and then freezes the query/key block while continuing to optimize the value block. Under a linear-attention formulation, we show that the warm-up stage can be interpreted as an inexact descent procedure on a regularized kernel-profile objective, while the frozen stage reduces to a restricted value-block optimization problem under a fixed attention kernel. Our analysis further reveals an explicit trade-off that governs the choice of warm-up length. Simulations validate the predicted bias-drift behavior, and real-data experiments demonstrate that FedFrozen improves both the stability and effectiveness of Transformer models in heterogeneous federated learning.

</details>


### [163] [Scene-Adaptive Continual Learning for CSI-based Human Activity Recognition with Mixture of Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06447)
*Wenhan Zheng,Yuyi Mao,Ivan Wang-Hei Ho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SAMoE-C，一种基于混合专家系统和聚类专家的跨域CSI人体活动识别方法，通过注意力语义路由器实现场景自适应激活，仅需极小的重放缓冲区即可稳定域判别，在四场景数据集上达到接近最优的精度并显著降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于CSI的人体活动识别在不同物理环境下面临域偏移导致的性能退化问题，现有持续学习方案存在扩展性差、依赖大容量重放缓冲区或推理成本线性增长等局限。

Method: SAMoE-C将跨域CSI-HAR建模为混合专家系统，采用基于注意力的语义路由器对每个输入仅激活选中的专家，同时设计新颖的训练协议，仅需极小的重放缓冲区来稳定路由器的域判别能力。

Result: 在四场景CSI数据集上，SAMoE-C在接近最优精度的同时，显著降低了推理成本。

Conclusion: 通过结合模块化专家、选择性激活路由器和轻量训练协议，SAMoE-C实现了可扩展的跨域CSI-HAR部署，具备低训练开销和高计算效率，适用于实际场景。

Abstract: Channel state information (CSI)-based human activity recognition (HAR) is vulnerable to performance degradation under domain shifts across varying physical environments. Continual learning (CL) offers a principled way to learn new domains sequentially while preserving past knowledge, but existing CL solutions for CSI-based HAR scale poorly with accumulating domains, rely on a large replay buffer, or incur linearly growing inference cost. In this letter, we propose Scene-Adaptive Mixture of Experts with Clustered Specialists (SAMoE-C), which formulates cross-domain CSI-based HAR as a mixture-of-experts system that enables scene-specific adaptation, via an attention-based semantic router that activates only selected experts for each input. Moreover, we develop a novel training protocol, which requires only a tiny replay buffer for stabilizing domain discrimination of the router. Experimental results on a four-scene CSI dataset demonstrate that SAMoE-C approaches the state-of-the-art accuracy, while maintaining a significantly lower inference cost. By jointly combining modular experts, selective activation with router and a lightweight training protocol, SAMoE-C enables scalable cross-domain CSI-based HAR deployment with low training overhead and high computational efficiency in real-world settings.

</details>


### [164] [Invariant Features in Language Models: Geometric Characterization and Model Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06458)
*Agnibh Dasgupta,Abdullah Tanvir,Xin Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出语义不变性是语言模型潜在空间中局部几何结构的结果，并通过对比子空间发现方法分离语义变化与语义保持的变异，验证了不变表示在零样本模型归因中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型对释义（paraphrasing）表现出强鲁棒性，表明语义信息可能通过稳定的内部表示编码，但此类不变性的结构和起源尚不明确。

Method: (1) 对不变潜在特征进行几何刻画；(2) 提出对比性子空间发现方法，分离语义变化与语义保持的变异；(3) 将不变表示应用于零样本模型归因。

Result: 不变结构出现在特定深度区域；语义位移主要位于干扰子空间之外；表示层面的干预表明不变成分在模型输出中发挥因果作用；不变表示捕获模型特定的几何模式，实现准确归因。

Conclusion: 语义不变性可被视为潜在表征的局部几何属性，为理解语言模型如何组织意义提供了一种原理性视角。

Abstract: Language models exhibit strong robustness to paraphrasing, suggesting that semantic information may be encoded through stable internal representations, yet the structure and origin of such invariance remain unclear. We propose a local geometric framework in which semantically equivalent inputs occupy structured regions in latent space, with paraphrastic variation along nuisance directions and semantic identity preserved in invariant subspaces. Building on this view, we make three contributions: (1) a geometric characterization of invariant latent features, (2) a contrastive subspace discovery method that separates semantic-changing from semantic-preserving variation, and (3) an application of invariant representations to zero-shot model attribution. Across models and layers, empirical results support these contributions. Invariant structure emerges in specific depth regions, semantic displacement lies largely outside the nuisance subspace, and representation-level interventions indicate a causal role of invariant components in model outputs. Invariant representations also capture model-specific geometric patterns, enabling accurate attribution. These findings suggest that semantic invariance can be viewed as a local geometric property of latent representations, offering a principled perspective on how language models organize meaning.

</details>


### [165] [Diversity Curves for Graph Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06466)
*Katharina Limbeck,Nadja Häusermann,Martin Carrasco,Guy Wolf,Bastian Rieck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“多样性曲线”（diversity curves）作为图级别的表示方法，通过追踪图在粗化层级上的结构多样性来生成可解释、可扩展、直接可比且尺寸感知的图嵌入，并利用边收缩粗化提升表达能力，在多个任务中优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图级表示方法难以处理不同基数的图之间的比较，尤其是在无监督任务中缺乏可解释、可扩展且可靠的尺寸感知图表示。

Method: 通过追踪图在边收缩粗化层级上的结构多样性（即图的“散布度”，一种新的等距不变量），构建被称为“多样性曲线”的图嵌入，并证明边收缩粗化能提升表达能力。

Result: 多样性曲线在聚类与可视化模拟图、区分单细胞图几何结构、比较分子图数据集结构以及表征几何形状等多个任务中优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 多样性曲线是一种可解释、高效且直接可比的图级表示方法，通过粗化层级追踪结构多样性，有效解决了不同基数图的比较问题。

Abstract: Graph-level representations are crucial tools for characterising structural differences between graphs. However, comparing graphs with different cardinalities, even when sampled from the same underlying distribution, remains challenging. Unsupervised tasks in particular require interpretable, scalable, and reliable size-aware graph representations. Our work addresses these issues by tracking the structural diversity of a graph across coarsening levels. The resulting graph embeddings, which we denote diversity curves, are interpretable by construction, efficient, and directly comparable across coarsening hierarchies. Specifically, we track the spread of graphs, a novel isometry invariant that is inherently well-suited for encoding the metric diversity and geometry of graphs. We utilise edge contraction coarsening and prove that this improves expressivity, thus leading to more powerful graph-level representations than structural descriptors alone. Demonstrating their utility over a range of baseline methods in practice, we use diversity curves to (i) cluster and visualise simulated graphs across varying sizes, (ii) distinguish the geometry of single-cell graphs, (iii) compare the structure of molecular graph datasets, and (iv) characterise geometric shapes.

</details>


### [166] [Hitting Time Isomorphism for Multi-Stage Planning with Foundation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06470)
*Magnus Victor Boock,Abdullah Akgül,Mustafa Mert Çelikok,Melih Kandemir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于算子理论的离线强化学习表示学习框架，通过击中时间观测恢复受控马尔可夫过程的有向时间几何结构，并据此设计了同构嵌入学习（IEL）算法，在离线迷宫导航任务上达到了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法常产生对称距离或不满足三角不等式，无法准确刻画有向时间几何结构，本文旨在学习一种希尔伯特空间位移几何，使期望击中时间可表示为潜在位移的线性泛函。

Method: 提出算子理论表示学习框架，证明潜在线性闭包下表示存在且唯一（至多相差有界线性同构）；给出有限维实现中击中时间误差界（由一步转移误差与环境瞬态谱半径放大）；提供有限样本保证（近似误差、统计复杂度、轨迹标签错配）；据此设计IEL算法，结合HILP一致性目标与显式击中时间回归。

Result: IEL在离线迷宫导航数据上学习基础策略（foundation policy）达到SOTA，代码已开源。

Conclusion: 不对称且可组合的几何结构能够实现鲁棒的基于图的多阶段长程导航规划。

Abstract: We present a new operator-theoretic representation learning framework for offline reinforcement learning that recovers the directed temporal geometry of a controlled Markov process from hitting time observations. While prior art often produces symmetric distances or fails to satisfy the triangle inequality, our framework learns a Hilbert-space displacement geometry where expected hitting times are realized as linear functionals of latent displacements. We prove that this representation exists under latent linear closure and is uniquely identifiable up to a bounded linear isomorphism. For finite-dimensional implementations, we show that global hitting-time error is bounded by one-step transition error amplified by the environment's transient spectral radius. Furthermore, we provide finite-sample guarantees accounting for approximation, statistical complexity, and trajectory-label mismatch. Derived from this theory, we curate Isomorphic Embedding Learning (IEL) as a new goal-agnostic foundation policy learning algorithm that anchors a HILP-style consistency objective with explicit hitting-time regression to ensure that the learned geometry reflects actual decision-time progress. This asymmetric and compositional structure enables robust graph-based multi-stage planning for long-horizon navigation. Our experiments demonstrate that IEL improves the state of the art of learning foundation policy policies from offline maze locomotion data. Our code can be found on https://github.com/MagnusBoock/IEL

</details>


### [167] [Efficient Serving for Dynamic Agent Workflows with Prediction-based KV-Cache Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06472)
*Haoyu Zheng,Fangcheng Fu,Jia Wu,Binhang Yuan,Yongqiang Zhang,Hao Wang,Yuanyuan Zhu,Xiao Yan,Jiawei Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PBKV是一种基于预测的KV-Cache管理系统，通过预测动态工作流中的智能体调用序列来优化缓存复用，在动态工作流上相比LRU实现最高1.85倍加速，在静态工作流上相比SOTA基线KVFlow实现最高1.26倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM工作流中，智能体间存在大量上下文共享使得KV-Cache复用可节省计算。但现有方法要么在智能体级别管理缓存而无法利用工作流内复用机会，要么在工作流级别管理但假设调用序列是静态的。实际工作流通常是动态的，智能体调用序列和缓存复用机会依赖于具体任务上下文，现有方法无法高效处理这种动态性。

Method: PBKV系统通过融合历史工作流的引导和目标工作流的上下文，预测未来几步的智能体调用序列。基于预测结果评估缓存条目的复用潜力，将高潜力条目保留在GPU内存中。同时，在缓存淘汰和预取过程中保守地使用预测结果，以增强对预测误差的鲁棒性。

Result: 在三个工作流基准测试上，PBKV在动态工作流上相比LRU实现最高1.85倍加速，在静态工作流上相比SOTA基线KVFlow实现最高1.26倍加速。

Conclusion: PBKV通过预测驱动的缓存管理策略，有效解决了动态工作流中KV-Cache复用效率低下的问题，兼顾了动态场景下的适应性和静态场景下的高性能。

Abstract: LLM-based workflows compose specialized agents to execute complex tasks, and these agents usually share substantial context, allowing KV-Cache reuse to save computation. Existing approaches either manage KV-Cache at agent level and fail to exploit the reuse opportunities within workflows, or manage cache at the workflow level but assume that each workflow calls a static sequence of agents. However, practical workflows are typically dynamic, where the sequence of invoked agents and thus induced cache reuse opportunities depend on the context of each task. To serve such dynamic workflows efficiently, we build a system dubbed PBKV (\textbf{P}rediction-\textbf{B}ased \textbf{KV}-Cache Management). For each workflow, PBKV predicts the agent invocations in several future steps by fusing the guidance from historical workflows and context of the target workflow. Based on the predictions, PBKV estimates the reuse potential of cache entries and keeps the high-potential entries in GPU memory. To be robust to prediction errors, PBKV utilizes the predictions conservatively during both cache eviction and prefetching. Experiments on three workflow benchmarks show that PBKV achieves up to $1.85\times$ speedup over LRU on dynamic workflows, and up to $1.26\times$ speedup over the SOTA baseline KVFlow on the static workflow.

</details>


### [168] [Q-MMR: Off-Policy Evaluation via Recursive Reweighting and Moment Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06474)
*Xiang Li,Nan Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Q-MMR理论框架，通过学到的数据点权重进行矩匹配，实现离线策略评估，在仅需$Q^\pi$可实现性条件下获得与函数类统计复杂度无关的有限样本保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线策略评估方法在函数近似下的理论保证往往依赖于函数类的统计复杂度或严格的覆盖条件，本文旨在建立一种更宽松的理论保证框架。

Method: Q-MMR通过矩匹配目标学习每个数据点的标量权重，以加权奖励近似目标策略下的期望回报；权重以自上而下的归纳方式学习，使用值函数判别器类。

Result: 建立了仅依赖于$Q^\pi$可实现性的数据依赖有限样本保证，且误差界与函数类统计复杂度无关；与重要性采样和线性FQE等方法建立了联系；对覆盖条件提供了新的理论见解。

Conclusion: Q-MMR为离线策略评估提供了一种新的理论视角，在更弱的假设下实现了维度无关的误差界，并深化了对离线强化学习中覆盖概念的理解。

Abstract: We present a novel theoretical framework, Q-MMR, for off-policy evaluation in finite-horizon MDPs. Q-MMR learns a set of scalar weights, one for each data point, such that the reweighted rewards approximate the expected return under the target policy. The weights are learned inductively in a top-down manner via a moment matching objective against a value-function discriminator class. Notably, and perhaps surprisingly, a data-dependent finite-sample guarantee for general function approximation can be established under only the realizability of $Q^π$, with a dimension-free bound -- that is, the error does not depend on the statistical complexity of the function class. We also establish connections to several existing methods, such as importance sampling and linear FQE. Further theoretical analyses shed new light on the nature of coverage, a concept of fundamental importance to offline RL.

</details>


### [169] [Operator-Guided Invariance Learning for Continuous Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06500)
*Zuyuan Zhang,Fei Xu Yu,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出VPSD-RL框架，利用李群作用和拉回算子在连续时间/状态/动作空间的强化学习中自动发现精确和近似的保价值结构，通过微分生成元学习与ODE流指数映射实现变换增强，提升数据效率与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间/状态/动作空间的RL通常数据密集且对 nuisance 变化和分布偏移脆弱，现有方法多集中于预设对称性和精确等变性，缺乏自动发现更一般保价值结构的通用方法。

Method: 将连续RL建模为受控扩散过程，定义李群作用下的保价值映射；证明保价值结构等价于拉回价值函数与前推动作与受控生成元及奖励泛函对易；通过HJB残差最小化给出近似保价值结构的严格保证；拟合可微漂移/扩散/奖励模型，通过确定方程残差最小化学习无穷小生成元，利用ODE流指数映射获得有限变换，并通过变换增强和变换一致性正则化融入RL训练。

Result: 证明了有界生成元/奖励失配下最优值函数沿近似轨道的定量稳定性，灵敏度由有效视界控制；在连续控制基准上观察到数据效率和鲁棒性的提升。

Conclusion: VPSD-RL为连续RL中的保价值结构发现提供了统一的理论框架与可实现的算法，兼顾精确与近似情形，在理论上保证了近似结构的稳定性，实验上验证了其数据效率和鲁棒性优势。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) with continuous time and state/action spaces is often data-intensive and brittle under nuisance variability and shift, motivating methods that exploit value-preserving structures to stabilize and improve learning. Most existing approaches focus on special cases, such as prescribed symmetries and exact equivariance, without addressing how to discover more general structures that require nonlinear operators to transform and map between continuous state/action systems with isomorphic value functions. We propose \textbf{VPSD-RL} (Value-Preserving Structure Discovery for Reinforcement Learning). It models continuous RL as a controlled diffusion with value-preserving mappings defined through Lie-group actions and associated pullback operators. We show that a value-preserving structure exists exactly when pulling back the value function and pushing forward actions commute with the controlled generator and reward functional. Further, approximate value-preserving structures with rigorous guarantees can be found when the Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman mismatch is small. This framework discovers exact and approximate value-preserving structures by searching for the associated Lie group operators. VPSD-RL fits differentiable drift, diffusion, and reward models; learns infinitesimal generators via determining-equation residual minimization; exponentiates them with ODE flows to obtain finite transformations; and integrates them into continuous RL through transition augmentation and transformation-consistency regularization. We show that bounded generator/reward mismatch implies quantitative stability of the optimal value function along approximate orbits, with sensitivity governed by the effective horizon, and observe improved data efficiency and robustness on continuous-control benchmarks.

</details>


### [170] [Cubit: Token Mixer with Kernel Ridge Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06501)
*Chuanyang Zheng,Jiankai Sun,Yihang Gao,Yuehao Wang,Liangchen Tan,Mac Schwager,Anderson Schneider,Yuriy Nevmyvaka,Xiaodong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Cubit，一种基于核岭回归（KRR）的下一代 Transformer 架构，替代了传统注意力机制中的 Nadaraya-Watson 回归，通过引入核矩阵的闭式解来增强长序列建模能力，并设计了 Limited-Range Rescale (LRR) 提升训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 Transformer 的注意力机制可被解释为 Nadaraya-Watson 回归，其数学基础较弱。本文旨在提出一种具有更强数学基础的架构，通过将注意力机制替换为核岭回归（KRR）来提升模型的表示能力和长序列建模性能。

Method: 1. 将注意力机制重新解释为 Nadaraya-Watson 回归；2. 提出 Cubit 架构，利用核岭回归的闭式解替换传统注意力计算，通过核相似度聚合值并结合核矩阵的逆进行归一化；3. 提出 Limited-Range Rescale (LRR) 方法，将值层控制在有限范围内以提升训练稳定性。

Result: 实验表明，Cubit 在长序列建模任务上优于传统 Transformer，且性能增益随训练序列长度增长而增加，表现出更强的长序列建模能力。

Conclusion: Cubit 作为一种基于 KRR 的架构，相比基于 Nadaraya-Watson 回归的 Transformer 具有更强的数学基础和长序列建模潜力，有望成为下一代深度学习架构。

Abstract: Since its introduction in 2017, the Transformer has become one of the most widely adopted architectures in modern deep learning. Despite extensive efforts to improve positional encoding, attention mechanisms, and feed-forward networks, the core token-mixing mechanism in Transformers remains attention. In this work, we show that the attention module in Transformers can be interpreted as performing Nadaraya-Watson regression, where it computes similarities between tokens and aggregates the corresponding values accordingly. Motivated by this perspective, we propose Cubit, a potential next-generation architecture that leverages Kernel Ridge Regression (KRR), while the vanilla Transformer relies on Nadaraya-Watson regression. Specifically, Cubit modifies the classical attention computation by incorporating the closed-form solution of KRR, combining value aggregation through kernel similarities with normalization via the inverse of the kernel matrix. To improve the training stability, we further propose the Limited-Range Rescale (LRR), which rescales the value layer within a controlled range. We argue that Cubit, as a KRR-based architecture, provides a stronger mathematical foundation than the vanilla Transformer, whose attention mechanism corresponds to Nadaraya-Watson regression. We validate this claim through comprehensive experiments. The experimental results suggest that Cubit may exhibit stronger long-sequence modeling capability. In particular, its performance gain over the Transformer appears to increase as the training sequence length grows.

</details>


### [171] [Is One Layer Enough? Understanding Inference Dynamics in Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06510)
*Amir Rezaei Balef,Mykhailo Koshil,Katharina Eggensperger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次大规模研究了6种表格上下文学习模型的逐层推理机制，发现深度方向存在显著冗余，并据此设计了一个仅需20%参数的单层循环模型，性能与原始模型相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于Transformer的表格基础模型在小到中等规模的表格预测任务中表现优异，但其推理机制尚未被充分探索。

Method: 对6种SOTA表格上下文学习模型进行大规模逐层机制分析，研究预测如何随深度涌现，识别推理的不同阶段，揭示与语言模型不同的潜空间动态，并基于发现的深度冗余设计单层循环模型。

Result: 多个模型存在显著的深度方向冗余，推理阶段表现为带有重叠计算的迭代精炼过程；单层循环模型仅用20%的参数即可达到可比性能。

Conclusion: 表格基础模型中的深度层存在冗余，可通过循环单层结构大幅压缩参数而不显著损失性能。

Abstract: Transformer-based tabular foundation models (TFMs) dominate small to medium tabular predictive benchmark tasks, yet their inference mechanisms remain largely unexplored. We present the first large-scale mechanistic study of layerwise dynamics in 6 state-of-the-art tabular in-context learning models. We explore how predictions emerge across depth, identify distinct stages of inference and reveal latent-space dynamics that differ from those of language models. Our findings indicate substantial depthwise redundancy across multiple models, suggesting iterative refinement with overlapping computations during inference stages. Guided by these insights, we design a proof-of-concept, looped single-layer model that uses only 20% of the original model's parameters while achieving comparable performance. The code is available at https://github.com/amirbalef/is_one_layer_enough.

</details>


### [172] [Agentic AIs Are the Missing Paradigm for Out-of-Distribution Generalization in Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06522)
*Xin Wang,Haibo Chen,Wenxuan Liu,Wenwu Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证了基础模型面临的分布外问题在结构上不同于传统OOD，模型中心方法存在参数覆盖天花板，而智能体系统作为补充范式是解决该问题的必要方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在开放世界部署中面临知识边界、能力上限、组合偏移和开放任务变化等新型分布偏移问题，且预训练与后训练分布往往仅被部分观测，现有模型中心范式无法解决这些结构上不同的OOD问题。

Method: 通过四步论证：(1)提出阶段感知的OOD形式化框架，容纳部分观测的多阶段训练分布；(2)证明参数覆盖天花板定理，说明存在模型中心方法（无论训练时还是测试时）在容忍度ε内无法处理的输入；(3)刻画智能体OOD系统的四个结构属性：感知、策略选择、外部行动和闭环验证，并证明其严格扩展了可达集；(4)回应七个反论证，承认其中两个，并勾勒研究议程。

Result: 证明模型中心方法存在固有的参数覆盖天花板；智能体系统能严格扩展可达集；模型中心与智能体范式是互补关系而非替代关系。

Conclusion: 基础模型的OOD是一个结构独特的难题，需要明确将智能体范式作为第一类研究方向与模型中心方法互补推进。

Abstract: Foundation models (FMs) are increasingly deployed in open-world settings where distribution shift is the rule rather than the exception. The out-of-distribution (OOD) phenomena they face -- knowledge boundaries, capability ceilings, compositional shifts, and open-ended task variation -- differ in kind from the settings that have shaped prior OOD research, and are further complicated because the pretraining and post-training distributions of modern FMs are often only partially observed. Our position is that OOD for foundation models is a structurally distinct problem that cannot be solved within the prevailing model-centric paradigm, and that agentic systems constitute the missing paradigm required to address it. We defend this claim through four steps. First, we give a stage-aware formalization of OOD that accommodates partially observed multi-stage training distributions. Second, we prove a parameter coverage ceiling: there exist practically relevant inputs that no model-centric method (training-time or test-time) can handle within tolerance $\varepsilon$, for reasons intrinsic to parameter-based representation. Third, we characterize agentic OOD systems by four structural properties -- perception, strategy selection, external action, and closed-loop verification -- and show that they strictly extend the reachable set beyond the ceiling. Fourth, we respond to seven counterarguments, conceding two, and outline a research agenda. We do not claim that agentic methods subsume model-centric ones; we argue that the two are complementary, and that progress on FM-OOD requires explicit recognition of the agentic paradigm as a first-class research direction.

</details>


### [173] [On the Implicit Reward Overfitting and the Low-rank Dynamics in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06523)
*Hao Ye,Jisheng Dang,Junfeng Fang,Bimei Wang,Yizhou Zhang,Ning Lv,Wencan Zhang,Hong Peng,Bin Hu,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过周期性秩-1替换发现RLVR存在隐式奖励过拟合现象，并揭示了RLVR训练的三个关键特性：秩-1组件仅保留数学推理能力、奇异值呈现重尾分布、左奇异向量在训练中表现出更强的对齐趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索RLVR训练中模型参数变化的本质规律，特别是秩-1组件的作用机制及其对模型行为的影响。

Method: 采用周期性秩-1替换方法，通过观察训练过程中奖励与测试性能的关系，并结合奇异值分解分析线性层的谱特性。

Result: 发现RLVR存在隐式奖励过拟合（训练奖励低但测试性能好）；秩-1组件不保留除数学推理外的其他知识；训练后模型线性层的奇异值呈重尾分布；秩-1组件的左奇异向量在训练中表现出更强的对齐趋势。

Conclusion: RLVR本质上通过优化特定奇异谱、提升采样效率来塑造模型参数，这些发现为改进现有强化学习范式及实现持续学习提供了潜在启示。

Abstract: Recent extensive research has demonstrated that the enhanced reasoning capabilities acquired by models through Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) are primarily concentrated within the rank-1 components. Predicated on this observation, we employed Periodic Rank-1 Substitution and identified a counterintuitive phenomenon: RLVR may exhibit implicit reward overfitting to the training dataset. Specifically, the model can achieve satisfactory performance on the test set even when its rewards remain relatively low during the training process. Furthermore, we characterize three distinct properties of RL training: (1) The effective rank-1 component in RLVR don't maintain other model knowledge except mathematical reasoning capability. (2) RLVR fundamentally functions by optimizing a specific singular spectrum. The distribution of singular values of almost all linear layers in RLVR-trained model behaves like heavy-tailed distribution. (3) the left singular vectors associated with rank-1 components demonstrate a stronger alignment tendency during training, which echoes the discovery that RLVR is optimizing sampling efficiency in essence. Taken together, our findings and analysis further reveal how RLVR shapes model parameters and offer potential insights for improving existing RL paradigms or other training paradigms to implement continual learning.

</details>


### [174] [Diffusion-Based Posterior Sampling: A Feynman-Kac Analysis of Bias and Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06538)
*Matias G. Delgadino,Sebastien Motsch,Advait Parulekar,William Porteous,Sanjay Shakkottai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过引入可处理的替代路径和Feynman-Kac表示，系统刻画了扩散后验采样器（DPS和STSL）的偏差，揭示了偏差来自数据条件协方差与奖励曲率的耦合，并解释了早期引导停止等缓解策略的原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散后验采样器被广泛用于逆问题，但其理论行为（偏差来源、低温不稳定性）尚未被充分理解，缺乏严格的偏差刻画框架。

Method: 引入连接真实后验与标准高斯的可处理替代路径，与采样器路径比较；密度比满足抛物型PDE，反应项衡量累积偏差；通过Feynman-Kac表示给出Radon-Nikodym修正的显式路径期望。

Result: 对DPS，修正项是Ornstein-Uhlenbeck路径期望，耦合数据条件协方差与奖励曲率；STSL的辅助漂移可解释为将轨迹导向低不确定区域，平坦化DPS反应项的空间变化部分；早期引导停止可缓解前向欧拉积分导致的低温不稳定性。

Conclusion: 该框架阐明了采样器偏差的本质，解释了现有修正策略的原理，并为设计稳定的变体提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Diffusion-based posterior samplers use pretrained diffusion priors to sample from measurement- or reward-conditioned posteriors, and are widely used for inverse problems. Yet their theoretical behavior remains poorly understood: even with exact prior scores, their outputs are biased, and in low-temperature regimes their discretizations can become unstable. We characterize this bias by introducing a tractable surrogate path connecting the true posterior to a standard Gaussian and comparing it to the sampler's path. Their density ratio satisfies a parabolic PDE whose reaction term measures the accumulated bias. A Feynman-Kac representation then expresses the Radon-Nikodym correction as an explicit path expectation, identifying which posterior regions are over- or under-sampled.
  We apply this framework to DPS and STSL, a related sampler. For DPS, the correction is an Ornstein-Uhlenbeck path expectation coupling the data conditional covariance with the reward curvature, revealing where DPS over- or under-samples. Next, we reinterpret STSL as an auxiliary drift that steers trajectories toward low-uncertainty regions, flattening the spatially varying part of the DPS reaction term. Finally, we characterize early guidance-stopping, a common mitigation for low-temperature instabilities caused by forward-Euler integration of the vector field. Together, these results clarify sampler bias, explain existing correctives, and guide stable variant designs.

</details>


### [175] [Hedging Memory Horizons for Non-Stationary Prediction via Online Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06541)
*Yutong Wang,Yannig Goude,Qiwei Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MELO方法，通过记忆对冲的指数加权最小二乘在线聚合，在分布漂移下实现自适应预测，不依赖先验知识或模型重训，在法国电力负荷COVID预测中降低RMSE 34.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线预测中分布漂移普遍存在，预测器需要在平稳期保持稳定、在漂移期快速适应，但最优适应记忆长度未知，现有方法难以自动平衡这种权衡。

Method: MELO方法：对任意非预期基础预测器池，构造多个遗忘因子的EWLS适应专家（指数加权最小二乘），再通过无参数在线聚合规则MLpol聚合原始预测与EWLS适应预测，实现跨适应尺度的对冲。

Result: 在法国COVID-19封锁期间电力负荷预测中，无需任何外部指标（封锁日期、政策协变量），MELO相比仅用基础预测器的MLpol降低RMSE 34.7%，且优于引入外部COVID政策协变量的TabICL基线。

Conclusion: MELO提供了一种轻量级、无需重训的在线聚合方案，在分布漂移场景下能自动匹配最优适应尺度，理论上有确定性oracle不等式保证，实践上计算开销低。

Abstract: We study online prediction under distribution shift, where inputs arrive chronologically and outcomes are revealed only after prediction. In this setting, predictors must remain stable in quiet regimes yet adapt when regimes shift, and the right adaptation memory is unknown in advance. We propose MELO (Memory-hedged Exponentially Weighted Least-Squares Online aggregation), a model-agnostic method that hedges across adaptation scales: it wraps any non-anticipating base-predictor pool with exponentially weighted least-squares (EWLS) adaptation experts at multiple forgetting factors, and aggregates raw and EWLS-adapted forecasts with MLpol, a parameter-free online aggregation rule. Under boundedness conditions, we establish deterministic oracle inequalities showing that it competes with both the best raw predictor and the best bounded, time-varying affine combinations of the base predictions, up to a path-length-dependent tracking cost and a sublinear aggregation overhead. We evaluate MELO on French national electricity-load forecasting through the COVID-19 lockdown using no regime indicators, lockdown dates, or policy covariates. MELO reduces overall RMSE by 34.7\% relative to base-only MLpol and achieves lower overall RMSE than a TabICL reference supplied with an external COVID policy-response covariate. Moreover, MELO requires only lightweight per-step recursive updates without model retraining.

</details>


### [176] [Sequential Design of Genetic Circuits Under Uncertainty With Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06552)
*Michal Kobiela,Diego A. Oyarzún,Michael U. Gutmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个序列化框架，利用强化学习策略和预训练的平摊方法，在无需显式参数推断的情况下，同时优化基因电路以应对分子随机性和跨实验室条件变化，并在异源基因表达和repressilator电路模型上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物系统设计受到分子反应内在随机性和实验条件跨实验室变异这两种不确定性的制约，现有贝叶斯方法虽能处理不确定性但需要昂贵的推断和优化步骤，导致实验周期延迟。

Method: 采用基于微分方程或马尔可夫跳跃过程的模拟器模型，结合强化学习策略方法，在跨可能不确定参数的分布上进行预训练（平摊方法），实现无需显式参数推断的即时观察自适应。

Result: 在异源基因表达和repressilator电路模型上展示了该框架能高效处理分子噪声和跨实验室变异性。

Conclusion: 所提出的平摊强化学习框架能够绕过传统贝叶斯方法中的计算瓶颈，实现快速、自适应的基因电路优化，为处理生物系统中的双重不确定性提供了高效方案。

Abstract: The design of biological systems is hindered by uncertainty arising from both intrinsic stochasticity of biomolecular reactions and variability across laboratory or experimental conditions. In this work, we present a sequential framework to optimize genetic circuits under both forms of uncertainty. By employing simulator models based on differential equations or Markov jump processes alongside a reinforcement learning (RL) policy-based approach, our method suggests experiments that adapt to unknown laboratory conditions while accounting for inherent stochasticity. While previous Bayesian methods address uncertainty through iterative experiment-inference-optimization cycles, they typically require computationally expensive inference and optimization steps after each experimental round, leading to delays. To overcome this bottleneck, we propose an amortized approach trained up-front across a distribution of possible uncertain parameters. This strategy sidesteps the need for explicit parameter inference during the design cycle, enabling immediate, observation-based adaptation. We demonstrate our framework on models for heterologous gene expression and a repressilator circuit, showing that it efficiently handles both molecular noise and cross-laboratory variability.

</details>


### [177] [Diverse Sampling in Diffusion Models with Marginal Preserving Particle Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06553)
*Gal Vinograd,Idan Achituve,Ethan Fetaya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EDDY是一种无需额外训练的引导机制，通过散度自由动力学（divergence-free dynamics）在保持边缘分布不变的前提下增加扩散模型和流匹配模型生成样本的多样性，并在合成分布和文本到图像生成任务中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型和流匹配模型在生成过程中容易产生模式坍塌或多样性不足的问题，现有方法往往需要在多样性与保真度之间权衡，且通常需要额外训练。作者希望在不损害边缘分布（即不牺牲生成质量）的条件下提升样本多样性。

Method: 利用Fokker-Planck方程的对称性，通过引入无散度（divergence-free）的漂移扰动来改变粒子轨迹，同时保证边缘分布不变。具体通过基于核的反对称成对矩阵场构造排斥方向，构建散度自由动力学，并针对文本到图像生成等计算密集型场景提出实用近似方案。

Result: 在合成分布和文本到图像生成实验中，EDDY相比常见基线方法，在保持强分布保真度的同时显著提升了生成样本的多样性。

Conclusion: EDDY提供了一种无需额外训练、理论保证的多样性提升方案，有效缓解了生成模型中的多样性-质量权衡问题。

Abstract: We present EDDY (Exact-marginal Diversification via Divergence-free dYnamics), a guidance mechanism for diffusion and flow matching models that promotes diversity among samples generated while maintaining quality. EDDY exploits symmetries of the Fokker-Planck equation, using drift perturbations that change particle trajectories while preserving the evolving marginal distribution. We instantiate this principle through kernel-based anti-symmetric pairwise matrix fields, constructed from the repulsive directions. The resulting divergence-free dynamics promote diversity at the joint particle level while preserving each particle's marginal distribution without any additional training. As computing the guidance can be computationally expensive in cases such as text-to-image generation with perceptual embeddings, we propose practical approximations as an effective and efficient solution. Experiments on synthetic distributions and text-to-image generation show that EDDY improves diversity while maintaining strong distributional fidelity compared to common baselines.

</details>


### [178] [Optimal Counterfactual Search in Tree Ensembles: A Study Across Modeling and Solution Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06561)
*Awa Khouna,Youssouf Emine,Julien Ferry,Thibaut Vidal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究树集成模型下满足合理性和可行动性约束的最优反事实解释计算问题。提出了约束规划（CP）方法CPCF，将数值特征编码为由分裂阈值诱导的区间域，离散特征保留原生有限域表示，形成紧凑的有限域公式。与MaxSAT（扩展至软投票集成）和MILP方法在10个数据集和3种树集成类型上进行比较，结果表明CP整体最优，MaxSAT擅长硬投票集成，MILP在中等分裂层数的摊销推理场景中具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实解释的信任依赖于其推荐变更是否真正最小化：次优解释可能大幅超出实际所需变更，且启发式错误对个体的影响不均，导致部分用户获得相关补救而其他用户承受不必要的高成本建议。因此需要计算满足合理性和可行动性约束的最优反事实解释。

Method: 提出CPCF方法，基于约束规划（CP）将数值特征编码为分裂阈值诱导的区间域、离散特征保留有限域表示，形成紧凑公式以支持多种距离目标。同时将原用于硬投票随机森林的MaxSAT扩展至软投票集成，并与现有最优MILP方法进行全面比较。

Result: 在10个数据集和3种树集成类型上的实验表明：CP取得最佳整体性能；MaxSAT在处理硬投票集成时表现优异；MILP在中等分裂层数的摊销推理设置中保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 不同数学规划范式各有适用场景：CP通用性最强，MaxSAT擅长硬投票集成，MILP适合中等复杂度的摊销推理。CPCF为树集成模型提供了高效的最优反事实解释求解方案。

Abstract: Trust in counterfactual explanations depends critically on whether their recommended changes are truly minimal: suboptimal explanations may vastly overshoot the actual changes needed to alter a decision, and heuristic errors can affect individuals unevenly, giving some users relevant recourse while assigning others unnecessarily costly recommendations. Consequently, we study the problem of computing optimal counterfactual explanations for tree ensembles under plausibility and actionability constraints. This is a combinatorial problem: for a fixed model, counterfactual search boils down to selecting consistent branching decisions and threshold-defined regions under a distance objective. We exploit this structure through CPCF, a constraint programming (CP) formulation in which numerical features are encoded as interval domains induced by split thresholds, while discrete features retain native finite-domain representations. This yields a compact finite-domain formulation that supports multiple distance objectives without continuous split-boundary search. We then place CPCF in a broader comparison across mathematical programming paradigms: we extend a maximum Boolean satisfiability (MaxSAT) formulation, originally designed for hard-voting random forests, to soft-voting ensembles, and compare against the current state-of-the-art mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) optimal approach. Across ten datasets and three types of tree ensembles, we analyze scalability, anytime performance, and sensitivity to distance metrics. We observe that CP achieves the best overall performance. More importantly, our results identify regimes in which the specific strengths of each paradigm make it best suited: CP is most versatile overall, MaxSAT handles hard-voting ensembles particularly well, and MILP remains competitive in amortized inference settings with a moderate number of split levels.

</details>


### [179] [Feature Dimensionality Outweighs Model Complexity in Breast Cancer Subtype Classification Using TCGA-BRCA Gene Expression Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06562)
*Meena Al Hasani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用TCGA-BRCA基因表达数据，评估了模型复杂度和特征选择对乳腺癌亚型分类的影响。结果表明逻辑回归在亚型级别表现最稳定均衡，随机森林在少数类亚型上表现不佳，SVM对特征维度敏感，强调了模型简单性、评估指标和特征选择在高维生物分类任务中的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基因表达数据具有高维度和样本量有限的特点，给机器学习模型带来挑战。准确分类乳腺癌亚型对于诊断和治疗选择至关重要，因此需要评估不同模型和特征选择策略在此类数据上的表现。

Method: 使用TCGA-BRCA基因表达数据，训练逻辑回归、随机森林和支持向量机（SVM）三种模型，采用不同数量的高变异基因（50到20,518个）进行特征选择，使用分层5折交叉验证，以准确率和宏F1分数评估性能。

Result: 所有模型均取得高准确率，但宏F1分析揭示了亚型级别性能的显著差异。逻辑回归表现最稳定均衡，包括对稀有类别的检测有所改善；随机森林虽整体准确率高，但在少数类亚型上表现不佳；SVM对特征维度敏感。

Conclusion: 在高维生物分类任务中，模型简单性、适当的评估指标（如宏F1）和特征选择至关重要，逻辑回归在此类场景下具有优势。

Abstract: Accurate classification of breast cancer subtypes from gene expression data is critical for diagnosis and treatment selection. However, such datasets are characterized by high dimensionality and limited sample size, posing challenges for machine learning models.
  In this study, we evaluate the impact of model complexity and feature selection on subtype classification performance using TCGA-BRCA gene expression data. Logistic regression, random forest, and support vector machine (SVM) models were trained using varying numbers of highly variable genes (50 to 20,518). Performance was evaluated using stratified 5-fold cross-validation and assessed with accuracy and macro F1 score. While all models achieved high accuracy, macro F1 analysis revealed substantial differences in subtype-level performance. Logistic regression demonstrated the most stable and balanced performance across subtypes, including improved detection of rare classes. Random forest underperformed on minority subtypes despite strong overall accuracy, while SVM showed sensitivity to feature dimensionality. These findings highlight the importance of model simplicity, evaluation metrics, and feature selection in high-dimensional biological classification tasks.

</details>


### [180] [SNAPO: Smooth Neural Adjoint Policy Optimization for Optimal Control via Differentiable Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06570)
*Dmitri Goloubentsev,Natalija Karpichina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SNAPO框架，将神经策略嵌入可微模拟器，通过平滑近似处理硬约束，利用伴随方法在一次反向传播中同时完成策略训练和所有输入/参数的精确梯度计算，在天然气储存、养老金管理和制药制造三个领域验证了高效性和灵敏度计算优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态规划在处理小规模问题时表现精确但状态维度增大时呈指数级扩展，而黑盒强化学习虽能处理高维状态但训练缓慢且无法产生灵敏度信息，亟需一种既能高效训练又能提供完整灵敏度的方法。

Method: 将神经策略嵌入已知且可微的模拟器中，用平滑近似替代硬约束，通过一次伴随反向传播计算目标函数关于所有策略参数和所有输入的精确梯度。

Result: 天然气存储领域在不到一分钟内完成训练，可无额外成本获取365条远期曲线灵敏度；养老金管理领域灵敏度计算速度提升6.5-200倍；制药制造领域通过5次伴随传播（74.5毫秒）获得20个ICH Q8监管灵敏度。

Conclusion: SNAPO框架在一次反向传播中同时完成策略训练和所有灵敏度计算，成本仅相当于一次反向传播，无论计算多少灵敏度，在多个实际领域展现出高效性和实用性。

Abstract: Many real-world problems require sequential decisions under uncertainty: when to inject or withdraw gas from storage, how to rebalance a pension portfolio each month, what temperature profile to run through a pharmaceutical reactor chain. Dynamic programming solves small instances exactly but scales exponentially in state dimensions. Black-box reinforcement learning handles high-dimensional states but trains slowly and produces no sensitivities. We introduce SNAPO (Smooth Neural Adjoint Policy Optimization), a framework that embeds a neural policy inside a known, differentiable simulator, replaces hard constraints with smooth approximations, and computes exact gradients of the objective with respect to all policy parameters and all inputs in a single adjoint pass. We demonstrate SNAPO on three domains: natural gas storage (training in under a minute, 365 forward curve sensitivities at no additional cost per sensitivity), pension fund asset-liability management (6.5x-200x sensitivity speedup over bump-and-revalue, scaling with the number of risk factors), and pharmaceutical manufacturing (cross-unit sensitivities through a 4-unit process chain, with 20 ICH Q8 regulatory sensitivities from 5 adjoint passes in 74.5 milliseconds). All sensitivities are produced by the same backward pass that trains the policy, at a cost proportional to one reverse pass regardless of how many sensitivities are computed.

</details>


### [181] [Directional Consistency as a Complementary Optimization Signal: The GONO Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06575)
*Victor Daniel Gera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现深度优化中方向对齐与损失收敛可解耦，并提出GONO优化器，通过基于梯度方向一致性自适应调整动量系数，实现了理论收敛保证与实验竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化器（Adam、SGD、RMSprop）缺乏利用梯度方向时序一致性的显式机制，仅依赖基于幅度的信号，无法区分平台、鞍点与真正收敛，导致方向对齐与损失收敛可能解耦。

Method: 提出GONO优化器，根据连续梯度余弦相似度cc_t自适应调整Adam的动量系数β1：方向一致时放大动量，方向震荡时抑制动量。理论上证明其匹配Adam的O(1/√T)收敛速率，并在无信息信号时退化为Adam。

Result: cc_t在震荡检测上F1=1.00（梯度范数仅0.45）；GONO在MNIST（98.15%）、CIFAR-10（43.14%）和ResNet-18（75.44%）上与AdamW保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 方向对齐是一个有理论依据且实际可用的优化信号，GONO验证了其有效性。

Abstract: We identify and formalize an underexplored phenomenon in deep learning optimization: directional alignment and loss convergence can be decoupled. An optimizer can exhibit near-perfect directional consistency (cc_t -> 1, measured via consecutive gradient cosine similarity) while the loss remains high or decreases slowly. This observation reveals that existing optimizers such as Adam, SGD, and RMSprop lack explicit mechanisms to exploit temporal consistency in gradient directions, relying instead on magnitude-based signals that fail to distinguish plateaus, saddle points, and genuine convergence. Motivated by this, we introduce GONO (Gradient-Oriented Norm-Adaptive Optimizer), which adapts Adam's momentum coefficient beta_1 based on cc_t: amplifying momentum under directional consistency and suppressing it during oscillation. We prove GONO matches Adam's O(1/sqrt(T)) convergence rate and reduces exactly to Adam when the signal is uninformative. Empirically, cc_t achieves oscillation detection with F1=1.00 (vs. 0.45 for gradient norm), and GONO remains competitive with AdamW on MNIST (98.15%), CIFAR-10 (43.14%), and ResNet-18 (75.44%), establishing directional alignment as a theoretically grounded, practically actionable optimization signal. Code: https://github.com/victordaniel/gono-optimizer

</details>


### [182] [On the Safety of Graph Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06576)
*Xiaoguang Guo,Zehong Wang,Ziming Li,Shawn Spitzel,Soonwoo Kwon,Tianyi Ma,Yanfang Ye,Chuxu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GRL-Safety基准，系统评估图表示学习方法在五类安全性维度（鲁棒性、OOD泛化、类别不平衡、公平性、可解释性）上的表现，涵盖12种代表性方法在25个数据集上的评估，发现安全性行为由表示设计与压力因素的交互决定，基础模型时代的方法呈现轴特异性优势而非全面领先，并识别出当前方法仍存在能力缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GRL评估主要关注干净迁移、适应性和任务覆盖，忽略了部署压力（如图信号干扰、上下文变化、标签支持受限、结构组偏移、预测证据不一致）对方法可靠性的影响，缺乏多维度系统性的安全性评估基准。
