Method: 构建GRL-Safety多轴安全性评估基准，在标准化评估条件下对12种代表性方法（涵盖拓扑嵌入、监督GNN、自监督图模型、图基础模型）在25个图数据集上进行评估，覆盖5个安全轴（损坏鲁棒性、OOD泛化、类别不平衡、公平性、可解释性），按轴和子条件分别报告，而非单一聚合分数。

Result: 获得三个跨轴洞察：1）安全性行为由表示设计与压力因素的交互决定，而非方法家族；2）基础模型时代的方法呈现轴特异性优势而非全面安全主导；3）多个部署场景即使对最佳方法仍困难，存在能力缺口。

Conclusion: GRL方法在部署可靠性上存在显著差异，需要超越模型选择层面的新鲁棒性、适应性或训练目标来弥补能力缺口。

Abstract: Graph representation learning (GRL) has evolved from topology-only graph embeddings to task-specific supervised GNNs, and more recently to reusable representations and graph foundation models (GFMs). However, existing evaluations mainly measure clean transfer, adaptation, and task coverage. It remains unclear whether GRL methods stay reliable when deployment stresses affect graph signals, graph contexts, label support, structural groups, or predictive evidence. We introduce GRL-Safety, a multi-axis safety evaluation benchmark for GRL. GRL-Safety evaluates twelve representative methods, spanning topology-only embedding methods, supervised GNNs, self-supervised graph models, and GFMs, on twenty-five graph datasets under standardized evaluation conditions while preserving method-native adaptation. The evaluation covers five safety axes: corruption robustness, OOD generalization, class imbalance, fairness, and interpretation, with per-axis and sub-condition reporting rather than a single aggregate score. Our analysis yields three cross-axis insights that can inspire future research. First, safety behavior is shaped by the interaction between representation design and the stressed graph factor, rather than by method family alone. Second, foundation-era methods show axis-specific strengths rather than broad safety dominance. Third, several deployment regimes remain difficult even for the best evaluated method, revealing capability gaps that require new robustness, adaptation, or training objectives beyond model selection. The benchmark, evaluation protocols, and code are available at: https://github.com/GXG-CS/GRL-Safety.

</details>


### [183] [PairAlign: A Framework for Sequence Tokenization via Self-Alignment with Applications to Audio Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06582)
*Adhiraj Banerjee,Vipul Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PairAlign是一个通过序列级自对齐实现紧凑音频标记化的框架，将标记化视为条件序列生成，通过学习标记身份、顺序、长度和EOS位置，在两个内容保持的视角间进行交叉预测，从而在保持编辑距离的同时减少存档标记数量55%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频标记化方法依赖量化或码本重建，局部分配标记，导致序列一致性、紧凑性、长度控制、终止符和编辑相似度等全局性质很少被直接优化。

Method: PairAlign将标记化视为条件序列生成：编码器将语音映射为连续条件，自回归解码器从BOS生成标记。在两个内容保持的视角间进行交叉预测训练，同时使用无关样本提供竞争序列，并引入EMA教师目标、交叉配对教师强制、前缀损坏、似然对比和长度控制等技术。

Result: 在3秒语音上学习到紧凑、非退化序列，词汇使用广泛且跨视角一致性强。在TIMIT检索中，保持编辑距离搜索的同时减少存档标记数55%。连续扫描探针显示局部重叠低于密集几何标记器，但具有更强的长度控制和100ms偏移下的有界编辑轨迹。

Conclusion: PairAlign是一个序列符号预测学习器，类似于JEPA风格的目标，它从一个视角预测另一个视角的抽象目标，但输出的是可变长度的符号序列而非连续潜变量。

Abstract: Many operations on sensory data -- comparison, memory, retrieval, and reasoning -- are naturally expressed over discrete symbolic structures. In language this interface is given by tokens; in audio, it must be learned. Existing audio tokenizers rely on quantization, clustering, or codec reconstruction, assigning tokens locally, so sequence consistency, compactness, length control, termination, and edit similarity are rarely optimized directly.
  We introduce PairAlign, a framework for compact audio tokenization through sequence-level self-alignment. PairAlign treats tokenization as conditional sequence generation: an encoder maps speech to a continuous condition, and an autoregressive decoder generates tokens from BOS, learning token identity, order, length, and EOS placement. Given two content-preserving views, each view's sequence is trained to be likely under the other's representation, while unrelated examples provide competing sequences. This gives a scalable surrogate for edit-distance preservation while discouraging many-to-one collapse.
  PairAlign starts from VQ-style tokenization and refines it with EMA-teacher targets, cross-paired teacher forcing, prefix corruption, likelihood contrast, and length control.
  On 3-second speech, PairAlign learns compact, non-degenerate sequences with broad vocabulary usage and strong cross-view consistency. On TIMIT retrieval, it preserves edit-distance search while reducing archive token count by 55%. A continuous-sweep probe shows lower local overlap than a dense geometric tokenizer, but stronger length control and bounded edit trajectories under 100 ms shifts. PairAlign is a sequence-symbolic predictive learner: like JEPA-style objectives, it predicts an abstract target from another view as a learned variable-length symbolic sequence, not a continuous latent.

</details>


### [184] [Distributionally-Robust Learning to Optimize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06585)
*Vinit Ranjan,Jisun Park,Bartolomeo Stellato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分布鲁棒的学习方法，用于优化凸优化中一阶算法的超参数。通过最小化Wasserstein分布鲁棒版本的性能估计问题（PEP），在算法参数（如步长）上学习，统一了经典的学习优化（L2O）和最坏情况最优算法设计（PEP）。通过随机梯度下降求解，在无约束二次最小化、LASSO和线性规划基准上，学习到的算法在可认证鲁棒性下取得了强泛化性能，优于最坏情况最优和原始L2O基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统学习优化（L2O）在训练实例上表现良好但缺乏泛化保证，而最坏情况最优设计（PEP）过于保守。本文旨在弥合两者差距，同时获得好的平均性能和鲁棒性保证。

Method: 将PEP问题扩展为Wasserstein分布鲁棒版本，通过调整鲁棒半径（ρ）在L2O和PEP之间插值；使用SGD求解该双层优化问题，内层为半定规划（SDP），通过隐式微分端到端训练。

Result: 在无约束二次最小化、LASSO和线性规划三个基准上，学习到的算法在鲁棒性保障下取得强泛化性能，优于最坏情况最优算法和原始L2O基线。

Conclusion: 证明了学习算法的真实风险以高概率被控制在样本内L2O最优值加上随样本量缩小的松弛项内，且不差于最坏情况PEP界，实现了平均性能与鲁棒保证的权衡。

Abstract: We propose a distributionally robust approach to learning hyperparameters for first-order methods in convex optimization. Given a dataset of problem instances, we minimize a Wasserstein distributionally robust version of the performance estimation problem (PEP) over algorithm parameters such as step sizes. Our framework unifies two extremes: as the robustness radius vanishes, we recover classical learning to optimize (L2O); as it grows, we recover worst-case optimal algorithm design via PEP. We solve the resulting problem with stochastic gradient descent, differentiating through the solution of an inner semidefinite program at each step. We prove high-probability bounds showing that the true risk of the learned algorithm is at most the in-sample L2O optimum plus a slack that shrinks with the sample size, and is no worse than the worst-case PEP bound. On unconstrained quadratic minimization, LASSO, and linear programming benchmarks, our learned algorithms achieve strong out-of-sample performance with certifiable robustness, outperforming both worst-case optimal and vanilla L2O baselines.

</details>


### [185] [Towards Metric-Faithful Neural Graph Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06588)
*Jyotirmaya Shivottam,Subhankar Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个连接编码器几何结构与神经图编辑距离（GED）估计质量的理论框架，证明了双Lipschitz编码器能改进GED预测和排序性能，并通过FSW-GNN作为即插即用模块验证了该观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管神经图匹配架构在GED估计方面取得了显著进展，但编码器几何结构在其中的作用仍不清楚。本文旨在建立理论框架理解编码器几何如何影响GED估计质量。

Method: 建立理论框架连接编码器几何与两类神经GED估计器（图相似度预测器和基于对齐的方法）的质量；提出双Lipschitz编码器（FSW-GNN）作为即插即用替换模块；在多个基线模型和数据集上进行实验验证。

Result: 几何感知的变体显著改进了GED预测和排序指标；消融实验和迁移实验支持这些改进来源于更好的表示几何结构。

Conclusion: 编码器几何结构可作为神经图匹配的有用设计原则。

Abstract: Graph Edit Distance (GED) is a fundamental, albeit NP-hard, metric for structural graph similarity. Recent neural graph matching architectures approximate GED by first encoding graphs with a Graph Neural Network (GNN) and then applying either a graph-level regression head or a matching-based alignment module. Despite substantial architectural progress, the role of encoder geometry in neural GED estimation remains poorly understood. In this paper, we develop a theoretical framework that connects encoder geometry to GED estimation quality for two broad classes of neural GED estimators: graph similarity predictors and alignment-based methods. On fixed graph collections, where the doubly-stochastic metric $d_{\mathrm{DS}}$ is comparable to GED, we show that graph-level bi-Lipschitz encoders yield controlled GED surrogates and improved ranking stability; for matching-based estimators, node-level bi-Lipschitz geometry propagates to encoder-induced alignment costs and the resulting optimized alignment objective. We instantiate this perspective using FSW-GNN, a bi-Lipschitz WL-equivalent encoder, as a drop-in replacement in representative neural GED architectures. Across representative baselines and benchmark datasets, the resulting geometry-aware variants significantly improve GED prediction and ranking metrics. A faithfulness case study of untrained encoders, together with ablations and transfer experiments, supports the view that these gains arise from improved representation geometry, positioning encoder geometry as a useful design principle for neural graph matching.

</details>


### [186] [Weight-Decay Turns Transformer Loss Landscapes Villani: Functional-Analytic Foundations for Optimization and Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06599)
*Abhijit Das,Sayantan Dutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次从泛函分析角度严格证明了带L2正则化的Transformer损失函数满足Villani coercive能量函数条件，推导了对数Sobolev和Poincaré常数，并通过GPT-Neo-125M实验验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权重衰减（L2正则化）在大语言模型中广泛使用，但其对Transformer损失landscape的理论作用机制尚未被严格刻画。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 1) 证明正则化损失函数满足Villani coercive能量函数的四个条件：无限可微、至少二次增长、Gaussian可积尾部、微分增长条件；2) 推导显式的log-Sobolev和Poincaré常数，建立正则化强度λ与模型维度d的联系；3) 提出Villani诊断量Ψ_s并通过Hutchinson trace探针高效估计；4) 在GPT-Neo-125M上实验验证。

Result: 理论方面：给出显式常数C_LS ≤ λ^{-1} + d/λ²，连接正则化强度与收敛保证；实验方面：在GPT-Neo-125M（超1亿参数）上验证了二次增长、Hessian谱膨胀和指数收敛行为与理论一致。

Conclusion: 权重衰减不仅经验上提升泛化，而且为深度学习中的快速Langevin混合和曲率感知优化建立了必要的数学条件。

Abstract: Weight decay is widely used as a regularizer in large language models, yet its precise role in shaping Transformer loss landscapes remains theoretically underexplored. This paper provides the first rigorous functional-analytic characterization of the standard Transformer objective--cross-entropy loss with $L^2$ regularization--by proving it satisfies Villani's criteria for coercive energy functions. Specifically, we show that the regularized loss $\mathcal{F}$ is infinitely differentiable, grows at least quadratically, has Gaussian-integrable tails, and satisfies the differential growth condition $-Δ\mathcal{F} + \tfrac{1}{s}\|\nabla\mathcal{F}\|^{2} \to \infty$ as $\|θ\| \to \infty$ for all $s>0$. From this structure, we derive explicit log-Sobolev and Poincaré constants $C_{\mathrm{LS}} \leq λ^{-1} + d/λ^{2}$, linking the regularization strength $λ$ and model dimension $d$ to finite-time convergence guarantees for noisy stochastic gradient descent and PAC-Bayesian generalization bounds that tighten with increasing $λ$. To validate our theory, we introduce a scalable Villani diagnostic $Ψ_s(θ) = -Δ\mathcal{F} + s^{-1}\|\nabla \mathcal{F}\|^2$ and estimate it efficiently using Hutchinson trace probes in models with over 100M parameters. Experiments on GPT-Neo-125M across Penn Treebank and WikiText-103 confirm the predicted quadratic growth of $Ψ_s$, spectral inflation of the Hessian, and exponential convergence behavior consistent with our log-Sobolev analysis. These results demonstrate that weight decay not only improves generalization empirically but also establishes the mathematical conditions required for fast Langevin mixing and theoretically grounded curvature-aware optimization in deep learning.

</details>


### [187] [How Many Iterations to Jailbreak? Dynamic Budget Allocation for Multi-Turn LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06605)
*Shai Feldman,Yaniv Romano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DAPRO，首个理论上有效的动态预算分配框架，用于在多轮LLM交互中为事件发生时间提供置信下界，理论上满足预算约束并提供无分布假设的有限样本覆盖保证，实验证明其相比静态基线更接近名义覆盖水平且方差更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多轮对话场景中评估LLM性能计算成本高昂，关键事件（如越狱攻击或任务完成）通常需要多次交互后才出现，且事件可能是稀疏的。现有符合生存分析框架采用静态预算分配，在多轮设置中效率低下。

Method: 提出DAPRO（Dynamic Allocation via PRojected Optimization）动态预算分配框架，用于对多轮LLM交互中的事件发生时间进行边界估计。理论上证明了其满足预算约束并提供无分布假设、有限样本的覆盖保证，不需要先前方法要求的删失与事件时间之间的条件独立性假设。关键理论贡献是提出了一种新的覆盖界，其规模与平均删失权重的平方根成正比，而非最坏情况权重。

Result: 在Llama 3.1和Qwen 2.5等LLM上，涵盖智能体任务成功、对抗性越狱、有毒内容生成和RAG幻觉等多种场景的实验表明，DAPRO在满足预算约束的同时，相比静态基线始终能够更接近名义覆盖水平，且方差更低。

Conclusion: DAPRO是一种有效的动态预算分配方法，可用于在有限计算资源下获得无偏、低方差的人群级评估指标估计（如越狱率），在理论上优于现有静态方法。

Abstract: Evaluating and predicting the performance of large language models (LLMs) in multi-turn conversational settings is critical yet computationally expensive; key events -- e.g., jailbreaks or successful task completion by an agent -- often emerge only after repeated interactions. These events might be rare, and under any feasible computational budget, remain unobserved.
  Recent conformal survival frameworks construct reliable lower predictive bounds (LPBs) on the number of iterations to trigger the event of interest, but rely on static budget allocation that is inefficient in multi-turn setups. To address this, we introduce \emph{Dynamic Allocation via PRojected Optimization} (DAPRO), the first theoretically valid dynamic budget allocation framework for bounding the time-to-event in multi-turn LLM interactions.
  We prove that DAPRO satisfies the budget constraint and provides distribution-free, finite-sample coverage guarantees without requiring the conditional independence between censoring and event times assumed by prior conformal survival approaches.
  A key theoretical contribution is a novel coverage bound that scales with the square root of the mean censoring weight rather than the worst-case weight, yielding provably tighter guarantees than prior work. Furthermore, DAPRO can be employed to obtain unbiased, low-variance estimates of population-level evaluation metrics, such as the jailbreak rate, under limited computing resources.
  Comprehensive experiments across agentic task success, adversarial jailbreaks, toxic content generation, and RAG hallucinations using LLMs such as Llama 3.1 and Qwen 2.5 demonstrate that DAPRO consistently achieves coverage closer to the nominal level with lower variance than static baselines, while satisfying the budget constraint.

</details>


### [188] [Transformers Efficiently Perform In-Context Logistic Regression via Normalized Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06609)
*Chenyang Zhang,Yuan Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了softmax注意力机制下的Transformer如何在线性分类数据上进行上下文学习，构建了能执行上下文逻辑回归的多层Transformer，其中每层精确执行一步归一化梯度下降，并通过循环应用单层训练模型实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Transformer如何通过注意力机制隐式执行算法以实现强大的上下文学习能力，特别是softmax注意力在线性分类任务中的工作机制。

Method: (1) 构造一类多层Transformer，每层执行一步归一化梯度下降以完成上下文逻辑回归；(2) 通过单层自注意力层的一步梯度下降训练获得该构造；(3) 循环应用训练好的单层得到循环模型。

Result: 提供了自注意力层训练收敛性的理论保证，以及循环模型在分布外泛化方面的理论保证。

Conclusion: 验证了softmax Transformer能够有效作为上下文学习器，推进了对上下文学习机制的理论理解。

Abstract: Transformers have demonstrated remarkable in-context learning (ICL) capabilities. The strong ICL performance of transformers is commonly believed to arise from their ability to implicitly execute certain algorithms on the context, thereby enhancing prediction and generation. In this work, we investigate how transformers with softmax attention perform in-context learning on linear classification data. We first construct a class of multi-layer transformers that can perform in-context logistic regression, with each layer exactly performing one step of normalized gradient descent on an in-context loss. Then, we show that our constructed transformer can be obtained through (i) training a single self-attention layer supervised by one-step gradient descent, and (ii) recurrently applying the trained layer to obtain a looped model. Training convergence guarantees of the self-attention layer and out-of-distribution generalization guarantees of the looped model are provided. Our results advance the theoretical understanding of ICL mechanism by showcasing how softmax transformers can effectively act as in-context learners.

</details>


### [189] [SoftSAE: Dynamic Top-K Selection for Adaptive Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06610)
*Jakub Stępień,Marcin Mazur,Jacek Tabor,Przemysław Spurek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SoftSAE提出了一种动态Top-K选择机制的稀疏自编码器，能够根据输入复杂度自适应调整激活特征数量，解决了固定稀疏度（如TopK SAE）无法适应数据局部内在维度变化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAE（如TopK SAE）使用固定的稀疏度K，但真实数据的局部内在维度在不同样本间变化显著：简单输入只需少量特征，复杂输入需要更多特征。固定K会导致简单样本引入噪声或复杂样本丢失重要结构。

Method: 提出SoftSAE，采用可微分的Soft Top-K算子，学习与输入相关的动态稀疏度k，使模型能根据每个输入的复杂度自适应调整激活特征数量。

Result: 实验结果表明SoftSAE不仅能发现有意义的特征，还能为每个概念选择正确的特征数量。

Conclusion: SoftSAE通过动态稀疏度机制使表示更好地匹配数据结构，解释长度能反映输入的信息量，是对固定稀疏度SAE的有效改进。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have become an important tool in mechanistic interpretability, helping to analyze internal representations in both Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision Transformers (ViTs). By decomposing polysemantic activations into sparse sets of monosemantic features, SAEs aim to translate neural network computations into human-understandable concepts. However, common architectures such as TopK SAEs rely on a fixed sparsity level. They enforce the same number of active features (K) across all inputs, ignoring the varying complexity of real-world data. Natural data often lies on manifolds with varying local intrinsic dimensionality, meaning the number of relevant factors can change significantly across samples. This suggests that a fixed sparsity level is not optimal. Simple inputs may require only a few features, while more complex ones need more expressive representations. Using a constant K can therefore introduce noise in simple cases or miss important structure in more complex ones. To address this issue, we propose SoftSAE, a sparse autoencoder with a Dynamic Top-K selection mechanism. Our method uses a differentiable Soft Top-K operator to learn an input-dependent sparsity level k. This allows the model to adjust the number of active features based on the complexity of each input. As a result, the representation better matches the structure of the data, and the explanation length reflects the amount of information in the input. Experimental results confirm that SoftSAE not only finds meaningful features, but also selects the right number of features for each concept. The source code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SoftSAE-8F71/.

</details>


### [190] [The Structural Origin of Attention Sink: Variance Discrepancy, Super Neurons, and Dimension Disparity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06611)
*Siquan Li,Kaiqi Jiang,Jiacheng Sun,Tianyang Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM中注意力汇聚现象的机制根源：自注意力中的值聚合过程导致系统性的方差差异，而FFN层中超神经元的激活会显著放大这一差异，从而迫使模型形成注意力汇聚作为结构锚点。通过两种干预实验验证了这一因果链，并提出head-wise RMSNorm架构改进来稳定预训练过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管注意力汇聚现象在LLM中普遍存在（初始token不成比例地垄断注意力分数），但其结构起源仍不明确，本文旨在提供其机制层面的解释。

Method: 1. 从理论上追溯注意力汇聚的根源到自注意力中的值聚合过程导致的方差差异；2. 证明FFN层中超神经元的激活会放大这种差异，具体通过通道稀疏的下投影引发首token表示的维度差异；3. 通过两种受控干预实验验证因果链：注意力掩码修改和特定token表示方差放大；4. 提出head-wise RMSNorm架构改进。

Result: 两种干预实验均能在任意位置复制注意力汇聚现象，验证了所提出的因果链；head-wise RMSNorm能在预训练中稳定值聚合输出，加速收敛。

Conclusion: 本文为注意力汇聚现象提供了完整的机制解释，并基于该理解提出了可控的架构改进方案，证明了恢复位置间统计奇偶性对加速预训练收敛的有效性。

Abstract: Despite the prevalence of the attention sink phenomenon in Large Language Models (LLMs), where initial tokens disproportionately monopolize attention scores, its structural origins remain elusive. This work provides a \textit{mechanistic explanation} for this phenomenon. First, we trace its root to the value aggregation process inherent in self-attention, which induces a systematic variance discrepancy. We further demonstrate that this discrepancy is drastically amplified by the activation of super neurons within Feed-Forward Network (FFN) layers. Specifically, the channel-sparse down-projections trigger a dimension disparity of the first-token representation, necessitating the formation of attention sinks as a structural anchor. Then, we validate this causal chain through two controlled interventions: (i) isolating the aggregation effect via attention mask modifications and (ii) amplifying the variance of targeted token representations. Both interventions can replicate attention sinks at arbitrary positions. Our mechanistic understanding offers a foundation for the systematic control of sink formation. Finally, as a proof of concept, we propose \textit{head-wise RMSNorm}, an architectural modification that stabilizes value aggregation outputs during pre-training. Our experiments demonstrate that restoring statistical parity across positions significantly accelerates convergence.

</details>


### [191] [When and Why SignSGD Outperforms SGD: A Theoretical Study Based on $\ell_1$-norm Lower Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06615)
*Hongyi Tao,Dingzhi Yu,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上证明了在ℓ₁范数平稳性、ℓ∞光滑性和可分离噪声模型下，SignSGD相比SGD能在稀疏噪声场景下将复杂度降低d倍（d为维度），并将该框架推广到矩阵域以分析Muon优化器，最后在124M参数GPT-2预训练中验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标准设置下SGD在ℓ₂范数平稳性上已达到极小极大最优，无法解释符号优化器为何在某些场景下优于SGD，缺乏对符号方法优势的理论理解。

Method: 引入ℓ₁范数平稳性、ℓ∞光滑性和可分离噪声模型来刻画符号更新的坐标级特性，推导SignSGD的匹配上下界；将该框架扩展到矩阵域分析Muon优化器；通过GPT-2预训练实验进行实证验证。

Result: 在稀疏噪声下SignSGD的复杂度上界比SGD下界低d倍；Muon优化器在矩阵域保持最优维度缩放；GPT-2实验验证了理论优势与收敛加速的一致性。

Conclusion: 符号优化器的理论优势需要特定的问题几何（ℓ₁/ℓ∞几何和可分离噪声）来体现，该框架为理解和设计符号方法提供了理论基础，且理论结果能预测实际训练行为。

Abstract: Sign-based optimization algorithms, such as SignSGD and Muon, have garnered significant attention for their remarkable performance in training large foundation models. Despite this empirical success, we still lack a theoretical understanding of when and why these sign-based methods outperform vanilla SGD. The core obstacle is that under standard smoothness and finite variance conditions, SGD is known to be minimax optimal for finding stationary points measured by $\ell_2$-norms, thereby fundamentally precluding any complexity gains for sign-based methods in standard settings. To overcome this barrier, we analyze sign-based optimizers leveraging $\ell_1$-norm stationarity, $\ell_\infty$-smoothness, and a separable noise model, which can better capture the coordinate-wise nature of signed updates. Under this distinct problem geometry, we derive matched upper and lower bounds for SignSGD and explicitly characterize the problem class in which SignSGD provably dominates SGD. Specifically, we compare the \emph{upper bound of SignSGD} with the \emph{lower bound of SGD}, illustrating that SignSGD effectively reduces the complexity by a factor of $d$ under \emph{sparse noise}, where $d$ is the problem dimension. Furthermore, we elevate this framework to the matrix domain, providing an equivalent optimal lower bound for the Muon optimizer, proving that extending the sign operator to matrices preserves this optimal scaling with dimensionality. Finally, we bridge our theoretical bounds to practice, demonstrating that the theoretical superiority of SignSGD accurately predicts its faster convergence during the pretraining of a 124M parameter GPT-2 model.

</details>


### [192] [Concept-Based Abductive and Contrastive Explanations for Behaviors of Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06640)
*Ronaldo Canizales,Divya Gopinath,Corina Păsăreanu,Ravi Mangal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出概念级溯因与对比解释方法，通过概念擦除技术建立因果联系，枚举最小概念集解释模型预测，支持单个样本和群体行为的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念解释方法缺乏因果联系或仅支持单一概念，而形式化溯因/对比解释仅处理低层特征（如像素），无法提供高层语义解释。本文旨在弥合这两类方法的鸿沟。

Method: 提出概念级溯因与对比解释框架，通过概念擦除过程建立因果关系，枚举所有最小概念集；通过聚合这些解释，支持单个图像和具有共同行为的图像集合的解释。

Result: 在多个模型、数据集和行为上验证了该方法在生成有用且用户友好的解释方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 概念级溯因与对比解释能够提供因果相关的最小高层概念集，既支持个体样本分析，也支持群体行为分析，是一种有效且可理解的解释方法。

Abstract: *Concept-based explanations* offer a promising approach for explaining the predictions of deep neural networks in terms of high-level, human-understandable concepts. However, existing methods either do not establish a causal connection between the concepts and model predictions or are limited in expressivity and only able to infer causal explanations involving single concepts. At the same time, the parallel line of work on *formal abductive and contrastive explanations* computes the minimal set of input features causally relevant for model outcomes but only considers low-level features such as pixels. Merging these two threads, in this work, we propose the notion of *concept-based abductive and contrastive explanations* that capture the minimal sets of high-level concepts causally relevant for model outcomes. We then present a family of algorithms that enumerate all minimal explanations while using *concept erasure* procedures to establish causal relationships. By appropriately aggregating such explanations, we are not only able to understand model predictions on individual images but also on collections of images where the model exhibits a user-specified, common *behavior*. We evaluate our approach on multiple models, datasets, and behaviors, and demonstrate its effectiveness in computing helpful, user-friendly explanations.

</details>


### [193] [Inductive Venn-Abers and related regressors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06646)
*Ivan Petej,Vladimir Vovk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将Venn-Abers预测器从二元分类和有界回归扩展到无界回归场景，通过引入共形预测元素实现。实验表明，基于Venn-Abers回归器导出的点回归器在更大训练集上能略微提升标准回归器的预测效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Venn-Abers预测器具有有效性保证等优良性质，但仅适用于二元分类及近期扩展的有界回归场景，无法处理实际中常见的无界回归问题，存在适用范围受限的局限性。

Method: 将Venn-Abers预测器推广到无界回归场景，通过引入共形预测(conformal prediction)元素来支持无界回归；在模拟和实证研究中评估所提出的Venn-Abers回归器导出的点回归器的预测效率。

Result: 仿真和实证研究表明，基于Venn-Abers回归器导出的点回归器在更大训练集上能够略微提升标准回归器的预测效率。

Conclusion: 成功将Venn-Abers预测器推广到无界回归场景，扩展了其适用范围；所提方法在较大训练集上可带来预测效率的改进。

Abstract: Venn-Abers predictors are probabilistic predictors that enjoy appealing properties of validity, but their major limitation is that they are applicable only to the case of binary classification, with a recent extension to bounded regression. We generalize them to the case of unbounded regression, which requires adding an element of conformal prediction. In our simulation and empirical studies we investigate the predictive efficiency of point regressors derived from Venn-Abers regressors and argue that they somewhat improve the predictive efficiency of standard regressors for larger training sets.

</details>


### [194] [When No Benchmark Exists: Validating Comparative LLM Safety Scoring Without Ground-Truth Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06652)
*Sushant Gautam,Finn Schwall,Annika Willoch Olstad,Fernando Vallecillos Ruiz,Birk Torpmann-Hagen,Sunniva Maria Stordal Bjørklund,Leon Moonen,Klas Pettersen,Michael A. Riegler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了基准测试缺失下的比较安全评分框架（benchmarkless comparative safety scoring），并设计了情景审计链（SimpleAudit）作为实证工具，通过在挪威语安全包上的验证，证明该方法能有效区分安全与去对齐模型，并应用于挪威公共部门采购案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在缺乏标注基准测试的情况下（如新语言、行业或监管领域），如何对候选语言模型进行可靠的安全比较，是实际部署中的关键问题。

Method: 形式化定义了基准测试缺失下的比较安全评分，提出工具性效度链（instrumental-validity chain），包含三个标准：对安全与去对齐控制对比的响应性、目标驱动方差主导性（超越审计员和评审者人为效应）、重跑稳定性。基于该链实现SimpleAudit工具，在挪威语安全包上验证，并使用方差分解（η²）和AUROC进行量化评估。

Result: 安全与去对齐目标分离的AUROC介于0.89-1.00；目标身份是主导方差成分（η²≈0.52）；严重度剖面的稳定性在10次重跑后达成。该方法同样适用于Petri工具。挪威公共采购案例表明，更安全的模型取决于情景类别和风险度量。

Conclusion: 情景审计的有效性依赖于情景包、评分标准、审计员、评审者、采样配置和重跑预算的完整报告，不应将分数简化为单一排名。比较安全评分必须在特定合同条件下才有意义。

Abstract: Many deployments must compare candidate language models for safety before a labeled benchmark exists for the relevant language, sector, or regulatory regime. We formalize this setting as benchmarkless comparative safety scoring and specify the contract under which a scenario-based audit can be interpreted as deployment evidence. Scores are valid only under a fixed scenario pack, rubric, auditor, judge, sampling configuration, and rerun budget. Because no labels are available, we replace ground-truth agreement with an instrumental-validity chain: responsiveness to a controlled safe-versus-abliterated contrast, dominance of target-driven variance over auditor and judge artifacts, and stability across reruns.
  We instantiate the chain in SimpleAudit, a local-first scoring instrument, and validate it on a Norwegian safety pack. Safe and abliterated targets separate with AUROC values between 0.89 and 1.00, target identity is the dominant variance component ($η^2 \approx 0.52$), and severity profiles stabilize by ten reruns. Applying the same chain to Petri shows that it admits both tools. The substantial differences arise upstream of the chain, in claim-contract enforcement and deployment fit. A Norwegian public-sector procurement case comparing Borealis and Gemma 3 demonstrates the resulting evidence in practice: the safer model depends on scenario category and risk measure. Consequently, scores, matched deltas, critical rates, uncertainty, and the auditor and judge used must be reported together rather than collapsed into a single ranking.

</details>


### [195] [Optimizer-Model Consistency: Full Finetuning with the Same Optimizer as Pretraining Forgets Less](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06654)
*Yuxing Liu,Jianyu Wang,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现预训练和全量微调使用相同优化器（即优化器-模型一致性）能实现更好的学习-遗忘权衡，效果优于其他优化器甚至LoRA。通过实验和理论分析，揭示了优化器对激活值的正则化效应导致损失景观不同，相同优化器产生的权重更新能更好地保留预训练知识。同时发现Muon优化器在推理任务微调中表现较差，因其倾向于死记硬背。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在大型语言模型的监督微调阶段，预训练与微调使用的优化器是否一致对模型性能（特别是遗忘-学习权衡）的影响，并解释这一现象背后的机制。

Method: 通过控制实验和理论分析，研究不同优化器（如AdamW、Muon）对模型激活值的正则化效应和损失景观的影响；对比全量微调与LoRA在不同优化器下的表现；设计合成语言建模实验分析Muon的归纳偏置。

Result: 1) 优化器-模型一致性（全量微调使用与预训练相同的优化器）在SFT中遗忘更少且新任务性能持平或更好；2) 优化器通过正则化效应塑造模型激活值的损失景观；3) Muon在微调推理任务时表现差，因其强死记硬背倾向不利于小数据量的模式习得。

Conclusion: 预训练与微调的优化器一致性对减少灾难性遗忘至关重要，优化器的选择不仅影响训练效率，还通过正则化效应影响模型的知识保留能力。Muon等优化器在特定场景下的局限性需要进一步关注。

Abstract: Optimizers play an important role in both pretraining and finetuning stages when training large language models (LLMs). In this paper, we present an observation that full finetuning with the same optimizer as in pretraining achieves a better learning-forgetting tradeoff, i.e., forgetting less while achieving the same or better performance on the new task, than other optimizers and, possibly surprisingly, LoRA, during the supervised finetuning (SFT) stage. We term this phenomenon optimizer-model consistency. To better understand it, through controlled experiments and theoretical analysis, we show that: 1) optimizers can shape the models by having regularization effects on the activations, leading to different landscapes around the pretrained checkpoints; 2) in response to this regularization effect, the weight update in SFT should follow some specific structures to lower forgetting of the knowledge learned in pretraining, which can be obtained by using the same optimizer. Moreover, we specifically compare Muon and AdamW when they are employed throughout the pretraining and SFT stages and find that Muon performs worse when finetuned for reasoning tasks. With a synthetic language modeling experiment, we demonstrate that this can come from Muon's strong tendency towards rote memorization, which may hurt pattern acquisition with a small amount of data, as for SFT.

</details>


### [196] [Verifier-Backed Hard Problem Generation for Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06660)
*Yuhang Lai,Jiazhan Feng,Yee Whye Teh,Ning Miao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出VHG框架，通过引入独立验证器构建三方自博弈（设定者-求解者-验证者），有效生成有效且困难的问题，在积分和数学推理任务上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在生成有效、困难且新颖的问题方面表现不佳，现有方法要么依赖昂贵的人类专家，要么采用朴素自博弈范式导致奖励破解和无效问题。

Method: 提出VHG框架，在传统设定者-求解者二元结构上引入独立验证者，使设定者的奖励由验证者评估的问题有效性和求解者评估的难度共同决定；实例化两种验证器变体：Hard符号验证器和Soft基于LLM的验证器。

Result: 在不定积分任务和通用数学推理任务上的实验结果表明，VHG在所有基线方法中取得了明显更优的性能。

Conclusion: 三方自博弈范式通过验证者约束有效解决了问题生成中的奖励破解问题，能够生成既有效又具有挑战性的问题。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate strong capabilities for solving scientific and mathematical problems, yet they struggle to produce valid, challenging, and novel problems - an essential component for advancing LLM training and enabling autonomous scientific research. Existing problem generation approaches either depend on expensive human expert involvement or adopt naive self-play paradigms, which frequently yield invalid problems due to reward hacking. This work introduces VHG, a verifier-enhanced hard problem generation framework built upon three-party self-play. By integrating an independent verifier into the conventional setter-solver duality, our design constrains the setter's reward to be jointly determined by problem validity (evaluated by the verifier) and difficulty (assessed by the solver). We instantiate two verifier variants: a Hard symbolic verifier and a Soft LLM-based verifier, with evaluations conducted on indefinite integral tasks and general mathematical reasoning tasks. Experimental results show that VHG substantially outperforms all baseline methods by a clear margin.

</details>


### [197] [UniPool: A Globally Shared Expert Pool for Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06665)
*Minbin Huang,Han Shi,Chuanyang Zheng,Yimeng Wu,Guoxuan Chen,Xintong Yu,Yichun Yin,Hong Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UniPool是一种新的MoE架构，用全局共享专家池替代传统的逐层专属专家集，通过池级辅助损失和NormRouter实现稳定训练。在多个模型规模上，UniPool在减少验证损失的同时，使用更少的专家参数即可匹配或超越传统MoE性能，证明专家参数可以随深度亚线性增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MoE架构中每层拥有独立的专家集，导致深度扩展与专家参数呈线性增长。作者通过路由探针实验发现深层路由存在冗余——用随机路由替代深层学习路由仅造成1.0-1.6个点的精度下降，说明当前分配规则效率低下。

Method: 提出UniPool架构：(1) 用单一全局共享专家池替代每层独立专家集；(2) 设计池级辅助损失均衡全池专家利用率；(3) 采用NormRouter实现稀疏且尺度稳定的路由分配。

Result: 在五个LLaMA架构模型规模（182M-978M参数）上，UniPool持续改进验证损失和困惑度，验证损失最高降低0.0386。精简版UniPool仅用41.6%-66.7%的专家参数即可匹配或超越传统MoE性能。

Conclusion: 共享池设计打破了专家参数必须随深度线性增长的假设，支持亚线性增长；池大小成为显式的深度缩放超参数；UniPool可与细粒度专家分解兼容。

Abstract: Modern Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures allocate expert capacity through a rigid per-layer rule: each transformer layer owns a separate expert set. This convention couples depth scaling with linear expert-parameter growth and assumes that every layer needs isolated expert capacity. However, recent analyses and our routing probe challenge this allocation rule: replacing a deeper layer's learned top-k router with uniform random routing drops downstream accuracy by only 1.0-1.6 points across multiple production MoE models. Motivated by this redundancy, we propose UniPool, an MoE architecture that treats expert capacity as a global architectural budget by replacing per-layer expert ownership with a single shared pool accessed by independent per-layer routers. To enable stable and balanced training under sharing, we introduce a pool-level auxiliary loss that balances expert utilization across the entire pool, and adopt NormRouter to provide sparse and scale-stable routing into the shared expert pool. Across five LLaMA-architecture model scales (182M, 469M, 650M, 830M, and 978M parameters) trained on 30B tokens from the Pile, UniPool consistently improves validation loss and perplexity over the matched vanilla MoE baselines. Across these scales, UniPool reduces validation loss by up to 0.0386 relative to vanilla MoE. Beyond raw loss improvement, our results identify pool size as an explicit depth-scaling hyperparameter: reduced-pool UniPool variants using only 41.6%-66.7% of the vanilla expert-parameter budget match or outperform layer-wise MoE at the tested scales. This shows that, under a shared-pool design, expert parameters need not grow linearly with depth; they can grow sublinearly while remaining more efficient and effective than vanilla MoE. Further analysis shows that UniPool's benefits compose with finer-grained expert decomposition.

</details>


<div id='cs.LO'></div>

# cs.LO [[Back]](#toc)

### [198] [A diagrammatic proof-theoretic semantics for the Greimas semiotic square](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05273)
*Michael Fowler*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文基于蜘蛛图形式系统，为格雷马斯符号学方阵的术语构造和矛盾、蕴含关系建立了一种图式证明系统，核心结果是四种元术语均可通过固定推理模式从基本构型导出，并重新解释了方阵中的"+"操作为推导构造而非逻辑组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图为格雷马斯符号学方阵提供一种形式化的、基于图式推理的证明论框架，以统一刻画其中四种元术语（如A、¬A、非A、非¬A等）的构造方式，并澄清其语义操作的性质，特别是"+"操作和否定操作与传统逻辑的区别。

Method: 采用蜘蛛图（spider diagrams）作为底层形式系统，定义基本术语的图式构型，将矛盾关系和蕴含关系解释为受推理规则支配的变换操作，通过证明树作为推演的证据。引入轮廓引入规则和栖息地变换规则，构造元术语的推导模式。

Result: 四种元术语均可从基本构型的合取对出发，通过固定的推导模式（轮廓引入+栖息地变换规则）一致地导出。图式否定不是布尔补，而是基于区域的语义对立操作。"+"操作被解释为推导构造而非逻辑组合。

Conclusion: 该框架为符号学方阵提供了组合的、基于规则的语义，复杂构型可从简单构型构造性地生成，扩展了蜘蛛图演算的表达能力，展示了图式推理可用于在统一推演框架内形式化非经典语义操作。

Abstract: We develop a diagrammatic proof system for a fragment of structural semantics inspired by the Greimas semiotic square, using spider diagrams as the underlying formalism. The basic terms are represented as diagrammatic configurations, and the relations of contradiction and implication are interpreted as transformations governed by a set of inference rules. These transformations are realised as derivations, with proof trees serving as witnesses. Our main result shows that the construction of the four meta-terms can be captured uniformly: each is derivable from a conjunctive pair of basic configurations via a fixed derivation schema composed of contour introduction and habitat transformation rules. This yields a proof-theoretic account of the combinatorial operation underlying meta-term formation, and provides a semantic interpretation of the Greimasian operation `+' as a derivational construction rather than a logical combination. We further show that diagrammatic negation in this setting is not a Boolean complement, but a restricted, zone-determined semantic counter-position, reflecting the relational character of opposition in structural semiotics. The resulting framework provides a compositional, rule-based semantics in which complex configurations are generated constructively from simpler ones. In addition to extending the expressive scope of spider diagram calculi, the system illustrates how diagrammatic reasoning can be used to formalise non-classical semantic operations within a unified inferential setting.

</details>


### [199] [Paraconsistent Semantics for Extended Fuzzy Logic Programs via Approximation Fixpoint Theory [Extended Version]](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05286)
*Pascal Kettmann,Hannes Strass,Jesse Heyninck,Jeroen Spaans*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文使用近似不动点理论，为同时包含失败否定和强否定的模糊逻辑程序定义了良好行为的语义，该框架推广了多种现有语义并产生了新语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逻辑程序中否定可以有多种解释（如"失败即否定"和强否定），为同时包含这两种否定的逻辑程序定义语义具有挑战性，当结合模糊性等其他扩展时更加困难。

Method: 使用近似不动点理论框架，为同时包含失败否定和强否定的模糊逻辑程序制定语义。

Result: 该框架不仅推广了多种现有语义，还衍生出一系列新的语义。

Conclusion: 近似不动点理论为处理混合否定的模糊逻辑程序提供了统一的语义基础，具有普适性和扩展性。

Abstract: In logic programming, negation can be interpreted in various ways. Probably best known is the concept of "negation as failure", where "$\mathit{not}\, p$" is true if we have no evidence for $p$. On the other hand, strong negation requires that we have evidence for $p$ being false. Defining semantics for logic programs containing both kinds of negation is a challenging task, and this becomes even more challenging when combining this with other extensions of logic programming, e.g. fuzziness. In this work, we use the framework of approximating fixpoint theory to formulate well-behaved semantics for fuzzy logic programs containing both "by-failure" and strong negation. We show that this framework generalizes several existing semantics as well as giving rise to a host of new semantics.

</details>


### [200] [A Practical Specification Language for Automatic Quantum Program Verification (Technical Report)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05786)
*Wei-Lun Tsai,Yu-Fang Chen,Ondřej Lengál*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 提出了一种扩展的基于集合的规约语言和线性复杂度的规约到自动机转换算法，实现了固定量子比特量子程序的完全自动Hoare风格验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子程序的Hoare风格验证方法要么不支持完全自动验证，要么在将高级基于集合的断言转换为自动机时面临指数级爆炸问题，严重限制了实用性。

Method: 引入扩展的基于集合的规约语言，并设计了一种规约到自动机的转换算法，通过受控自动机构建和量子比特重排序，使复杂度达到线性（与量子比特数成正比）。

Result: 生成的紧凑自动机能够以以往不可行的规模对固定量子比特量子程序进行完全自动的Hoare风格验证，同时在未牺牲效率的情况下大幅提升了表达能力。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持效率的同时显著提高了规约语言的表达能力，实现了量子程序验证的可扩展性突破。

Abstract: Hoare-style verification provides a principled foundation for reasoning about the correctness of quantum programs, but existing approaches do not allow fully automatic verification. While automata-based verification scales well when specifications are given directly as automata, prior frameworks incur exponential blow-up when translating high-level set-based assertions into automata, which severely limits practicality. We introduce an extended set-based specification language and a specification-to-automata translation algorithm whose complexity is linear in the number of qubits, enabled by controlled automaton construction and qubit reordering. The resulting compact automata enable fully automatic Hoare-style verification of fixed-qubit quantum programs at previously infeasible scales, while substantially improving expressiveness without compromising efficiency.

</details>


### [201] [Self-Correcting Gossip Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05801)
*Giorgio Cignarale,Hans van Ditmarsch,Stephan Felber,Malvin Gattinger,Hugo Rincon Galeana,Vaishnavi Sundararajan*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文研究带错误的自我修正八卦协议，提出用动态认知逻辑替代时间认知逻辑来建模分布式系统中的错误修正，探讨无中心协调下的传输错误修正、对最优性的影响，以及与有限记忆和全信息协议的对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作在时间认知逻辑中研究了带错误的分布式协议，但缺乏从动态认知逻辑角度系统分析自我修正八卦协议的框架。

Method: 提出动态认知逻辑（Dynamic Epistemic Logic）来建模和推理错误消息的自我修正过程，分析无中心协调下的协议执行机制。

Result: 展示了如何在没有中央协调的情况下修正传输错误，分析了该修正机制对协议最优性的影响，并与有限记忆协议和全信息协议进行了比较。

Conclusion: 动态认知逻辑为分析和设计自我修正的分布式八卦协议提供了新的理论工具，在错误容忍和最优性之间存在重要权衡。

Abstract: We investigate self-correcting gossip protocols with errors. In distributed computing, protocols with errors have been widely investigated in temporal epistemic logics. Instead, we propose a dynamic epistemic logic. We show how to correct transmission errors due to faulty messages without a central authority coordinating protocol execution, how this affects optimality, and how this compares to bounded memory and full information protocols.

</details>


### [202] [Decidability Results for Fragments of First-Order Logic via a Symbolic Model Property](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05840)
*Neta Elad,Sharon Shoham*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出将符号结构从线性整数算术推广到任意标准基理论，利用符号模型性质证明多个扩展分层公式片段的可判定性，并通过通用构造方法实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将符号结构推广到任意标准基理论，以更通用的方式利用符号模型性质证明一阶逻辑片段的可判定性，特别是放松分层公式中量词交替约束的扩展片段。

Method: 1) 将符号结构从线性整数算术推广到任意具有标准模型的基理论；2) 定义符号模型性质；3) 通过从任意模型构造符号模型的通用方法，证明扩展分层公式片段的符号模型性质，允许在限制条件下引入自循环函数。

Result: 证明了多个扩展分层公式片段的可判定性，这些片段放松了量词交替约束，允许一种排序具有自循环函数（在特定限制下）。

Conclusion: 符号模型性质为证明一阶逻辑片段可判定性提供了一般性框架，且文中采用的通用构造方法可实例化到其他受类似限制的片段。

Abstract: Recently, symbolic structures were proposed as finite representations of potentially infinite first-order structures, where Linear Integer Arithmetic terms and formulas define the domain and interpretations of a structure. We generalize symbolic structures to use any base theory that admits a standard model. Symbolic structures induce a symbolic model property, which holds for a fragment of first-order logic if every satisfiable formula in the fragment has a symbolic model. The symbolic model property implies decidability, since the model-checking problem for symbolic structures is decidable. We use the symbolic model property to prove decidability for several fragments that extend the fragment of stratified formulas, relaxing the quantifier-alternation constraints by allowing one sort to have self-looping functions, under certain restrictions. To establish the symbolic model property for these fragments we construct a symbolic model for a formula from an arbitrary model. The construction and its correctness are proved in a generic fashion, which may be instantiated to other similarly restricted fragments.

</details>


### [203] [Graded Monad Coalgebras for Continuous-Time Transition Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06268)
*Elena Di Lavore,Jonas Forster,Mario Román*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文引入分级余代数（graded coalgebras）和分级单子（graded monads）来建模连续时间迁移系统，发展了包括分级分配律、终端余代数存在条件等理论，定义了分支时间语义和迹语义，并建立了余代数模态逻辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统函子余代数只能描述离散步进演化的迁移系统，无法处理连续时间系统。本文旨在填补这一空白，将余代数理论推广到连续时间领域。

Method: 引入分级单子的分级余代数作为连续时间迁移系统的模型；发展分级余代数理论（包括分级分配律、终端余代数存在条件）；定义分支时间和迹两种语义，并与Feller-Dynkin过程建立联系；构建余代数模态逻辑并给出不变性和表达性的判定标准。

Result: 成功建立了分级余代数理论框架，给出了终端余代数的存在条件，定义了两种语义并关联到已有工作，建立了模态逻辑的不变性和表达性判别准则。

Conclusion: 分级余代数为连续时间迁移系统提供了统一的余代数理论框架，将经典余代数理论推广到了连续时间领域，统一了分支时间和迹语义，并建立了相应的模态逻辑。

Abstract: Functor coalgebras capture a wide range of transition systems that must however evolve in discrete steps. We introduce graded coalgebras of graded monads and propose them to model continuous-time transition systems. We develop the theory of graded coalgebras-including graded distributive laws between graded monads-and we give conditions for the existence of terminal coalgebras. We define both branching-time and trace semantics, linking them to recent work on Feller-Dynkin processes. Finally, we develop coalgebraic modal logics for both process semantics and state criteria for invariance and expressivity.

</details>


### [204] [Relational Dualities and Bisimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06533)
*Piotr Kozicki,Alex Kavvos*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文通过构建Tarski对偶和Thomason对偶的关系扩展，将系统间的关系（如互模拟）与谓词间的关系对应起来，为不同系统间的公式推理提供了证明系统基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kripke语义通过范畴对偶连接了关系框架与代数结构，但已有对偶理论仅处理系统间的映射（如函数），未能覆盖系统间的关系（如互模拟）。本文旨在将对偶现象扩展到关系层面，建立系统间关系与谓词间关系之间的对应。

Method: 构建了Tarski对偶（针对无穷经典命题逻辑）和Thomason对偶（针对无穷经典模态逻辑）的关系扩展版本，将关系框架范畴中的关系与代数范畴中的关系对应起来。

Result: 成功建立了系统间关系（如互模拟）与谓词间关系之间的对偶对应，并由此衍生出支持不同系统间公式推理的证明系统。

Conclusion: 将经典的范畴对偶从映射层面推广到关系层面，为程序逻辑中跨系统的推理提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The Kripke semantics of various logics arises via categorical dualities between a category of relational frames and their maps, and a category of algebras and logical homomorphisms. When the relational frames are considered as computational systems (e.g. the states of a machine), the corresponding algebra is one of logical predicates on these systems (e.g. predicates on these states, i.e. program logics). Our aim is to extend this phenomenon to relations, putting well-behaved relations between systems (e.g. bisimulations) in correspondence with relations between predicates. This is achieved by constructing particular relational extensions of Tarski duality (for infinitary classical propositional logic) and Thomason duality (for infinitary classical modal logic). We sketch how these dualities give rise to a proof system that relates formulae between different systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.PL'></div>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [Beyond BLEU: A Semantic Evaluation Method for Code Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05282)
*Julius Näumann,Sven Keidel,Amir Molzam Sharifloo,Mira Mezini*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于语义等价性的代码翻译评估方法，通过语义正确性分数替代BLEU等语法指标，并在二进制反编译任务中验证了LLM方法优于启发式方法，同时发现BLEU与语义正确性相关性极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码翻译评估指标（如BLEU）仅衡量句法相似性，忽略了程序语义等价性，无法准确评估翻译的正确性。

Method: 将编译器测试方法应用于代码翻译领域，提出语义正确性分数（即翻译后产生正确执行结果的比例），并用于评估微调后的LLM反编译器与启发式反编译器。

Result: LLM方法显著优于启发式方法；BLEU分数与语义正确性的相关系数仅-0.127至0.354，表明语法指标无法预测功能准确性。

Conclusion: 基于语义等价性的评估方法更适用于代码翻译任务，语法相似性指标与真实正确性无关。

Abstract: Code translation is one of the core capabilities of LLMs. However, evaluating the correctness of translations remains difficult, as commonly used metrics such as BLEU measure only syntactic similarity, disregarding program semantics. We propose a novel evaluation methodology for code translation tasks, emphasizing semantic equivalence over surface-level string similarity. Our approach applies established compiler testing methodology to a new domain, allowing the assessment of an LLM fine-tuned for binary lifting tasks (i.e. decompiling binaries to higher-level representations). We introduce a semantic correctness score, defined as the proportion of translations that produce correct execution outcomes, and demonstrate its application by evaluating LLM-based and heuristic decompilers. Our findings show that LLM-based approaches significantly outperform heuristic ones, while BLEU scores show negligible correlation with semantic correctness (r = -0.127 to 0.354), demonstrating that syntactic metrics fail to predict functional accuracy.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [206] [A GPU-Accelerated Hybrid Method for a Class of Multi-Depot Vehicle Routing Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05208)
*Zhenyu Lei,Jin-Kao Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合算法解决多车场车辆路径问题（MDVRP），整合了学习驱动的多样性控制交叉算子、多惩罚评估函数引导的多车场可行-不可行搜索框架、专用车场局部搜索算子，以及基于张量的GPU加速与多移动更新策略。在三个MDVRP变体的基准实例上，该算法尤其是大规模实例上表现出与最先进方法高度竞争的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MDVRP在多种实际应用中普遍存在，但由于其固有复杂性，求解计算上极具挑战性，现有方法在大规模问题上仍有提升空间。

Method: 提出一种混合算法，包含：(1) 学习驱动的多样性控制路径交换交叉算子；(2) 多惩罚评估函数引导的多车场可行-不可行搜索框架；(3) 两个专用车场相关局部搜索算子；(4) 基于张量的GPU加速与多移动更新策略的增强版本。

Result: 在三个MDVRP变体的基准实例上，所提算法与最先进方法相比具有高度竞争力，尤其在大规模实例上表现突出。

Conclusion: 该混合算法通过集成学习驱动搜索、多惩罚评估、专用局部算子和GPU加速，有效提升了MDVRP的求解质量和计算效率，特别是在大规模场景下具有显著优势。

Abstract: Multi-depot vehicle routing problems (MDVRPs) are prevalent in a variety of practical applications. However, they are computationally challenging to solve due to their inherent complexity. This paper proposes an effective hybrid algorithm for a class of MDVRPs. The algorithm integrates a learning-driven, diversity-controlled route-exchange crossover and a multi-depot-supported feasible-and-infeasible search framework guided by a multi-penalty evaluation function. Two dedicated depot-related local search operators are incorporated to further strengthen the search capability in multi-depot settings. To improve computational efficiency and scalability, an enhanced version of the algorithm is developed that uses a tensor-based GPU acceleration combined with a novel multi-move update strategy. Extensive computational experiments on benchmark instances of three MDVRP variants show that the proposed algorithms are highly competitive with state-of-the-art methods, especially for large-scale instances.

</details>


### [207] [DexSim2Real: Foundation Model-Guided Sim-to-Real Transfer for Generalizable Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05241)
*Zijian Zeng,Fei Ding,Huiming Yang,Xianwei Li,Yuhao Liao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexSim2Real是一个利用视觉-语言基础模型弥合灵巧操作模拟到现实差距的集成框架，通过基础模型引导域随机化、触觉-视觉交叉注意力策略和渐进式技能课程三大组件，在6个灵巧操作任务中达到78.2%的真实世界成功率，将模拟到现实性能差距缩小至仅8.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟到现实的迁移是灵巧操作策略部署的关键瓶颈，现有方法依赖人工设计的域随机化或任务特定适应，缺乏跨多样操作场景的泛化能力。

Method: 提出DexSim2Real框架，包含三个组件：(1) 基础模型引导的域随机化(FM-DR)，使用视觉语言模型作为视觉真实性批评家，通过闭环CMA-ES优化仿真参数；(2) 触觉-视觉交叉注意力策略(TVCAP)，将交叉注意力视觉-触觉融合适配到零样本模拟到现实强化学习；(3) 渐进式技能课程(PSC)，基于LLM任务分解并针对接触密集型灵巧任务定制难度调度器。

Result: 在6个挑战性灵巧操作任务和盲评中，DexSim2Real达到78.2%的平均真实世界成功率，优于DrEureka和DeXtreme，将模拟到现实性能差距缩小至仅8.3%。

Conclusion: DexSim2Real通过视觉语言基础模型的直接视觉反馈，有效弥合了灵巧操作的模拟到现实鸿沟，验证了基础模型引导的域随机化在复杂操作任务中的优势。

Abstract: Sim-to-real transfer remains a critical bottleneck for deploying dexterous manipulation policies learned in simulation to real-world robots. Existing approaches rely on manually designed domain randomization or task-specific adaptation, limiting their generalizability across diverse manipulation scenarios. We present DexSim2Real, an integrated framework that leverages vision-language foundation models to bridge the sim-to-real gap for dexterous manipulation. Our system combines three components: (1) Foundation Model-Guided Domain Randomization (FM-DR), which uses a vision-language model as a visual realism critic to optimize simulation parameters via closed-loop CMA-ES, complementing text-based approaches like DrEureka with direct visual feedback; (2) a Tactile-Visual Cross-Attention Policy (TVCAP) that adapts cross-attention visuo-tactile fusion to zero-shot sim-to-real RL; and (3) a Progressive Skill Curriculum (PSC) that builds on LLM-based task decomposition with a difficulty scheduler tailored to contact-rich dexterous tasks. Extensive experiments on six challenging manipulation tasks with blinded evaluation demonstrate that DexSim2Real achieves a 78.2% average real-world success rate, outperforming DrEureka and DeXtreme while reducing the sim-to-real performance gap to only 8.3%.

</details>


### [208] [Track A*: Fast Visibility-Aware Trajectory Planning for Active Target Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05338)
*Hanxuan Chen,Kangli Wang,Ji Pei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TA* 是一种面向离线主动目标跟踪的搜索轨迹规划器，在四维时空网格上结合分层 DAG 搜索、BVH 障碍距离缓存、波束剪枝与多射线可见性评估，在 1000 场景测试中实现 100% 收敛，平均规划时间降低 23.0 倍，可见性仅下降 0.15 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线参考轨迹对构建多模态跟踪数据集和在可重复条件下基准测试在线跟踪规划器至关重要，现有方法在计算效率与可见性质量之间难以兼顾。

Method: 在离散化的四维时空网格 (x, y, z, t) 上，将问题建模为分层有向无环图 (DAG) 搜索，并引入三种工程优化：基于包围体层次结构 (BVH) 的跨时间障碍距离缓存、逐层波束剪枝、可配置多射线可见性评估器，采用波束剪枝启发式搜索高效求解。

Result: 在 1000 场景 CARLA 压力测试中全部收敛，耗时 45 秒（32 个 worker）；与未优化的优先队列 A* 基线相比，平均规划时间降低 23.0 倍，最差情况降低 11.8 倍，收敛率从 56.9% 提升至 100%；在基线收敛子集上，可见性平均仅下降 0.15 个百分点，无场景下降超过 5 个百分点。

Conclusion: TA* 是一种实用的离线参考轨迹规划器，在牺牲严格理论最优性的前提下实现了实际可扩展性，但在 Town07 密集植被等环境中仍存在局限和失败案例。

Abstract: Offline reference trajectories for active target tracking are needed both for building multi-modal tracking datasets and for benchmarking online tracking planners under repeatable conditions. We present Track A star (TA star), an offline search-based trajectory planner that targets the visibility-aware target tracking objective on a discretized four-dimensional spatio-temporal grid (x, y, z, t). TA star combines a layered Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG) search with three engineering optimizations: cross-time obstacle distance caching against a Bounding Volume Hierarchy (BVH), per-layer beam pruning, and a configurable multi-ray visibility evaluator. TA star employs a beam-pruned heuristic search on this discrete graph to efficiently find high-quality tracking trajectories. While it trades strict theoretical optimality for practical scalability, our empirical results demonstrate robust, near-baseline visibility performance at a fraction of the computational cost. On a 1000-scenario stress test across eight CARLA Optimized maps, TA star converges on all scenarios and completes in 45 s using 32 workers; on a 248-scenario controlled comparison against an unoptimized priority-queue A star baseline (BinaryHeap implementation) under identical scenario inputs and a 5 x 10^6 expansion cap, TA star reduces mean planning time by 23.0x and worst-case planning time by 11.8x, while raising convergence from 56.9% to 100%. On the n=141 baseline-converged subset, TA star changes average visibility by only -0.15 percentage points (pp), with no scenario exceeding a 5 pp drop. We position TA star as a practical offline reference planner under these specific conditions, with limitations and failure cases discussed for environments such as Town07 dense vegetation.

</details>


### [209] [Monitoring autonomous persistent surveillance missions using invariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06062)
*Vladislav Nenchev,Prodromos Sotiriadis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了当自主机器人的决策模块为黑盒时，如何通过运行时监控实现持久监视任务。环境被划分为有限区域，每个区域的不确定状态在观测时下降、未观测时上升。系统被建模为具有线性参数变化动力学的状态依赖混合系统，并设计了基于离线计算不变量的监控器。针对大规模空间难以计算全局不变量的问题，提出了一种组合式监控方法：通过分布式计算每个不确定区域的低维不变量，并在在线阶段检查其合取。在常见独立性假设下，该组合监控器相对于全局系统不变量是可靠且完备的。通过真实机器人在迷宫中的持久监视案例验证了其实际适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常假设自主系统的决策模块是白盒或可访问的，但在实际应用中，许多商用或第三方自治堆栈是黑盒的，无法获取内部状态与决策逻辑。因此，需要在黑盒假设下设计有效的运行时监控方法，确保持久监视任务中不确定性的可控性。

Method: 将环境划分为有限区域并建模不确定状态演化；将闭环系统建模为状态依赖混合系统（线性参数变化动力学）；离线计算全局不变量作为监控基准；针对大规模空间，提出组合式监控：分布式计算各区域的低维不变量，在线检查其合取条件。在独立性假设下证明了组合方法的可靠性和完备性。

Result: 在真实机器人持久监视迷宫的真实案例中，该方法成功验证了组合监控器的有效性，表明其能够在黑盒自治堆栈条件下实现可靠的运行时监视，且组合方法克服了全局不变量计算的可扩展性问题。

Conclusion: 提出了一种针对黑盒自主机器人持久监视任务的运行时监控框架，通过组合式不变量计算与在线合取检查，在保证可靠性和完备性的同时解决了大规模环境下的计算难题，实验验证了其实用性。

Abstract: This paper studies runtime monitoring for persistent surveillance by autonomous robots when the autonomy stack is a black box. The environment is partitioned into finitely many parts, each carrying an uncertainty state that decreases when observed and increases otherwise. We model the closed loop as a state-dependent hybrid system with linear parameter varying dynamics and design a monitor based on an invariant computed offline. As this invariant is typically hard to obtain for large to-be-surveyed spaces, we propose a compositional monitor obtained by decentralized computation of low-dimensional invariant sets for each uncertainty region, and checking their conjunction online. Under common independence assumptions, the compositional monitor is sound and complete with respect to the full-system invariant. The approach is applied in a case study with a real robot persistently monitoring a labyrinth, emphasizing its applicability in practice.

</details>


### [210] [Passive Fault Tolerance through Tension-to-Thrust Feed-Forward: Hybrid Input-to-State Stability for Decentralized Multi-UAV Slung-Load Transport under Abrupt Cable Severance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05339)
*Hadi Hajieghrary,Paul Schmitt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种被动架构，将每架无人机的实测缆绳张力直接馈入高度推力指令，并辅以PD、防摆和投影级联控制，实现多无人机吊挂运输中突发缆绳断裂后的分散式恢复。通过条件混合实际输入-状态稳定性（ISS）证书证明恢复包络，在五机仿真中验证了0.312-0.328 m RMSE和76.1-95.2 mm峰值下垂，张力前馈是关键恢复机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多无人机吊挂运输中缆绳突发断裂会重新分配载荷并改变主动约束集，而现有控制器依赖协调力分配、同伴状态交换或固定缆绳拓扑，缺乏经过认证的分散式恢复机制。

Method: 提出被动架构，将每架无人机测量的缆绳张力直接作为高度推力指令的前馈项（T_i^{ff}=T_i），并采用PD、防摆和投影级联控制器保持本地跟踪可行性。主要贡献是条件混合实际输入-状态稳定性（ISS）证书，将松弛偏移有界紧缆缩减、断后Lyapunov有界跳跃、故障间衰减和每故障周期收缩ρ∈(0,1)组合成显式恢复包络。

Result: 在Drake多体仿真中，五架无人机、10 kg载荷、Kelvin-Voigt缆绳、Dryden风场下，单次和双次断裂场景中闭环达到0.312-0.328 m RMSE、76.1-95.2 mm峰值下垂，恢复时间在一个载荷摆周期内。禁用张力前馈后巡航误差增加34-39%，峰值下垂放大3.6-4.0倍。

Conclusion: 本地张力前馈是所测试分散式级联中的主导被动恢复机制，该架构无需协调力分配或同伴状态交换即可实现突发缆绳断裂后的认证恢复。

Abstract: Abrupt cable severance in multi-UAV slung-load transport redistributes load and changes the active constraint set, leaving limited time for fault diagnosis and reconfiguration. Existing controllers rely on coordinated force allocation, peer-state exchange, or fixed cable topology, and therefore lack a certified decentralized recovery mechanism for unannounced severance. We present a passive architecture that routes each vehicle's measured cable tension directly into its altitude thrust command, $T_i^{\mathrm{ff}}=T_i$, while a surrounding proportional-derivative, anti-swing, and projection cascade preserves local tracking feasibility. The main contribution is a conditional hybrid practical input-to-state-stability certificate that composes a slack-excursion-bounded taut-cable reduction, bounded post-severance Lyapunov jumps, inter-fault decay, and per-fault-cycle contraction $ρ\in (0,1)$ into an explicit recovery envelope under stated actuator, slack, and dwell assumptions. We validate the controller in Drake multibody simulation with five vehicles, a 10 kg payload, Kelvin-Voigt cables, Dryden wind, and single- and dual-severance schedules: the closed loop attains 0.312-0.328 m RMSE, 76.1-95.2 mm peak sag, and recovery within one payload-pendulum period. Disabling the identity inflates cruise error by 34-39% and peak sag by 3.6x-4.0x, identifying local tension feed-forward as the dominant passive recovery mechanism in the tested decentralized cascade.

</details>


### [211] [Creative Robot Tool Use by Counterfactual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05411)
*M. Tuluhan Akbulut,Varun Satheesh,Ahmed Jaafar,Alper Ahmetoglu,Shane Parr,Aditya Ganeshan,Shivam Vats,George Konidaris*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于因果推理的机器人创造性工具使用框架，通过动力学模型中的模拟实验发现工具与任务间的因果关系，结合VLM特征建议和反事实工具生成进行因果特征发现，并基于因果特征进行工具分类和技能迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人工具使用缺乏因果推理能力，无法识别工具超越其主要目的的功能特性，导致工具选择不可靠且技能迁移能力弱。

Method: 1. 在动力学模型中进行模拟实验发现因果关系；2. 将因果发现解耦为VLM特征建议和反事实工具生成（通过几何与物理特征扰动）；3. 基于因果特征对新物体分类；4. 通过条件关键点匹配迁移工具使用技能。

Result: 在三种任务（用不同棍子够远端物体、用不同物品舀碗中糖果、用不同箱子/板条箱作为垫脚平台取高处物体）中，因果特征识别和物理工具属性基础化导致更可靠的工具选择和更强的技能关键点迁移。

Conclusion: 将因果推理与物理基础相结合能够提升机器人创造性工具使用的可靠性和泛化能力。

Abstract: We propose a causal reasoning framework for creative robot tool use where a suitable tool for a task is correctly identified for use beyond its primary objectives. The proposed framework first discovers the causal relationships between the tool and the task by conducting simulated experiments in a dynamics model. We decouple the causal discovery problem into two complementary components: VLM-based feature suggestion and counterfactual tool generation via targeted geometric and physical feature perturbations. Then, novel objects are classified based on identified causal features, and the tool use skill is transferred via keypoint matching conditioned on the identified causal features. By reconstructing the task in a dynamics model, our approach grounds tool use in the physics of the problem. We illustrate our approach in reaching a distant object with different sticks, scooping candies from a bowl using diverse items, and using different boxes or crates as stepping platforms to retrieve an object from a high shelf. Our baseline comparisons show that identifying causal features and grounding them in physical tool properties leads to more reliable tool selection and stronger skill keypoint transfer.

</details>


### [212] [Lie Group Formulation of Recursive Dynamics Algorithms of Higher Order for Floating-Base Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06498)
*Ahmed Ali,Chiara Gabellieri,Antonio Franchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了李群框架下浮动机器人（基座位姿在SE(3)上演化，机构构型在T^{n1}×R^{n2}上）牛顿-欧拉、聚合体惯性和混合动力学算法的高阶时间导数计算方法，验证了Coriolis矩阵的无源性、惯性张量的导数不变性，并在12自由度空中机械臂上实现了最高五阶动力学解析与数值求解，计算开销随导数阶次二次增长，远优于自动微分的指数增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有浮动机器人动力学算法（牛顿-欧拉、聚合体惯性、混合动力学）仅支持零阶和一阶导数计算，缺乏对高阶时间导数的通用求解框架，限制了在模型预测控制、轨迹优化、灵敏度分析等需要高阶动力学信息的应用场景中的使用。

Method: 采用SE(3)上的空间旋量表示，推导了Lie群框架下浮动机器人三种动力学算法的高阶时间导数递归；将递归整合为闭合形式运动方程，识别了满足无源性的Coriolis矩阵，并证明了聚合体惯性张量在所有导数阶次下保持不变；在12自由度空中机械臂上进行了解析与数值验证。

Result: 成功推导了7次Lie导数下的闭合运动方程；在12自由度空中机械臂上实现了最高五阶动力学的解析表达与数值仿真；计算开销随导数阶次呈二次增长（O(d²)），而自动微分基线呈指数增长（O(e^d)）。

Conclusion: 所提方法为浮动机器人系统的高阶动力学计算提供了一种高效、可扩展的解析框架，相比自动微分具有显著的计算优势，可广泛应用于轨迹优化、最优控制、参数估计等需要高阶导数信息的机器人学任务。

Abstract: In this paper, we describe procedures for computing higher-order time derivatives of the Lie-group Newton-Euler, Articulated-Body Inertia, and hybrid dynamics algorithms for floating-base trees, where the base configuration evolves on SE(3) and the attached mechanism is an open kinematic tree with configuration on the (n1+n2)-dimensional manifold T^{n1} \times R^{n2}, using spatial representation of twists. After presenting the algorithms, we collect the resulting recursions into closed-form equations of motion, identifying an admissible Coriolis matrix satisfying the passivity property, and showing that the articulated inertia tensor remains unchanged across all time derivatives. We then apply the developed methods to a 12-DoF aerial manipulator to derive analytical expressions for its geometric forward and inverse dynamics along with their first time derivatives whereas the numerical simulations successfully evaluate these dynamics up to fifth order. Finally, to demonstrate their practical utility, we benchmark the proposed extensions and show that, in the considered tests, their computational cost scales quadratically with the derivative order, whereas the automatic-differentiation baseline exhibits exponential scaling.

</details>


### [213] [Contact-Free Grasp Stability Prediction with In-Hand Time-of-Flight Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05461)
*Kyle DuFrene,Cindy Grimm*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种使用多区域飞行时间传感器的无接触式抓取稳定性预测方法，无需抓取物体即可在15Hz频率下预测抓取稳定性，在2500+次真实抓取数据上训练的分类器达到85.5%-86.0%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抓取规划方法在高噪声传感器等条件下性能下降，而基于触觉的抓取稳定性分类器需要接触并抓取物体才能检测失败，效率低下。

Method: 在夹爪远端指节安装多区域飞行时间传感器，在接触物体前采集深度信息，训练分类器预测抓取稳定性，无需实际抓取即可输出预测结果，循环频率达15Hz。

Result: 在15种物体上收集2500+次真实抓取数据训练模型，对3个未见物体的验证集准确率85.5%，对3个未见物体的测试集准确率86.0%。

Conclusion: 无接触式抓取稳定性预测方法有效加快了稳定性分类过程，在未知物体上展现了良好的泛化能力和分类性能。

Abstract: Current approaches to grasp planning for robotics demonstrate high success rates, but degrade with noisy sensors and other factors. Previous works have proposed tactile-based grasp stability classifiers to detect failures, but these approaches rely on making contact and grasping the object to do so. We propose a contact-free grasp stability predictor using multi-zone time-of-flight sensors mounted in the distal links of a gripper. Our method, as it does not require grasping the object to make a prediction, significantly speeds up the stability classification process, cycling at 15 Hz. We collected over 2,500 real-world grasps across 15 objects to train a classifier. Additionally, we conducted grasp attempts over six additional unseen objects, three for validation and model selection, and three for model testing. Our approach demonstrated strong classification performance, with an accuracy of 85.5% on validation and 86.0% on test objects.

</details>


### [214] [Robust $\mathcal{H}_\infty$ Controller Design For INDI-Controlled Quadrotor Using Online Parameter Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05483)
*Tom Aantjes,Till M. Blaha,Spilios Theodoulis,Ewoud J. J. Smeur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对基于增量非线性动态逆（INDI）的四旋翼控制器，提出了一种结合在线参数辨识与鲁棒增益调度的外环增益整定方法，通过信号基H∞闭环成形合成增益调度级联姿态控制器，在仿真和飞行实验中验证了其在参数不确定性下的良好跟踪性能与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然INDI控制器的物理参数可在半秒内在线估计，但缺乏一种鲁棒的方法根据模型参数调节外环增益，这限制了INDI控制器在实际应用中的性能。

Method: 采用基于INDI的内环结合在线系统参数辨识，使用信号基H∞闭环成形为对称四旋翼合成增益调度级联姿态控制器，并加入前馈滤波器。

Result: 控制器展现出良好的稳定性裕度；非线性仿真验证了不确定性下的有效跟踪性能；飞行实验中即使辨识参数远超预设不确定范围，当执行器时间常数低于40ms时仍能保持可接受的飞行性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的鲁棒增益调度方法能够有效整定INDI控制器外环增益，在参数大幅不确定条件下依然保持良好性能，执行器时间常数是关键约束（需低于40ms）。

Abstract: It has recently been shown that all physical parameters of an Incremental Nonlinear Dynamic Inversion (INDI) controller can be estimated onboard a multirotor within half a second, which is fast enough to do the full identification during a throw in the air. However, a robust method to tune outer loop gains for this feedback-linearizing INDI controller depending on the model parameters is still missing. This work presents the design of a robust gain-scheduled controller for attitude control of quadrotor, using an INDI-based inner loop with online identification of its system parameters. A gain-scheduled cascaded attitude controller with a feedforward filter is synthesized for a symmetric quadrotor using signal-based $\mathcal{H}_\infty$ closed-loop shaping. The resulting controller exhibits good stability margins, with nonlinear simulations confirming effective tracking performance under uncertainty. Experimental evaluation is also conducted through flight tests with full online parameter identification. Even though the identified parameters during these tests are far outside the defined uncertainty range, acceptable flight performance comparable to simulation results is maintained for actuator time constants below 40 ms.

</details>


### [215] [Cross-Modal Navigation with Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06595)
*Shuo Liu,Xinzichen Li,Christopher Amato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CRONA框架，利用多智能体强化学习实现跨模态导航，通过控制相关的辅助信念和集中式多模态评论家提升协作效率。实验表明，多智能体方法在视觉-声学导航任务中显著优于单智能体基线，并揭示了不同协作模式在不同场景下的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量多模态数据获取困难，单模态模型难以处理丰富的多模态输入，且跨模态协作在多智能体框架下的潜力尚未充分挖掘。

Method: 提出CRONA多智能体强化学习框架，利用控制相关的辅助信念和集中式多模态评论家（含全局状态）提升跨模态智能体间的协作。

Result: 在视觉-声学导航任务上，多智能体方法在性能和效率上显著优于单智能体基线。同质协作适用于短程显著线索导航；异质协作（互补模态）普遍高效；复杂大环境需更丰富的多模态感知和更大模型容量。

Conclusion: 跨模态多智能体协作是可扩展的导航范式，不同场景需匹配不同的协作策略，未来需关注更复杂环境下的感知与模型扩展。

Abstract: Robust embodied navigation relies on complementary sensory cues. However, high-quality and well-aligned multi-modal data is often difficult to obtain in practice. Training a monolithic model is also challenging as rich multi-modal inputs induce complex representations and substantially enlarge the policy space. Cross-modal collaboration among lightweight modality-specialized agents offers a scalable paradigm. It enables flexible deployment and parallel execution, while preserving the strength of each modality. In this paper, we propose \textbf{CRONA}, a Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) framework for \textbf{Cro}ss-Modal \textbf{Na}vigation. CRONA improves collaboration by leveraging control-relevant auxiliary beliefs and a centralized multi-modal critic with global state. Experiments on visual-acoustic navigation tasks show that multi-agent methods significantly improve performance and efficiency over single-agent baselines. We find that homogeneous collaboration with limited modalities is sufficient for short-range navigation under salient cues; heterogeneous collaboration among agents with complementary modalities is generally efficient and effective; and navigation in large, complex environments requires both richer multi-modal perception and increased model capacity.

</details>


### [216] [Real-world Latency Analysis of Vehicular Visible Light Communication with Multiple LED Transmitters and an Event-Based Camera](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05541)
*Ryota Soga,Tsukasa Shimizu,Shintaro Shiba,Quan Kong,Shan Lu,Takaya Yamazato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于事件相机的可见光通信系统，针对车辆到一切(V2X)应用，解决了带宽饱和、多发射机接收和延迟表征三大挑战。采用正事件模式并设计协议抑制事件生成，同时保持通信距离和宽视场；提出多发射机识别方法，实现最多三个LED的同时接收；在真实车辆场景中评估端到端延迟，满足协同感知要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机具有高时间分辨率、低延迟和宽动态范围等优势，是可见光通信(VLC)的理想接收器，但现有方案面临带宽饱和、无法同时接收多个发射机信号以及缺乏实际场景延迟表征等问题，亟需系统性解决方案。

Method: 采用正事件模式并设计专用协议抑制事件生成；提出多发射机识别与分离方法；在真实车辆场景中测量端到端延迟。

Result: 实现了最多三个LED的同时接收；端到端延迟满足协同感知需求；在保持通信距离和宽视场的前提下有效抑制了带宽饱和。

Conclusion: 基于事件相机的VLC系统可成为现有V2X技术（如射频）的可行补充方案。

Abstract: Event cameras offer high temporal resolution, low latency, and wide dynamic range, making them promising receivers for visible light communication (VLC) in vehicle-to-everything (V2X) applications. This work presents an event-camera-based VLC system addressing three key challenges: bandwidth saturation, multi-transmitter reception, and latency characterization.
  We adopt a positive-event-only mode and design a protocol that suppresses event generation while maintaining communication distance and a wide field of view. We also propose a method to identify multiple transmitters and demonstrate simultaneous reception from up to three LEDs. Finally, we evaluate end-to-end latency in real vehicular scenarios and show that the system meets cooperative perception requirements. These results demonstrate that event-camera-based VLC is a feasible complement to existing V2X technologies (e.g., RF).

</details>


### [217] [On the Emergence of Pendular Structure in Multi-Contact Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05707)
*Lingxue Lyu,Zihui Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了角动量变化率惩罚项如何使质心OCP的最优解趋近于摆线力模式，分析了全秩支撑、N=2支撑（如对角小跑）和添加任务项三种情况下的最优行为，并在点质量四足机器人和Unitree Go1上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LIPM（线性倒立摆模型）在腿部运动控制中广泛应用，但通常仅作为建模选择，而非控制器成本函数实际偏好的结果。本文试图建立二者之间的显式联系。

Method: 从一个小型质心OCP（最优化控制问题）出发，惩罚角动量变化率，分析最优解的结构。分别考虑全秩支撑、N=2支撑（对角小跑）和添加非零角动量变化率任务项三种情况，并在点质量四足机器人和Unitree Go1 MuJoCo仿真中验证。

Result: 全秩支撑下，最优解趋向摆线力模式，速率由力矩雅可比矩阵的SVD决定，常数由足距几何确定，与实验误差在16%以内；N=2支撑下，摩擦锥引入角动量变化率的不可调下界，且存在一个闭式可写的临界水平加速度处的非光滑可行性拐点；添加任务项可预测地将最优解移出摆线集。

Conclusion: 这些结果与经典ZMP/DCM框架并不遥远。通过开环QP和扭矩级闭环控制器验证，同时指出了渐近分析在闭环控制中不再准确的情况。

Abstract: LIPM is everywhere in legged-locomotion control, but almost always as a modeling choice rather than as something the
  controller's cost actually prefers. This note tries to make that link more explicit. Working from a small centroidal
  OCP that penalizes the rate of angular momentum, we look at what its optimum tends to look like. Three things come
  out. With full-rank stance, the optimum drifts toward a pendular force pattern at a rate determined by the SVD of the
  moment Jacobian; the constant is set by foot-span geometry and matches the experiments to within 16%. With N=2 stance,
  as in trot, the friction cone introduces a lower bound on $\|\dot{H}_G\|$ that no amount of weight tuning fixes; we
  also see a non-smooth feasibility kink at a critical horizontal acceleration that we can write in closed form. Adding
  a task term that asks for a nonzero $\dot{H}_G$ moves the optimum off the pendular set in a predictable way. None of
  this is far from the classical ZMP/DCM picture. We test these claims on a point-mass quadruped and on the Unitree Go1
  in MuJoCo (open-loop QP and a torque-level closed-loop controller), and we note where the asymptotic story stops being
  a good description of what the closed loop actually does.

</details>


### [218] [MaMi-HOI: Harmonizing Global Kinematics and Local Geometry for Human-Object Interaction Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05756)
*Hao Wang,Shiqi Wang,Qi Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MaMi-HOI框架，通过几何感知接近适配器（GAPA）和运动和谐适配器（KHA）解决扩散模型在3D人-物交互生成中的"几何遗忘"问题，在保证自然运动的同时实现精确物体接触，支持长时程复杂轨迹任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在3D人-物交互生成中擅长语义对齐但难以维持精确物体接触，作者发现深层扩散模型中语义特征会掩盖物体几何特征，导致模型失去对物体几何的感知能力（即"几何遗忘"现象）。

Method: 提出MaMi-HOI层次化框架：(1) 几何感知接近适配器（GAPA）显式重新注入密集物体细节，进行残差修正实现精确接触；(2) 运动和谐适配器（KHA）主动对齐全身姿态与空间目标，确保骨架主动适应约束而不牺牲自然性。

Result: 实验证明MaMi-HOI在自然运动和精确接触两方面均达到最优，并能扩展到长时程复杂轨迹任务，弥合全局导航与高保真操作之间的差距。

Conclusion: 通过GAPA和KHA的双适配器设计，有效解决了扩散模型中的几何遗忘问题，实现了宏观运动流畅性与微观空间精度的统一。

Abstract: Generating realistic 3D Human-Object Interactions (HOI) is a fundamental task for applications ranging from embodied AI to virtual content creation, which requires harmonizing high-level semantic intent with strict low-level physical constraints. Existing methods excel at semantic alignment, however, they struggle to maintain precise object contact. We reveal a key finding termed \textit{Geometric Forgetting}: as diffusion model depth increases, semantic feature tend to overshadow object geometry feature, causing the model to lose its perception to object geometry. To address this, we propose MaMi-HOI, a hierarchical framework reconciling \textbf{Ma}cro-level kinematic fluidity with \textbf{Mi}cro-level spatial precision. First, to counteract geometric forgetting, we introduce the Geometry-Aware Proximity Adapter (GAPA), which explicitly re-injects dense object details to perform residual snapping corrections for precise contact. Nevertheless, such aggressive local enforcement can disrupt global dynamics, leading to robotic stiffness. In response, we introduce the Kinematic Harmony Adapter (KHA), which proactively aligns whole-body posture with spatial objectives, ensuring the skeleton actively accommodates constraints without compromising naturalness. Extensive experiments validate that MaMi-HOI simultaneously achieves natural motion and precise contact. Crucially, it extends generation capabilities to long-term tasks with complex trajectories, effectively bridging the gap between global navigation and high-fidelity manipulation in 3D scenes. Code is available at https://github.com/DON738110198/MaMi-HOI.git

</details>


### [219] [A Comparative Study of INDI and NDI with Nonlinear Disturbance Observer for Aerial Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05825)
*Benedetta Rota,Mirko Mizzoni,Amr Afifi,Pieter van Goor,Antonio Franchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过仿真对比了增量非线性动态逆（INDI）与非线性动态逆+非线性扰动观测器（NDI+NDO）在全驱动空中机器人中的鲁棒性，发现INDI在参数变化、外部扰动和测量噪声等模型失配和组合应力场景下具有更强的鲁棒性，而NDI+NDO在标称性能上相当但非理想条件下更敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为全驱动空中机器人选择鲁棒性更优的非线性控制策略提供系统性比较依据，明确增量式与基于观测器的动态逆方法各自的优势与局限。

Method: 搭建仿真环境，设计涵盖参数变化、外部扰动和测量噪声的代表性运行场景，对INDI和NDI+NDO进行系统性的跟踪性能、鲁棒性和控制力度的对比分析。

Result: INDI在多种模型失配和组合应力场景下表现出更强的鲁棒性；NDI+NDO在标称性能上与INDI相当，但在多种非理想条件下敏感性更高。

Conclusion: 该研究为空中机器人应用中增量式与基于观测器的动态逆策略的相对优势和局限性提供了实用指导。

Abstract: This work presents a simulation-based comparative robustness analysis of Incremental Nonlinear Dynamic Inversion (INDI) and Nonlinear Dynamic Inversion augmented with a nonlinear disturbance observer (NDI+NDO) for fully actuated aerial robots. A systematic simulation campaign across representative operating scenarios is conducted, where we compare tracking performance, robustness, control effort, under parametric variations, external disturbances, and measurement noise. Results show that INDI demonstrates stronger robustness in several model-mismatch and combined-stress cases, while NDI+NDO primarily matches nominal performance but exhibits greater sensitivity under several non-ideal conditions. These findings provide practical guidance on the relative strengths and limitations of incremental and observer-based inversion strategies for aerial robotic applications.

</details>


### [220] [Cycle-resolved Cephalopod-Inspired Pulsed-Jet Robot With High-Volume Expulsion and Drag-Reduced Gliding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05875)
*Yiyuan Zhang,Anye Zhong,Junkai Chen,Wenci Xin,Cecilia Laschi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文受头足类动物脉冲式喷流推进的循环机制启发，提出了一种采用刚柔混合折纸结构的机器人，通过大变形收缩（腔体体积减少75%、投影阻力面积减少75.7%）实现喷射-滑翔-再填充的完整循环，峰值速度达0.5 m/s（3.8 BL/s），平均速度超0.2 m/s（1.5 BL/s），并建立了循环解析框架以研究各阶段对整体性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 头足类动物的喷流推进并非孤立的喷射事件，而是由喷射、被动滑翔和 mantle 再填充组成的协调循环。现有机器人大多仅模拟单次喷射，忽略了循环中滑翔减阻和再填充机制对整体性能的贡献，缺乏统一的实验平台来研究完整的喷射-滑翔-再填充动力学。

Method: 设计了一种刚柔混合折纸mantle：刚性折叠板与柔性硅胶框架结合，实现主动驱动的大变形。通过折纸几何引导变形，在喷射阶段实现75%的有效腔体体积缩减，在收缩滑翔构型下将投影截面阻力面积减少约75.7%。采用循环解析框架分别研究排出体积、滑翔时长和再填充路径对整体循环性能的影响。

Result: 机器人首个喷射循环内峰值速度约0.5 m/s（3.8 BL/s），平均速度超0.2 m/s（1.5 BL/s）。实验验证了高排出体积比在速度生成中的作用、不同滑翔时长下的减阻效果，以及mantle孔启发式被动进气阀对再填充的辅助作用。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅提供了主动变形头足类喷流推进的机器人实现，还建立了一个统一的实验平台，用于研究脉冲式喷流推进中喷射-滑翔-再填充的动力学特性，为高效水下推进提供了新思路。

Abstract: Cephalopod pulsed-jet locomotion is not a single isolated expulsion event, but a coordinated cycle involving jet expulsion, passive gliding, and mantle refilling. Inspired by this cycle-resolved biological strategy, this paper presents a cephalopod-inspired pulsed-jet robot with a rigid-soft hybrid origami mantle that enables large, actively driven, and geometry-guided body deformation. The proposed mantle integrates rigid folding panels with a compliant silicone framework, allowing a 75% effective cavity-volume reduction during expulsion and reducing the projected cross-sectional drag area by approximately 75.7% in the contracted gliding configuration. Using this platform, we formulate a cycle-resolved framework to separately investigate how expelled volume, glide duration, and refill pathway influence whole-cycle locomotion performance. Experiments show that the robot reaches a peak speed of approximately 0.5 m/s (3.8 BL/s) and an average speed exceeding 0.2 m/s (1.5 BL/s) within the first jetting cycle. The results further demonstrate the roles of high expelled-volume-ratio contraction in speed generation, reduced-drag-area gliding under different glide durations, and mantle-aperture-inspired passive inlet valves in assisting refill. This work provides both a robotic implementation of actively deformable cephalopod-like jet propulsion and a unified experimental platform for studying expulsion-gliding-refilling dynamics in pulsed-jet locomotion.

</details>


### [221] [Generating Roadside LiDAR Datasets from Vehicle-Side Datasets via Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05897)
*Yuhan Xia,Runxin Zhao,Hanyang Zhuang,Chunxiang Wang,Ming Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VRS框架，通过将车载LiDAR数据合成为路边LiDAR数据集，解决路边感知模型训练中标注数据稀缺的问题，实验证明合成数据能有效补充真实数据并提升检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路边感知是扩展ITS感知范围和提升交通安全的关键手段，但大规模标注的路边LiDAR数据集极度稀缺，限制了高性能路边感知模型的训练。

Method: 提出VRS框架：1) 从车载数据集出发，通过LiDAR新视角合成生成标注的路边LiDAR数据；2) 采用车辆点云补全补偿车载观测中缺失的几何信息；3) 引入基于occupancy的可见性约束处理跨视角渲染中的大视点变化。

Result: 在路边3D目标检测任务上的实验表明，合成数据能有效补充真实路边数据，缓解真实数据有限的局限性，并提升模型对未见路边视角的泛化能力。

Conclusion: VRS是一种可扩展的路边数据生成方案，通过车载到路边的数据合成有效弥合领域差距，为ITS中的路边感知模型训练提供了低成本、高质量的替代数据源。

Abstract: Intelligent Transportation Systems (ITS) require reliable environmental perception to support safe and efficient transportation. With the rapid development of Vehicle-to-everything (V2X), roadside perception has become an effective means to extend sensing coverage and improve traffic safety. However, the scarcity of large-scale annotated roadside LiDAR datasets poses a major challenge for training high-performance roadside perception models.
