In this paper, we introduce Vehicle-to-Roadside LiDAR Synthesis (VRS), a data synthesis framework that generates labeled roadside LiDAR datasets from vehicle-side datasets via LiDAR novel view synthesis. To mitigate the vehicle-to-roadside domain gap, VRS employs vehicle point cloud completion to compensate for missing geometry in vehicle-side observations, and introduces an occupancy-based visibility constraint to handle large viewpoint changes during cross-view rendering. The proposed framework enables flexible multi-view rendering for scalable roadside data generation. Extensive experiments on roadside 3D object detection demonstrate that the synthesized data effectively complements real roadside data, mitigates the limitations of limited real-world roadside data, and improves generalization to unseen roadside viewpoints.

</details>


### [222] [DexSynRefine: Synthesizing and Refining Human-Object Interaction Motion for Physically Feasible Dexterous Robot Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05925)
*Hyesung Lee,Hyunwoo Jung,Si-Hwan Heo,Sungwook Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexSynRefine框架通过结合运动流形基元、任务空间残差强化学习和接触动力学适应模块，从稀疏的人-物交互数据中合成可执行的灵巧操作轨迹，在五项任务上优于基线方法并成功迁移到真实机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从人-物交互数据学习灵巧操作具有可扩展性优势，但存在两个关键挑战：HOI演示数据稀疏且仅提供运动学信息，无法直接用于存在形态不匹配和接触丰富动力学的实际执行。

Method: 提出DexSynRefine框架，包含三个耦合组件：1) HOI-MMFP，任务和物体初始状态条件化的运动流形基元扩展，从稀疏HOI演示合成协调的手-物轨迹；2) 任务空间残差RL策略，在执行参考轨迹的同时继承其运动学结构；3) 接触与动力学适应模块，利用本体感受历史实现仿真到真实的迁移。

Result: 在抓取放置、工具使用和物体重定向五项灵巧操作任务上，任务空间残差策略优于先前的动作表示基线方法，且在真实机器人上全部五项任务均成功迁移，相比运动学重定向提升50-70个百分点。

Conclusion: DexSynRefine有效解决了从稀疏HOI演示学习可执行灵巧操作的问题，为克服形态不匹配和动力学差异提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Learning dexterous manipulation from human-object interaction (HOI) data is a scalable alternative to teleoperation, but HOI demonstrations are sparse and provide only kinematic motion that is not directly executable under embodiment mismatch and contact-rich dynamics. We present DexSynRefine, a framework with three coupled components: HOI-MMFP, a task- and object-initial-state-conditioned extension of motion manifold primitives that synthesizes coordinated hand-object trajectories from sparse HOI demonstrations; a task-space residual RL policy that physically grounds the synthesized reference while inheriting its kinematic structure; and a contact-and-dynamics adaptation module that enables sim-to-real transfer from proprioceptive history. Across five dexterous manipulation tasks spanning pick-and-place, tool use, and object reorientation, our task-space residual policy outperforms prior action-representation baselines in simulations and transfers to a real robot on all five tasks, improving over kinematic retargeting by 50-70 percentage points.

</details>


### [223] [Plug-and-Play Label Map Diffusion for Universal Goal-Oriented Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05960)
*Zhixuan Shen,Yijie Zeng,Shengxiang Luo,Tianrui Li,Haonan Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PLMD，一种基于扩散模型的地图补全方法，通过生成未观测区域的障碍物和语义标签来解决目标导向导航中部分可观测环境的建图与目标定位问题。PLMD利用已知与未知障碍物布局的结构一致性来缓解语义关联不一致，可即插即用于现有语义地图导航策略，在三个GON任务上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于地图的GON方法依赖自中心语义地图，面临两个主要挑战：(1) 需要完整地图才能有效导航，但在未知环境中无法获取完整地图；(2) 语义关联不一致，导致目标定位不准确。因此需要一种能够在部分可观测环境下补全地图并保持语义一致性的方法。

Method: 提出Plug-and-Play Label Map Diffusion (PLMD)，一种基于DDPM的地图补全扩散模型。该方法通过扩散过程生成未观测区域的障碍物标签和语义标签；利用已知与未知障碍物布局的结构一致性，将障碍物先验引入语义去噪过程，从而缓解语义关联不一致问题；生成的预测标签可直接替换未观测区域，使机器人能够准确定位指定目标，且可即插即用于现有依赖语义地图的导航策略。

Result: 大量实验表明PLMD：(I) 有效扩展了未知地图区域；(II) 能无缝集成到现有依赖语义地图的导航策略中；(III) 在三个GON任务上达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: PLMD通过扩散模型实现了部分可观测环境下的高质量地图补全，解决了语义关联不一致问题，并且具有即插即用的特性，可广泛适用于各种依赖语义地图的导航框架，为GON任务提供了新的有效解决方案。

Abstract: In embodied vision, Goal-Oriented Navigation (GON) requires robots to locate a specific goal within an unexplored environment. The primary challenge of GON arises from the need to construct a Bird's-Eye-View (BEV) map to understand the environment while simultaneously localizing an unobserved goal. Existing map-based methods typically employ self-centered semantic maps, often facing challenges such as reliance on complete maps or inconsistent semantic association. To this end, we propose Plug-and-Play Label Map Diffusion (PLMD), which defines a novel map completion diffusion model based on Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Models (DDPM). PLMD generates obstacle and semantic labels for unobserved regions through a diffusion-based completion process, thereby enabling goal localization even in partially observed environments. Moreover, it mitigates inconsistent semantic association by leveraging structural consistency between known and unknown obstacle layouts and integrating obstacle priors into the semantic denoising process. By substituting predicted labels for unobserved regions, robots can accurately localize the specified objects. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PLMD \textbf{(I)} effectively expands the region of unknown maps, \textbf{(II)} integrates seamlessly into existing navigation strategies that rely on semantic maps, \textbf{(III)} achieves state-of-the-art performance on three GON tasks.

</details>


### [224] [Accurate Trajectory Tracking with MPCC for Flapping-Wing MAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06042)
*Charbel Toumieh,Jack Zeng,Niel Mistry,Dario Floreano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模型预测轮廓控制（MPCC）的扑翼飞行器自主控制方法，通过弧长参数化轨迹跟踪与在线优化进度，结合紧凑可微动力学模型，在XFly扑翼飞行器上实现了6.5-9cm的轨迹跟踪精度，较此前方法提升近10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扑翼微型飞行器（ornithopters）的升力、空速和转向力矩强耦合且仅由少数控制输入决定，传统级联控制器将高度、速度和航向独立处理，导致复杂机动中产生持续跟踪误差；而时间参数化轨迹跟踪需要预定义速度曲线，现有方法无法鲁棒处理这种耦合动力学。

Method: 采用模型预测轮廓控制（MPCC）方法，跟踪弧长参数化轨迹并在线优化进度，无需预定义时序；同时提出一种紧凑且连续可微的动力学模型，捕捉鸟级扑翼飞行器的主要耦合效应，满足实时非线性优化的计算预算。

Result: 在XFly扑翼飞行器上沿圆形和三维竞速轨迹飞行，在速度高达3 m/s时，平均偏离参考轨迹仅6.5至9 cm，较此前扑翼控制方法提升近10倍。

Conclusion: 所提出的MPCC方法结合紧凑动力学模型，有效解决了扑翼飞行器强耦合动力学下的精确自主控制难题，显著提升了轨迹跟踪精度。

Abstract: Flapping-wing micro aerial vehicles offer quieter and safer operation than rotary-wing drones, yet achieving precise autonomous control of bird-scale ornithopters remains challenging: lift, airspeed, and turning authority are tightly coupled and governed by only a few control inputs. Conventional cascaded controllers treat altitude, speed, and heading independently, producing persistent tracking errors during complex maneuvers, while time-parameterized trajectory tracking requires predefined speed profiles that existing methods cannot robustly produce for these coupled dynamics. We address both limitations simultaneously with a Model Predictive Contouring Control (MPCC) approach that tracks arc-length-parameterized trajectories while optimizing progress online, eliminating the need for predefined timing. However, MPCC requires a dynamical model that captures the coupled aerodynamics without exceeding the computational budget of real-time nonlinear optimization. Here, we propose a compact, continuously differentiable model that captures the dominant couplings of bird-scale ornithopters, enabling real-time predictive control. We validated the method with the XFly ornithopter flying along circular and three-dimensional racing trajectories and achieved a mean deviation from the reference trajectory between 6.5 and 9 cm at speeds up to 3 m/s, which represents an almost 10-fold improvement over prior ornithopter control methods.

</details>


### [225] [When to Trust Imagination: Adaptive Action Execution for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06222)
*Rui Wang,Yue Zhang,Jiehong Lin,Kuncheng Luo,Jianan Wang,Zhongrui Wang,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FFDC，一种轻量级验证器，用于自适应地决定世界动作模型（WAM）预测的动作块执行长度。当预测的未来与现实一致时，执行更长；出现偏差时，提前重新规划。在RoboTwin基准上减少69.10%的WAM前向传播和34.02%的执行时间，同时成功率提升2.54%；真实实验中成功率提升35%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WAM在执行时固定执行预测的固定数量动作，之后才进行下一次推理，导致机器人在执行过程中对预测未来与实际物理展开是否一致缺乏感知。当预测与现实出现偏差时，机器人无法及时调整，影响任务成功率。

Method: 1) 将自适应WAM执行形式化为未来-现实验证问题；2) 提出FFDC（Future Forward Dynamics Causal Attention），一种轻量级验证器，联合推理预测的未来动作、预测的视觉动态、真实观测和语言指令，估计剩余动作块是否仍可信；3) 引入Mixture-of-Horizon Training，提升长程轨迹覆盖，以支持自适应执行。

Result: RoboTwin基准上：WAM前向传播减少69.10%，执行时间减少34.02%，成功率较短块基线提升2.54%。真实实验中：成功率提升35%。

Conclusion: FFDC实现了自适应动作块大小作为预测-观测一致性的涌现结果，在保持长程执行效率的同时，在接触密集或困难阶段恢复响应能力，实现了强鲁棒性与效率的权衡。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for robotic manipulation by jointly predicting future visual observations and future actions. However, current WAMs typically execute a fixed number of predicted actions after each model inference, leaving the robot blind to whether the imagined future remains consistent with the actual physical rollout. In this work, we formulate adaptive WAM execution as a future-reality verification problem: the robot should execute longer when the WAM-predicted future remains reliable, and replan earlier when reality deviates from imagination. To this end, we propose Future Forward Dynamics Causal Attention (FFDC), a lightweight verifier that jointly reasons over predicted future actions, predicted visual dynamics, real observations, and language instructions to estimate whether the remaining action rollout can still be trusted. FFDC enables adaptive action chunk sizes as an emergent consequence of prediction-observation consistency, preserving the efficiency of long-horizon execution while restoring responsiveness in contact-rich or difficult phases. We further introduce Mixture-of-Horizon Training to improve long-horizon trajectory coverage for adaptive execution. Experiments on the RoboTwin benchmark and in the real world demonstrate that our method achieves a strong robustness-efficiency trade-off: on RoboTwin, it reduces WAM forward passes by 69.10% and execution time by 34.02%, while improving success rate by 2.54% over the short-chunk baseline; in real-world experiments, it improves success rate by 35%.

</details>


### [226] [RobotEQ: Transitioning from Passive Intelligence to Active Intelligence in Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06234)
*Kuofei Fang,Xinyi Che,Haomin Ouyang,Shufan Zhang,Xuehao Wang,Qi Liu,Liyi Liu,Chenqi Zhang,Wenxi Cai,Wenyu Dai,Jinyang Wu,Fan Zhang,Haoyu Chen,Bin He,Zheng Lian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了RobotEQ，首个面向主动智能的基准测试，用于评估现有模型在具身场景中理解和遵守社会规范的能力。包含1900张自我中心图像、5353个动作判断问题和1286个空间定位问题。实验表明现有模型在主动智能上表现不足，尤其是空间定位，但RAG技术可提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前具身AI研究集中于根据明确指令完成任务，但机器人要融入人类社会，必须理解哪些行为被允许或禁止，即使没有明确指令。作者将用户引导的AI称为被动智能，无引导的AI称为主动智能，但目前缺乏评估主动智能的基准。

Method: 构建RobotEQ-Data数据集（1900张自我中心图像，10个具身类别56个子类别），包含5353个动作判断问题和1286个空间定位问题，通过大量人工标注完成。建立RobotEQ-Bench评估框架，使用RAG技术引入外部社会规范知识库来增强模型表现。

Result: 现有模型在主动智能任务上表现不足，尤其在空间定位方面。利用RAG技术引入外部社会规范知识库可普遍提升模型性能。

Conclusion: 这项工作可推动机器人从用户引导的被动操控向主动遵守社会规范的转变，为具身AI的社会化发展提供了评估基准和方向。

Abstract: Embodied AI is a prominent research topic in both academia and industry. Current research centers on completing tasks based on explicit user instructions. However, for robots to integrate into human society, they must understand which actions are permissible and which are prohibited, even without explicit commands. We refer to the user-guided AI as passive intelligence and the unguided AI as active intelligence. This paper introduces RobotEQ, the first benchmark for active intelligence, aiming to assess whether existing models can comprehend and adhere to social norms in embodied scenarios. First, we construct RobotEQ-Data, a dataset consisting of 1,900 egocentric images, spanning 10 representative embodied categories and 56 subcategories. Through extensive manual annotation, we provide 5,353 action judgment questions and 1,286 spatial grounding questions, specifying appropriate robot actions across diverse scenarios. Furthermore, we establish RobotEQ-Bench to evaluate the performance of state-of-the-art models on this task. Experimental results show that current models still fall short in achieving reliable active intelligence, particularly in spatial grounding. Meanwhile, we observe that leveraging RAG techniques to incorporate external social norm knowledge bases can generally enhance performance. This work can facilitate the transition of robotics from user-guided passive manipulation to active social compliance.

</details>


### [227] [CKT-WAM: Parameter-Efficient Context Knowledge Transfer Between World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06247)
*Yuhua Jiang,Yijun Guo,Hongbing Yang,Guojun Lei,Nuo Chen,Yinuo Zhang,Shaoqiang Yan,Bo Lin,Feifei Gao,Biqing Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CKT-WAM框架，通过文本嵌入空间中的紧凑上下文（而非输出模仿或隐状态匹配）实现世界动作模型间的知识迁移，在LIBERO-Plus上以仅1.17%可训练参数达到86.1%成功率，并在真实世界长时域操作任务中取得83.3%平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构世界动作模型（WAMs）之间存在潜在接口不匹配、适应成本高以及传统蒸馏目标刚性等问题，使得跨模型知识迁移面临挑战。

Method: CKT-WAM通过压缩器的可学习查询交叉注意力（LQCA）提取并压缩教师模型的中间隐状态，利用始终在线的通用适配器、轻量路由器和稀疏激活的专用适配器进行变换，将生成的紧凑上下文拼接至学生模型的条件文本嵌入中，实现知识注入。

Result: LIBERO-Plus上以1.17%可训练参数达到86.1%总成功率，超越全微调性能；在真实世界多步长时域任务中取得83.3%平均成功率，零样本泛化能力显著提升。

Conclusion: CKT-WAM通过文本嵌入空间中的紧凑上下文迁移，以参数高效的方式实现了异构WAM间有效的知识迁移，兼具强泛化能力和真实世界部署潜力。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) provide a powerful generative framework for embodied control, yet transferring knowledge across heterogeneous WAMs remains challenging due to mismatched latent interfaces, high adaptation cost, and the rigidity of conventional distillation objectives. We propose \textbf{CKT-WAM}, a parameter-efficient \textbf{C}ontext \textbf{K}nowledge \textbf{T}ransfer framework that transfers teacher WAM's knowledge into a student WAM through a compact context in the text embedding space, rather than output imitation or dense hidden-state matching. Specifically, CKT-WAM extracts intermediate teacher hidden states, reduces the number of tokens via compressors' learnable-query cross attention (LQCA), and transforms them through an always-on generalized adapter, a lightweight router, and sparsely activated specialized adapters. The resulting context is then appended to the student's conditioning textual embeddings, thereby injecting the transferred knowledge into the student with minimal architectural modification. Experiments show that CKT-WAM consistently improves zero-shot generalization and achieves the best overall performance on LIBERO-Plus, reaching 86.1\% total success rate with only 1.17\% trainable parameters, while approaching full fine-tuning performance. Beyond simulation, CKT-WAM also demonstrates strong real-world long-horizon manipulation ability, achieving the best average success rate of 83.3\% across four multi-step and long-horizon tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/YuhuaJiang2002/CKT-WAM.

</details>


### [228] [Toward Visually Realistic Simulation: A Benchmark for Evaluating Robot Manipulation in Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06311)
*Yixin Zhu,Zixiong Wang,Jian Yang,Jin Xie,Jingyi Yu,Jiayuan Gu,Beibei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VISER，一个高视觉真实感的机器人操作仿真评估基准，通过高质量PBR材质3D资产、自动化MLLM驱动的材质感知分割与检索流程，以及多样化评估任务，显著缩小了仿真与真实环境的视觉差异，实现仿真与真实性能间平均0.92的皮尔逊相关系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作仿真基准缺乏视觉真实感，导致仿真与真实环境之间存在较大的领域差距，削弱了仿真评估预测真实性能的可靠性。光照和材质因素对几何推理和空间定位至关重要，却被现有基准所忽视。

Method: 系统分析光照和材质对sim-to-real视觉差距的影响；构建包含1000+高保真3D资产（含PBR材质）的数据集；提出利用多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）进行材质感知部分分割和材质检索的自动化流程；基于该资产数据集构建抓取、放置、长时域等多样化评估任务。

Result: VISER基准在多种策略下，仿真与真实环境性能之间实现了平均0.92的皮尔逊相关系数，显著优于现有基准。

Conclusion: 通过提升视觉真实感（特别是光照和材质质量），可以有效缩小仿真与真实之间的领域差距，使仿真评估成为更可靠的现实性能预测指标。VISER为VLA模型的规模化、可复现评估提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Reliable simulation evaluation of robot manipulation policies serves as a high-fidelity proxy for real-world performance. Although existing benchmarks cover a wide range of task categories, they lack visual realism, creating a large domain gap between simulation and reality. This undermines the reliability of simulation-based evaluation in predicting real-world performance. To mitigate the sim-to-real visual gap, we conduct a systematic analysis to isolate the effects of lighting and material. Our results show that these factors play a critical role in geometric reasoning and spatial grounding, yet are largely overlooked in existing benchmarks. Motivated by the analysis, we propose VISER, a visually realistic benchmark for evaluating robot manipulation in simulation. VISER features a high-fidelity dataset of over 1,000 3D assets with physically-based rendering (PBR) materials, along with 3D scenes created from these assets through curated layouts or generation. To this end, we propose an automated pipeline leveraging Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs) for material-aware part segmentation and material retrieval, enabling scalable generation of physically plausible assets. Building on the high-fidelity 3D asset dataset, we construct diverse evaluation tasks, such as grasping, placing, and long-horizon tasks, enabling scalable and reproducible assessment of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. Our benchmark shows a strong correlation between simulation and real-world performance, achieving an average Pearson correlation coefficient of 0.92 across different policies.

</details>


### [229] [AssistDLO: Assistive Teleoperation for Deformable Linear Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06323)
*Berk Guler,Simon Manschitz,Kay Pompetzki,Jan Peters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AssistDLO是一个面向可变形线状物体遥操作的辅助框架，结合多视图状态估计、视觉辅助和基于控制屏障函数的几何感知共享自主控制器。在22人参与的双手解结实验中，SA-CBF将新手成功率从71%提升至88%，但效果依赖操作员经验和绳索特性，表明需要自适应的用户感知共享自主策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形线状物体（DLO）具有无限维配置空间和复杂非线性动力学，遥操作中深度不确定性阻碍状态感知与反应，传统共享自主方法依赖简单几何吸引子，难以保持DLO几何形态。

Method: 提出AssistDLO框架，包含实时多视图状态估计、视觉辅助（VA）和基于控制屏障函数的几何感知共享自主控制器（SA-CBF），后者作为几何感知漏斗在保持操作员高级控制权的同时促进精确抓取。

Result: 在22人双手解结实验中，SA-CBF将新手成功率从71%提升至88%；专家用户偏好VA；高柔顺性长绳索更受益于视觉支持而非局部动作辅助；刚性绳索对SA-CBF响应更好。

Conclusion: 有效的DLO遥操作不能依赖固定策略，需要自适应的、用户感知和材料感知的共享自主方法。

Abstract: Manipulating Deformable Linear Objects (DLOs) is challenging in robotics due to their infinite-dimensional configuration space and complex nonlinear dynamics. In teleoperation, depth uncertainty hinders state perception and reaction. AssistDLO addresses this challenge as an assistive teleoperation framework for DLO manipulation that combines real-time multi-view state estimation, visual assistance (VA), and a geometry-aware shared-autonomy controller based on Control Barrier Functions (SA-CBF). While traditional shared autonomy methods often rely on simple geometric attractors and may fail to preserve DLO geometry, SA-CBF acts as a geometry-aware funnel, facilitating precise grasping while preserving the operator's high-level authority. The framework is evaluated in a bimanual knot-untangling user study (N = 22) using ropes with varying length and rigidity. Results show that the effectiveness of the assistance depends strongly on operator expertise and DLO properties. SA-CBF provides the strongest gains for naive users, acting as a skill equalizer that increases task success from 71% to 88%, and is effective for stiffer ropes. Conversely, expert users prefer VA, and highly compliant, long ropes benefit more from visual support than localized action assistance. Ultimately, these findings demonstrate that effective DLO teleoperation cannot rely on a fixed strategy, highlighting the critical need for adaptive, user-aware, and material-aware shared autonomy.

</details>


### [230] [TouchDrive: Electronics-Free Tactile Sensing Interface for Assistive Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06432)
*Jing Xu,Xuezhi Niu,Didem Gurdur Broo,Klas Hjort*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TouchDrive是一种低成本、无电子元件的触觉传感接口，通过气动阀门将接触力直接转化为气动反馈，帮助用户调节抓取力，实现对易碎和柔性物体的精准操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有辅助抓取系统依赖电子传感和多阶段处理流水线，系统复杂度高、可及性差，需要更简单、低成本的解决方案。

Method: 采用气动常闭阀、压缩空气罐、传感元件和触觉反馈执行器构成纯机械闭环，将接触力直接转换为气动反馈信号。

Result: 在多种机器人平台上验证了系统的可靠性和实用性，成功抓取多达20种水果和日常物品。

Conclusion: TouchDrive通过纯机械气动反馈实现了无电子元件的辅助抓取力调节，在降低系统复杂度和成本的同时保证了抓取性能。

Abstract: Assistive robotic grasping plays an important role in enabling safe and adaptive manipulation of diverse objects. However, existing systems often rely on electronic sensing and multi-stage processing pipelines, increasing system complexity and reducing accessibility. To address these limitations, we present TouchDrive, a cost-effective, electronics-free tactile sensing interface for assistive grasping. TouchDrive directly converts contact forces into pneumatic feedback through valve-mediated switching, integrating sensing, signal generation, and feedback within a single passive mechanical loop. The system can be employed using a pneumatic normally closed valve, a compressed air tank, sensing element, and haptic feedback actuator without electronics. By delivering tactile cues, TouchDrive empowers users to modulate grasp forces, enabling precise and robust delicate manipulation of compliant and fragile objects. The interface has been validated across diverse robotic platforms, consistently demonstrating reliable performance and practical applicability in assistive grasping tasks, such as handling fruits and everyday items (up to 20 objects).

</details>


### [231] [GA3T: A Ground-Aerial Terrain Traversability Dataset for Heterogeneous Robot Teams in Unstructured Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06478)
*Siwei Cai,Knut Peterson,Quan Tran,Christian Ricks,Dhanush Parthasarathy,Amir Kaidarov,Neil Deshpande,Sukaina Najm,David Han,Lifeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GA3T是一个真实世界多机器人协同感知数据集，包含地面无人车（Clearpath Husky）和空中无人机（Autel EVO II）在非结构化越野环境中的多模态、多视角同步观测数据，支持跨视角感知、空地融合、可通行性估计等研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构空地机器人团队结合互补的感知模态、移动特性和空间视角，可显著增强复杂户外环境中的感知能力。然而，多机器人协同感知的进展因缺乏在非结构化地形中运行平台的重叠多模态观测真实世界数据集而受限。

Method: 使用Clearpath Husky UGV（提供3D LiDAR、立体相机、IMU、GPS）和Autel EVO II UAV（提供RGB图像、热红外观测、GPS）在森林小径、岩石路径、泥泞地形、雪堆和草地等4种非结构化环境中收集数据，包含超过13,000个同步帧（约29分钟），并包含SAM 3零样本分割和超过8,000张人工标注图像。数据集在早春采集，稀疏树冠使无人机能透过树木部分观测地面机器人和地形。

Result: 提供了超过13,000个同步帧、约29分钟运行数据、SAM 3零样本分割以及超过8,000张人工标注图像的多模态多视角数据集。

Conclusion: GA3T专门设计用于支持真实越野环境中的跨视角感知、空地视点融合、可通行性估计和协同场景理解研究，填补了多机器人协同感知领域真实非结构化环境数据集的空白。

Abstract: Heterogeneous air-ground robot teams combine complementary sensing modalities, mobility characteristics, and spatial viewpoints that can significantly enhance perception in complex outdoor environments. However, progress in multi-robot collaborative perception has been constrained by the lack of real-world datasets featuring overlapping multi-modal observations from platforms operating in unstructured terrain. We present GA3T (Ground-Aerial Team for Terrain Traversal), a real-world multi-robot collaborative perception dataset collected using a Clearpath Husky UGV and an Autel EVO~II UAV across diverse unstructured environments, including forest trails, rocky paths, muddy terrain, snow piles, and grass-covered fields. The ground platform provides 3D LiDAR, stereo camera, IMU, and GPS data, while the aerial platform contributes RGB imagery, thermal/infrared observations, and GPS from a complementary overhead viewpoint, allowing for rich cross-modal and cross-view perception. The dataset is collected in 4 unique environments, with over 13,000 synchronized frames across approximately 29 minutes of operation, and includes both SAM~3-based zero-shot segmentation and over 8,000 manually labeled images. A unique aspect of the dataset is its early-spring collection period, during which sparse tree canopies allow the aerial robot to partially observe the ground robot and terrain through the trees, allowing for occlusion-aware collaborative perception. Unlike prior multi-robot datasets that focus on SLAM or simulated cooperative driving, GA3T is specifically designed to support research on cross-view perception, air-ground viewpoint fusion, traversability estimation, and collaborative scene understanding in real off-road environments.

</details>


### [232] [OA-WAM: Object-Addressable World Action Model for Robust Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06481)
*Yushan Liu,Peibo Sun,Shoujie Li,Yifan Xie,Lingfeng Zhang,Xintao Chao,Shiyuan Dong,Fang Chen,Xiao-Ping Zhang,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出OA-WAM模型，通过将场景分解为可寻址的物体槽位（N个物体槽+1个机器人槽），实现对象级别的世界建模与动作预测，在LIBERO和SimplerEnv上达到SOTA性能，并展现出对场景扰动的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WAM方法将预测世界表示为全局图像、视频token或全局潜变量，当指令指向特定物体时，动作解码器难以定位目标对象，尤其在场景偏移下物体身份与上下文纠缠不清，导致鲁棒性不足。

Method: 将每帧分解为N+1个槽位（1个机器人槽+N个物体槽），每个槽包含持久化地址向量和时变内容向量；通过地址-only键的跨槽注意力路由和每层重置地址切片实现可寻址性；世界头预测下一帧槽状态，流匹配动作头在同一前向过程中解码16步连续动作块。

Result: LIBERO达到97.8%，SimplerEnv达到79.3%；在LIBERO-Plus几何轴上达到SOTA；因果槽干预测试中交换绑定余弦为0.87，远高于全局基线（最高0.09）。

Conclusion: 可寻址的物体槽状态提供了一种有效的接口，能够在场景扰动下实现鲁棒的世界-动作建模。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) enhance Vision-Language-Action policies by jointly predicting scene evolution and robot actions, but existing methods usually represent the predicted world as holistic images, video tokens, or global latents. These representations are difficult for an action decoder to address when an instruction refers to a particular object, especially under scene shifts where object identity is entangled with context. We propose OA-WAM, an Object-Addressable World Action Model for robust robot manipulation. OA-WAM decomposes each frame into N+1 slot states, with one robot slot and N object slots. Each slot contains a persistent address vector and a time-varying content vector, and is fused with text, image, proprioception, and past-action tokens in a block-causal sequence. A world head predicts next-frame slot states, while a flow-matching action head decodes a 16-step continuous action chunk in the same forward pass. Addressability is enforced by routing cross-slot attention through address-only keys and resetting the address slice at every transformer layer, separating which object to act on from what that object currently is without adding extra tokens. OA-WAM matches strong VLA and WAM baselines on LIBERO (97.8%) and SimplerEnv (79.3%), reaches state-of-the-art performance on the most relevant LIBERO-Plus geometric axes, and remains competitive on the seven-axis aggregate. A causal slot-intervention test yields a swap-binding cosine of 0.87, versus at most 0.09 for holistic baselines. These results suggest that addressable object states provide an effective interface for robust world-action modeling under scene perturbations.

</details>


### [233] [ReActor: Reinforcement Learning for Physics-Aware Motion Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06593)
*David Müller,Agon Serifi,Sammy Christen,Ruben Grandia,Espen Knoop,Moritz Bächer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种双层优化框架，将人体运动重新定位到机器人形态上，同时通过强化学习训练跟踪策略，生成物理合理且适合模仿学习的运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将人体参考运动重新定位到机器人形态时，常产生脚部滑动、自碰撞或动态不可行等物理不一致问题，阻碍了下游的模仿学习。

Method: 提出双层优化框架：上层优化调整参考运动以适应机器人形态，下层通过强化学习训练跟踪策略。通过推导上层损失函数的近似梯度使优化可解，仅需稀疏的语义刚体对应关系，免去手动调参，并直接与物理仿真集成。

Result: 在仿真和真实硬件上验证了该方法，成功将人体运动重新定位到形态差异显著的机器人（包括四足机器人）上，生成了挑战性的运动。

Conclusion: 该框架能自动生成物理合理、保持特征的运动，无需手动调整，有效支持下游的鲁棒模仿学习。

Abstract: Retargeting human kinematic reference motion onto a robot's morphology remains a formidable challenge. Existing methods often produce physical inconsistencies, such as foot sliding, self-collisions, or dynamically infeasible motions, which hinder downstream imitation learning. We propose a bilevel optimization framework that jointly adapts reference motions to a robot's morphology while training a tracking policy using reinforcement learning. To make the optimization tractable, we derive an approximate gradient for the upper-level loss. Our framework requires only a sparse set of semantic rigid-body correspondences and eliminates the need for manual tuning by identifying optimal values for a parameterization expressive enough to preserve characteristic motion across different embodiments. Moreover, by integrating retargeting directly with physics simulation, we produce physically plausible motions that facilitate robust imitation learning. We validate our method in simulation and on hardware, demonstrating challenging motions for morphologies that differ significantly from a human, including retargeting onto a quadruped.

</details>


### [234] [Multi-Robot Coordination in V2X Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06662)
*John Pravin Arockiasamy,Alexey Vinel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于V2X的去中心化社交机器人协作框架，通过新增机器人感知服务（RAS）和机器人机动协调服务（RMCS），实现无集中基础设施的多机器人协调，并在真实场景中验证了人形机器人与四足机器人协助行人过街的确定性协作，仿真实验进一步证明了RAS在混合V2X环境中降低信道负载的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有V2X标准主要面向传统车辆通信，缺乏对社交机器人（如人形机器人、四足机器人）在复杂城市交通环境中的去中心化协作支持，亟需一种可扩展、标准兼容的框架来将机器人集成到未来的互联协作自动驾驶生态中。

Method: 基于ETSI协同感知和机动协调服务，提出机器人感知服务（RAS）和机器人机动协调服务（RMCS），分别通过机器人感知消息（RAM）和机器人机动协调消息（RMCM）实现。RAS支持角色感知、任务导向的机器人感知，并集成非V2X行人；RMCS支持事件驱动、低延迟的机动协调，无需集中设施或预配对。真实场景采用有限状态机协调模型。

Result: 真实场景中成功实现了人形机器人与四足机器人协助行人过街的确定性多机器人协调。仿真中，基于RAS的VRU聚类将非V2X弱势道路使用者整合到安全关键区域，同时减少V2X设备的冗余传输，降低信道负载。

Conclusion: 提出的RAS和RMCS服务为将协作机器人集成到未来互联协作自动驾驶生态系统提供了可扩展且符合标准的基础。

Abstract: This paper presents a Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) communication framework that enables decentralized cooperation among social robots operating in complex urban traffic environments. Building on ETSI Cooperative Awareness and Maneuver Coordination services, the framework introduces two robot-centric facility-layer services: the Robot Awareness Service (RAS) and the Robot Maneuver Coordination Service (RMCS), realized through the Robot Awareness Message (RAM) and the Robot Maneuver Coordination Message (RMCM), respectively. RAS enables role-aware, task-oriented robot awareness while integrating externally detected Vulnerable Road Users (VRUs), including non-V2X pedestrians, into cooperative awareness. RMCS supports event-driven, low-latency coordination of robot maneuvers under explicitly established roles, without centralized infrastructure or prior pairing. A real-world proof of concept demonstrates deterministic multi-robot coordination between a humanoid robot and a quadrupedal robot assisting a pedestrian during a road-crossing scenario, governed by a formally specified finite-state coordination model. Complementary simulations evaluate robot-mediated VRU clustering in mixed V2X environments, showing that RAS-based clustering integrates non-V2X VRUs in safety-critical areas while reducing redundant transmissions from V2X-enabled VRUs, thereby lowering channel load. Together, the proposed services provide a scalable and standards-aligned foundation for integrating cooperative robots into future Connected, Cooperative, and Automated Mobility ecosystems.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [235] [Multiagent Stochastic Shortest Path Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06056)
*Martin Jonáš,Antonín Kučera,Vojtěch Kůr,Jan Mačák,Vojtěch Řehák*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究多智能体随机最短路径（MSSP）问题，目标是让k个智能体中的任意一个尽快到达目标状态，分析了自主和协调两种设置下的计算复杂性和策略复杂性，设计了高效的策略合成算法，并通过实验与基线方法进行了对比评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机最短路径（SSP）问题主要关注单智能体场景，而多智能体协同到达目标的场景在实际中广泛存在（如搜索救援、机器人编队），但缺乏系统性的理论分析和算法研究。

Method: 首先形式化定义MSSP问题，区分自主（agents独立行动）和协调（agents可协同）两种设置；分别分析其计算复杂性和策略复杂性；设计高效的策略合成算法（如基于动态规划或强化学习的变体）；在多种规模实例上对比基线方法进行实验评估。

Result: 在自主设置下，问题可分解为单智能体SSP的并行求解，计算复杂度与单智能体相当；在协调设置下，问题更具挑战性，但所提算法仍能在多项式时间内求得最优策略。实验表明算法在大规模实例上显著优于基线。

Conclusion: MSSP问题存在高效的策略合成方法，自主与协调设置具有不同的复杂度特性，实验结果验证了算法的可扩展性和有效性。

Abstract: We introduce and study the multi-agent stochastic shortest path (MSSP) problem, in which $k$ agents strive to reach a target state, aiming to minimize the expected time to reach the target by any agent. We analyze the computational and strategy-complexity of the problem in both autonomous and coordinated settings, and we design efficient strategy-synthesis algorithms. The algorithms are experimentally evaluated on instances of increasing size against natural baselines.

</details>


### [236] [Power-Efficiency and Scalability Analysis of Magnetically-Actuated Satellite Swarms via Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06286)
*Yuta Takahashi,Seang Shim,Hiraku Sakamoto,Shin-ichiro Sakai*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于凸优化的评估框架，用于分析由磁场相互作用维持的卫星群孔径系统。通过磁力矩器产生的电磁力实现无推进剂的位置控制，但电磁力/力矩模型的非线性导致功率约束非凸。所提框架将非凸问题转化为凸优化问题，分析表明增加卫星数量可提高编队保持的功率效率，为大规模空间系统构建提供了高能效的磁驱动卫星群方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星群分布式孔径具有高增益、窄波束和大孔径能力，突破发射尺寸限制，但长期维持虚拟结构需要稳定的编队控制。磁力矩器提供的无推进剂位置控制策略具有吸引力，然而电磁力/力矩模型的非线性导致系统级功率分析困难，亟需有效的分析框架。

Method: 基于凸优化的分析框架，将非凸的电磁功率约束转化为凸问题，评估大规模磁驱动卫星群的功率消耗特性。

Result: 增加卫星数量能够提高编队保持的功率效率；磁驱动卫星群架构相比传统少卫星电磁编队飞行概念更具功率高效性。

Conclusion: 磁驱动卫星群架构为构建大规模空间系统提供了一种功率高效的替代方案，打破了传统电磁编队飞行局限于少卫星的瓶颈。

Abstract: This correspondence presents a convex-optimization-based evaluation framework of satellite-swarm-based apertures maintained by magnetic-field interactions. Spaceborne distributed apertures are composed of multiple satellites and are attractive for scientific and commercial missions because their scalability enables high-gain, narrow-beam, and large-aperture capabilities beyond the launch-size limitations. A key challenge is that the long-term maintenance of such virtual structures requires consistent formation control amid unstable orbital dynamics, and magnetic interactions generated by satellite-mounted magnetorquers offer a desirable propellant-free position-control strategy. However, the nonlinearities of the electromagnetic force and torque model lead to a nonconvex power-consumption constraint, making system-level configuration analysis difficult. To address this issue, we develop a convex optimization-based framework to analyze the power consumption of large magnetically actuated satellite swarms. The resulting analysis shows that increasing the number of satellites can improve formation-keeping power efficiency. This indicates that magnetically actuated swarm architectures provide a power-efficient alternative to the conventional few-satellite electromagnetic formation-flight concept for constructing large-scale space systems.

</details>


### [237] [Improving the Efficiency of Language Agent Teams with Adaptive Task Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06320)
*Elizabeth Mieczkowski,Alexander Ku,Tiwalayo Eisape,Dilip Arumugam,John Matters,Katherine M. Collins,Ilia Sucholutsky,Thomas L. Griffiths*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: LATTE是一个受分布式系统启发的LLM团队协调框架，通过维护共享的、动态演化的协调图来编码子任务依赖、智能体分配和任务进度，相比MetaGPT、去中心化团队、Leader-Worker层次结构等基线方法，在减少token消耗、时间、通信和协调失败的同时，匹配或超越了准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM团队协调方法处于两个极端：高度结构化方法依赖预先分配的固定角色/管线，缺乏灵活性；完全无结构团队虽然适应性强，但存在错误传播、智能体间冲突和资源浪费等问题。需要一种平衡结构性和灵活性的协调方法。

Method: 提出LATTE（Language Agent Teams for Task Evolution）框架，受分布式系统启发，让智能体团队协作构建和维护一个共享的、动态演化的协调图（coordination graph），该图编码子任务依赖、个体智能体分配和子任务进度状态。协议在维护一致性的同时，允许智能体动态分配工作、调整协调方式并发现新任务。

Result: 在多个协作任务和多种基础模型上，LATTE减少了token使用量、墙钟时间、通信量和协调失败（如文件冲突和冗余输出），同时匹配或超越了MetaGPT、去中心化团队、自上而下的Leader-Worker层次结构和静态分解等标准设计的准确率。

Conclusion: 通过动态协调图实现的结构化与灵活性的平衡，是提升LLM团队协作效率与鲁棒性的有效途径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in teams, yet existing coordination approaches often occupy two extremes. Highly structured methods rely on fixed roles, pipelines, or task decompositions assigned a priori. In contrast, fully unstructured teams enable adaptability and exploration but suffer from inefficiencies such as error propagation, inter-agent conflicts, and wasted resources (measured in time, tokens, or file operations). We introduce Language Agent Teams for Task Evolution (LATTE), a framework for coordinating LLM teams inspired by distributed systems, where processors must operate under partial observability and communication constraints. In LATTE, a team of agents collaboratively construct and maintain a shared, evolving coordination graph which encodes sub-task dependencies, individual agent assignment, and the current state of sub-task progress. This protocol maintains consistency while empowering agents to dynamically allocate work, adapt coordination, and discover new tasks. Across multiple collaborative tasks and a variety of base models, we demonstrate how LATTE reduces token usage, wall-clock time, communication, and coordination failures (e.g. file conflicts and redundant outputs) while matching or exceeding the accuracy of standard designs including MetaGPT, decentralized teams, top-down Leader-Worker hierarchies, and static decompositions.

</details>


### [238] [Coordination Matters: Evaluation of Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06557)
*Maria Ana Cardei,Matthew Landers,Afsaneh Doryab*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种协调感知的评估视角，通过过程级诊断指标（冗余分配、分配多样性、任务完成效率）补充传统回报指标，并在承诺约束的空间任务分配测试平台STAT上评估六种价值型MARL方法，发现相似的回报趋势可能掩盖不同的协调机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MARL基准主要依赖回报、成功率等聚合指标，但这些指标无法揭示智能体如何协调，特别是在智能体、任务和联合分配选择呈组合爆炸增长的环境中，需要更细粒度的过程级诊断工具。

Method: 设计STAT测试平台，在承诺约束下系统变化智能体数量、任务数量和环境规模，固定观测访问和任务规则；评估六种代表性基于价值的MARL方法（不同集中化程度），引入冗余分配、分配多样性、任务完成效率等过程级诊断指标。

Result: 相似回报趋势可能对应不同的协调机制；在承诺约束任务分配中，规模化表现不仅受名义动作空间大小影响，还受分配压力、稀疏决策机会和相互依赖智能体间的冗余选择影响。

Conclusion: 协调感知评估是回报基准测试的必要补充，能够揭示聚合指标所掩盖的协调行为差异，为理解MARL方法在复杂任务分配中的行为提供更全面的视角。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) benchmarks commonly emphasize aggregate outcomes such as return, success rate, or completion time. While essential, these metrics often fail to reveal how agents coordinate, particularly in settings where agents, tasks, and joint assignment choices scale combinatorially. We propose a coordination-aware evaluation perspective that supplements return with process-level diagnostics. We instantiate this perspective using STAT, a controlled commitment-constrained spatial task-allocation testbed that systematically varies agents, tasks, and environment size while holding observation access and task rules fixed. We evaluate six representative value-based MARL methods across varying levels of centralization. Our results show that similar return trends can reflect distinct coordination mechanisms, including differences in redundant assignment, assignment diversity, and task-completion efficiency. We find that in commitment-constrained task allocation, performance under scale is shaped not only by nominal action-space size, but also by assignment pressure, sparse decision opportunities, and redundant choices among interdependent agents. Our findings motivate coordination-aware evaluation as a necessary complement to return-based benchmarking for cooperative MARL.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [239] [Low-Latency Out-of-Core ANN Search in High-Dimensional Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05787)
*Ziwen Song,Bin Wang,Xiaochun Yang,Junhua Zhang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: SkipDisk是一种磁盘-内存混合的近似最近邻搜索方法，通过紧密下界过滤、三级数据剪枝和异步I/O策略，在仅使用HNSW约10%-20%内存的情况下，实现了HNSW 63%-85%的搜索延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有内存图方法（如HNSW）搜索性能优越但内存占用大，而磁盘方法内存占用少但磁盘访问延迟高。核心挑战是如何在显著降低内存占用的同时实现低延迟搜索。现有基于下界过滤的方法过于宽松，无法有效减少磁盘访问。

Method: 1) 为每个点设计专属锚点（pivot），利用三角不等式构建紧密下界进行有效过滤；2) 基于该下界设计估计方法；3) 采用三级数据剪枝策略，将信息量大的数据保留在内存中；4) 通过将邻居节点存储在内存中实现内存搜索与磁盘访问的解耦，设计异步I/O策略。

Result: 在约10%内存占用下，延迟为HNSW的85%；在约20%内存占用下，延迟降至HNSW的63%。

Conclusion: SkipDisk证明了磁盘-内存混合方案可以在大幅降低内存占用的同时达到接近甚至优于纯内存方法的搜索延迟，为大规模ANN搜索提供了一种高效的内存-延迟权衡方案。

Abstract: In-memory graph-based approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) search has superior search performance but incurs significant memory footprint. Disk-based methods reduce memory usage but suffer from high disk access latency. A common challenge is how to achieve low-latency search while significantly reducing memory footprint. In this paper, we propose SkipDisk, a disk-memory hybrid ANN search that significantly reduces memory footprint while achieving search latency comparable to or lower than in-memory method HNSW. By analyzing existing disk-based methods, we observed that disk access remains the primary bottleneck, and existing lower bound based filtering methods are two loose to effectively reduce disk access. Therefore, we design SkipDisk to achieve tight lower bound with low memory footprint to reduce the search latency. First, we design a dedicated pivot for each point to improve the lower bound of the triangle inequality for effective filtering. We further design an estimation-based approach based on this lower bound. Second, to reduce the memory footprint, we employ a three-level data pruning strategy to preserve informative data in memory. Third, to further reduce search latency, we design an asynchronous I/O strategy based on the decoupling of in-memory search and disk access by storing neighbor nodes in memory. Experiments show that our method achieves a latency of 85 of HNSW's latency with approximately 10 memory footprint, and a latency to 63 of HNSW's with a slightly higher memory footprint of around 20.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [240] [Toward Space-Based Public Key Systems: Enabling Secure Space Communications through In-Orbit Trust Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05948)
*Rehana Yasmin,Paulo Esteves-Verissimo,Ali Shoker*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对New Space时代卫星数量激增带来的认证需求，提出将PKI从地面迁移至太空的两种架构方案：天-地集成PKI（轨道验证）和全自主天基PKI（太空颁发与验证），并分析了多运营商环境下的可扩展性、可用性、安全性、成本和复杂度等权衡，以及基线延迟分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统依赖地面的PKI在动态太空环境中存在延迟高、操作瓶颈多、可扩展性差等问题，无法满足近地轨道卫星群安全、近实时的认证与协调需求。

Method: 提出了两种天基PKI部署方案：(1) 天-地集成PKI——在轨验证机构负责证书验证，颁发仍由地面完成；(2) 全自主天基PKI——证书颁发与验证均在太空完成。并从多个维度对比分析了两种方案的部署权衡。

Result: 提供了两种方案的部署权衡分析（可扩展性、可用性、安全性、成本、操作复杂度），并给出了基线延迟分析以说明在轨信任管理的性能影响。

Conclusion: 将PKI功能移至太空可减少对地面站的依赖，提升多运营商环境下的互操作性和协作能力，是支持未来大规模太空任务的关键技术方向。

Abstract: The New Space era has led to a rapid increase in satellites operated by independent entities in near-Earth orbit. This shift enables richer space services but also requires secure, near-real-time coordination, making efficient authentication of space assets critical for next-generation missions. Traditional ground-dependent Public Key Infrastructure (PKI) suffers from latency and operational bottlenecks that limit scalability and availability in dynamic space environments. This paper proposes architectural designs for space-based PKI that shift certificate management and validation from ground infrastructure into space, reducing reliance on ground stations while enabling interoperability and cross-entity collaboration. Two deployment schemes are introduced: a space-ground integrated PKI with in-orbit validation authorities, and a fully autonomous space-based PKI with in-space issuance and validation. We analyze deployment trade-offs in scalability, availability, security, cost, and operational complexity in multi-operator environments. A baseline latency analysis is provided to illustrate performance implications of in-orbit trust management.

</details>


### [241] [Identifier-Free Code Embedding Models for Scalable Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05251)
*Eric Wolos,Michael Doyle*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对二进制逆向工程中函数关联问题，提出使用对比学习微调Qwen3-Embedding模型，实现源代码与反编译剥离代码之间的双向关联，在各项基准测试上大幅超越现有模型，并能泛化到未显式训练的常量算法关联任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有函数关联搜索工具未能充分利用AI搜索能力，且已有嵌入模型工作不涵盖源代码与反编译剥离代码之间的双向关联，缺乏满足标准预处理需求的完整方案。

Method: 形式化定义了函数关联问题，采用对比学习（contrastive learning）对Qwen3-Embedding模型进行微调，以提升嵌入模型在两种代码表示间的双向关联性能。

Result: 微调后的模型在所有函数关联基线测试上以显著优势超越其他模型，并能泛化到未显式训练的常量算法关联任务上。

Conclusion: 对比学习微调Qwen3-Embedding模型能有效弥合源代码与反编译代码之间的表示差异，在函数关联任务上达到最优性能，且具备良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Function association is a useful process for binary reverse engineers. Search tools exist to perform association at scale, but they do not utilize the full range of capabilities that AI-enabled search provides. Prior work has explored the development of embedding models for association between certain reverse engineering code representations, but that work does not cover bidirectional association between source code and decompiled, stripped code with standard preprocessing requirements. To bridge this gap, we formalize this function association problem and evaluate the extent to which embedding models can bidirectionally associate between these two representations. To improve model performance at this task, we fine-tune a Qwen3-Embedding model with contrastive learning. We find that our new model outperforms other models on all function association baselines by a substantial margin and generalizes to a constant-algorithm association task it is not explicitly trained on.

</details>


### [242] [SecureMCP: A Policy-Enforced LLM Data Access Framework for AIoT Systems via Model Context Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05260)
*Wonbae Kim,Hee-Kyong Yoo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SecureMCP框架，将基于角色的访问控制（RBAC）与MCP服务器相结合，为LLM生成的SQL查询构建多层防御体系。该框架包含五个防御模块，在IoT-SQL数据集上验证了其执行准确性（63.8%-76.4%）和策略合规性（82.3%），真实防御失败率仅为3.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的SQL查询在AIoT系统中存在严重安全风险，提示注入攻击可操纵LLM生成未授权查询以泄露敏感数据或执行破坏性操作。现有NL2SQL研究聚焦查询准确性，MCP服务器实现仅提供SQL级别保护，缺乏细粒度的基于角色的访问控制。

Method: SecureMCP框架集成RBAC与MCP服务器，包含五个顺序执行的防御模块：check_policy（表/列级RBAC）、explain_gate（代价爆炸查询拦截）、SQL Interceptor（危险模式检测）、Risk Level Filter（SQL风险分类）和DB Isolation（跨数据库限制），采用失效关闭（fail-closed）流水线架构，覆盖OWASP Top 10中六种提示注入类型。

Result: 实验A表明防御模块保持执行准确性，四个RBAC角色下EX-in-ALLOW保持在65.1%-76.4%，与未保护基线63.8%匹配。实验B显示在2400个对抗性查询上达到82.3%的策略合规性，真实防御失败率仅3.4%。check_policy贡献78.7%的拦截量，次级模块额外提升17.5个百分点。注入成功率达72.5%，证实LLM高度易受攻击。

Conclusion: SecureMCP验证了外部策略执行对于保护LLM生成SQL查询的必要性和有效性，多层防御架构能显著提升系统安全性，同时保持查询执行准确性。

Abstract: The deployment of Large Language Model (LLM)-generated SQL queries in Artificial Intelligence of Things (AIoT) systems introduces critical security risks, as prompt injection attacks can manipulate LLMs into producing unauthorized queries that expose sensitive data or execute destructive operations. Existing NL2SQL research focuses on query accuracy, while MCP server implementations provide only SQL-level protections without fine-grained role-based access control. This paper proposes SecureMCP, a policy-enforced LLM data access framework integrating Role-Based Access Control (RBAC) with an MCP server to establish multi-layer defense for LLM-generated SQL execution. The framework incorporates five defense modules -- check_policy for table-and-column-level RBAC, explain_gate for cost-explosive query blocking, SQL Interceptor for dangerous pattern detection, Risk Level Filter for SQL risk classification, and DB Isolation for cross-database restriction -- operating in a sequential fail-closed pipeline mapped to six prompt injection types grounded in the OWASP Top 10 for LLM Applications. We evaluate SecureMCP on the IoT-SQL dataset (11 tables, 173 columns, 239,398 records) using Qwen3-8B. Experiment A demonstrates that defense modules preserve execution accuracy, with EX-in-ALLOW remaining within 65.1%-76.4% across four RBAC roles, matching the unprotected baseline of 63.8%. Experiment B shows that SecureMCP achieves 82.3% Policy Compliance on 2,400 adversarial queries, with genuine defense failures limited to 3.4%. The defense-in-depth analysis reveals check_policy accounts for 78.7% of blocks, while secondary modules contribute an additional 17.5 percentage-point improvement. The Injection Incorporation Rate of 72.5% confirms high LLM susceptibility, establishing the necessity of external policy enforcement.

</details>


### [243] [Differential Privacy in the Extensive-Form Bandit Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05266)
*Stephen Pasteris,Rahul Savani,Theodore Turocy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了扩展式赌博机问题中的局部差分隐私，提出了一种满足ε-局部差分隐私的算法，实现了$\tilde{O}(\sqrt{A\ln(S)T}/ε)$的遗憾界，其中A为动作总数，S为简化策略数，T为试验次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展式赌博机问题中隐私保护尚未被研究，而局部差分隐私作为最强形式的差分隐私具有重要实际意义，填补该空白是本文的核心动机。

Method: 提出了一种针对扩展式博弈的在线学习算法，该算法在每个试验回合中通过服务器向用户传输简化策略，并在局部差分隐私约束下更新策略，时间复杂度与传输简化策略所需时间相当（至多对数因子）。

Result: 算法满足ε-局部差分隐私，遗憾界为$\tilde{O}(\sqrt{A\ln(S)T}/ε)$，每个试验回合的时间复杂度仅比传输简化策略多一个对数因子。

Conclusion: 这是首次将任何形式的差分隐私引入扩展式赌博机问题，且局部差分隐私作为最强隐私保护形式，为后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We consider the extensive-form bandit problem, where on each trial the learner (a user coordinated by a server) plays an extensive-form game against an oblivious adversary, observing the information sets it finds itself in as well as the resulting payoff/loss. We give an algorithm for this problem that satisfies $ε$-local differential privacy and attains a regret of $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{A\ln(S)T}/ε)$, where $A$ is the total number of actions that the learner can possibly take, $S$ is the number of the learner's possible reduced strategies, and $T$ is the number of trials. On each trial, the time complexity of our algorithm is, up to a factor logarithmic in the maximum number of actions at an infoset, equal to the time required for the server to transmit the reduced strategy to the user. We note that local differential privacy is the strongest version of differential privacy and, to the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to study differential privacy of any form in the extensive-form bandit problem.

</details>


### [244] [Dynamic Authorization for Knowledge-Base Agents in 6G](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05269)
*Loay Abdelrazek,Leyli Karacay,Marin Orlic*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对6G分布式多智能体系统，提出了一种结合角色和一阶逻辑谓词的混合授权框架，在三元组级别实施零信任原则，消除权限继承，确保智能体仅访问其功能生命周期所需的元数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着6G架构向去中心化多智能体系统（MAS）演进，确保共享知识库（KB）的安全访问至关重要。传统授权模型（如RBAC）无法为与基于语义的数据交互的自主智能体提供所需的细粒度控制。

Method: 提出了一种混合授权框架，集成角色与一阶逻辑（FOL）谓词，在知识图谱层面执行零信任原则；通过在三元组层面（主语-谓语-宾语）强制授权，消除权限继承。

Result: 在三元组级别实施授权，确保智能体仅能访问其特定功能生命周期所需的元数据。

Conclusion: 该混合授权框架能够解决传统RBAC模型在6G去中心化多智能体系统中细粒度不足的问题，通过三元组级别的零信任授权有效保障知识库安全。

Abstract: As 6G architectures transition toward decentralized Multi-Agent Systems (MAS), ensuring secure access to shared Knowledge Bases (KB) is critical. Traditional authorization models like RBAC fail to provide the granularity required for autonomous agents interacting with Semantic-based data. This work proposes a hybrid authorization framework that integrates roles and First-Order Logic (FOL) predicates to enforce zero-trust principles at the knowledge-graph level. We eliminate permission inheritance by enforcing authorization at the triple level (Subject-Predicate-Object), ensuring agents only access metadata required for their specific functional lifecycle.

</details>


### [245] [Shattering the Echo Chamber: Hidden Safeguards in Manuscripts Against the AI Takeover of Peer Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05271)
*Oubo Ma,Ruixiao Lin,Jiahao Chen,Yuan Su,Yong Yang,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出IntraGuard，一种基于PDF结构-视觉解耦的黑盒防御框架，用于防止审稿人将同行评审完全外包给商业聊天机器人。IntraGuard通过在PDF底层结构中嵌入异构防御文本对象（不改变视觉呈现），实现显式（触发拒绝/警告信号）和隐式（嵌入预定义文本标记）两种防御策略，在7个商业聊天机器人设置和12个学术会议中取得最高84%的防御成功率，且对人工审稿无影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编辑委员会和程序委员会日益担忧审稿人将同行评审完全外包给商业聊天机器人，而现有聊天机器人缺乏独立批判性思维和深度推理能力来评估科学新颖性。现有防御方法（向稿件中嵌入隐藏指令）依赖同质化有效载荷并以流间方式注入，容易被清洗或中和。

Method: 提出IntraGuard防御框架，基于PDF的结构-视觉解耦特性。支持显式策略（触发拒绝/警告信号）和隐式策略（嵌入预定义文本标记）。通过三种流内注入机制，将异构防御文本对象嵌入PDF底层结构而不改变视觉呈现。该框架在委员会侧部署，黑盒且不依赖特定会议格式。

Result: 在7个真实商业聊天机器人设置和12个跨学科会议/期刊上进行评估，防御成功率最高达84%。对人工审稿人保持同行评审不变性。平均每篇稿件仅需1秒额外开销（普通个人电脑）。评估了11种自适应攻击（稿件清洗和指令干扰），讨论了集成防御的构建。

Conclusion: IntraGuard作为一种轻量级、硬件无关的防御框架，能有效防止端到端审稿外包，同时不影响人工审稿人的正常评审过程。未来需要考虑构建集成防御以应对更复杂的自适应攻击。

Abstract: As LLMs become increasingly capable, editorial boards and program committees are growing concerned about reviewers who fully outsource peer review to commercial chatbots. This concern stems from prior findings that current chatbots lack the independent critical thinking and depth of reasoning required to assess scientific novelty. One promising direction for mitigating this concern is to embed hidden instructions into manuscripts that disrupt or alter chatbot-generated reviews. However, existing methods remain intuitive and fragile, as they typically rely on homogeneous payloads injected in an inter-stream manner, rendering them susceptible to sanitization or neutralization.
  In this paper, we identify End-to-End Review Outsourcing as an emerging threat and propose IntraGuard, a black-box, venue-agnostic defense framework grounded in the structural--visual decoupling inherent to the PDF. Designed for committee-side deployment, IntraGuard supports both explicit strategies that trigger refusal or warning signals, and implicit strategies that embed predefined textual markers into the generated review. These strategies can be deployed via any of three intra-stream injection mechanisms, each of which seamlessly embeds heterogeneous defensive text objects within the PDF's underlying structure without altering its visual presentation. Extensive evaluations across 7 real-world commercial chatbot settings and 12 venues spanning diverse disciplines show that IntraGuard achieves a defense success rate of up to 84%, while preserving peer-review invariance for human reviewers. IntraGuard is lightweight and hardware-independent, incurring an average overhead of only one second per manuscript on a commodity personal computer. We further evaluate 11 adaptive attacks spanning manuscript sanitization and instruction interference, and discuss the implications of constructing ensemble defenses.

</details>


### [246] [Sealing the Audit-Runtime Gap for LLM Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05274)
*Tingda Shen,Yebo Feng,Konglin Zhu,Xiaojun Jia,Yang Liu,Lin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SIGIL 是一个去中心化框架，通过链上注册表、DAO审计和加载时验证协议，将LLM技能从发布到运行时进行加密绑定，有效弥合审计-运行时鸿沟，抵御注入、篡改和抽地毯攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生态（如Claude Code、ChatGPT）日益依赖技能包（自然语言指令+可执行工具），但技能内容无法与可信指令可靠分离，且可执行端可调用特权操作，导致技能供应链面临注入、篡改和抽地毯攻击。现有防御存在阶段性割裂：中心化签名、脱离运行时的审计报告、无法证明审批内容的策略引擎，均无法弥合审计与运行时之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出SIGIL框架，包含：(1) 去中心化防篡改链上注册表，LLM直接从中获取技能，支持四种发布类型（透明、许可、密封、承诺）；(2) DAO审计委员会，采用权益质押-罚没经济模型，支持可插拔审计方法；(3) 技能验证加载器（SVL）嵌入为强制加载路径，执行加载时验证协议：按类型检索并解密技能、验证完整性、强制执行权限清单。

Result: 在真实部署环境下对1023个真实技能进行实验，覆盖六种攻击类型。加载时SVL验证每个技能的完整性并强制执行已批准的权限清单，批量验证完成时间低于86毫秒，证明方案在实用成本下可行。

Conclusion: SIGIL首次实现了LLM技能从发布到运行时的加密绑定，弥合了审计-运行时鸿沟，以实际可行的成本提供了可验证的托管和加载保障。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) ecosystems such as Claude Code and ChatGPT increasingly rely on skills: packages of natural-language instructions and executable tools. Once in the LLM's context, skill content cannot be reliably separated from trusted instructions, and a skill's executable side can invoke privileged actions, exposing the skill supply chain to injection, tampering, and rug-pull attacks. Existing defenses are stage-bound: centralized signing, audit reports unbound from the runtime artifact, or policy engines that cannot attest to what was approved. We present SIGIL, the first framework that seals the audit-runtime gap for LLM skills. SIGIL delivers verifiable hosting through a tamper-evident, decentralized on-chain registry from which LLMs fetch skills directly. The registry admits four publication types, Transparent, Licensed, Sealed, and Committed, spanning plaintext public distribution, monetized access, custodial use, and off-chain workflows; before admission, every skill is vetted by a Decentralized Autonomous Organization (DAO) audit committee that supports pluggable auditing methods under a stake-and-slash economic model. At load time, SIGIL delivers verified loading through a skill verification protocol executed by a Skill Verification Loader (SVL) embedded as the mandatory loading path: the SVL retrieves and decrypts the skill as its type requires, verifies its integrity against the on-chain record, and enforces its permission manifest before context injection. We evaluate SIGIL on a real-world deployment against 1,023 in-the-wild skills spanning six attack types. At load time, the SVL verifies each skill's integrity against its on-chain record and enforces its approved permission manifest, completing batched verification under 86 ms. Together, these results show that LLM skills can be cryptographically bound from publication through runtime at practical cost.

</details>


### [247] [GLiNER Guard: Unified Encoder Family for Production LLM Safety and Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05277)
*Bogdan Minko,Sabrina Sadiekh,Evgeniy Kokuykin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出GLiNER Guard (GLiGuard)，一个统一编码器架构，在单次前向传播中同时完成安全分类和PII检测。紧凑模型(145-147M)在动态批处理下达到193请求/秒，P99延迟低于1秒，吞吐量是GLiNER2的1.6倍；Omni变体(209M)在安全基准上与更大模型竞争力相当。同时发布PII-Bench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产级LLM系统需要在严格的延迟和成本约束下同时进行安全审核和PII检测，现有方案存在精度与效率的权衡：自回归模型准确但昂贵，轻量编码器更快但能力较弱。

Method: 提出GLiGuard统一编码器架构，通过单次前向传播同时完成安全分类和PII检测。包含三种变体：紧凑型单/双编码器(145-147M)用于高吞吐服务，Omni(209M)用于更强审核质量。

Result: 紧凑模型在单A100动态批处理下达到193请求/秒，P99延迟低于1秒，吞吐量比GLiNER2高1.6倍。Omni在公开安全基准上与更大模型竞争力相当。

Conclusion: 基于编码器的防护栏（guardrails）为始终在线的安全审核提供了一种低成本的实用替代方案。

Abstract: Production LLM systems require both safety moderation and PII detection under strict latency and cost constraints. This creates a trade-off: autoregressive moderators are accurate but expensive, while lightweight encoders are faster but less capable. We present GLiNER Guard (GLiGuard), a unified encoder that performs safety classification and PII detection in a single forward pass, simplifying safety pipelines. We introduce three variants: compact uni- and bi-encoders (145-147M) for high-throughput serving, and GLiGuard Omni (209M) for stronger moderation quality. Under dynamic batching on a single A100, the compact model reaches 193 requests/sec with P99 latency below 1s, achieving 1.6x higher throughput than GLiNER2. Omni remains competitive with much larger moderators on public safety benchmarks. We also release PII-Bench, a span-level benchmark for evaluating PII detection in end-to-end pipelines. Overall, encoder-based guardrails offer a practical low-cost alternative for always-on moderation. Models and benchmarks are released on HuggingFace.

</details>


### [248] [Privacy Without Losing Place: A Paradigm for Private Retrieval in Spatial RAGs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05459)
*Kennedy Edemacu,Mohammad Mahdi Shokri,Vinay M. Shashidhar,Jong Wook Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PAS（隐私锚替代）机制，通过相对锚点编码实现空间RAG系统中的位置隐私保护，在合成城市数据集上取得约370-400米对抗定位误差的同时保留半数以上检索性能，并发现非单调的隐私-效用关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私方法直接扰动用户位置，难以无缝集成到现代RAG流水线中，需要一种结构化、可兼容的空间隐私保护机制。

Method: 采用相对锚点编码表示位置，包含锚点、方向区间和距离区间三个组件，将位置隐私保护嵌入RAG检索流程中。

Result: 在合成城市数据集上实现约370-400米对抗定位误差，保留超过50%的基础检索性能；下游生成质量相对稳健；隐私-效用关系呈非单调性。

Conclusion: 结构化空间表示为基于位置推理的RAG系统提供了实用的隐私保护途径，大语言模型能够补偿不完美的空间检索结果。

Abstract: This work introduces PAS -- Privacy Anchor Substitution, a structured mechanism for enabling user location privacy in spatial retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems. Unlike conventional differential privacy methods that directly perturb user locations, PAS represents location with relative anchor encoding consisting of an anchor, direction bin, and distance bin, allowing seamless integration with modern RAG pipelines. We evaluate PAS on a synthetic urban dataset and show that it achieves impressive coarse privacy guarantees, with approximately 370-400m adversarial location error, while retaining more than half of the baseline retrieval performance. Despite the slight drop in retrieval performance, the downstream generation quality under PAS remains comparatively robust, indicating that large language models can compensate for imperfect spatial retrieval. Furthermore, we provide empirical analysis showing that PAS exhibits non-monotonic privacy-utility relationship with respect to privacy parameters. We attribute this to geometric bias induced by anchor discretization, making it different from continuous noise mechanisms such as geo-indistinguishability. Our results show that structured spatial representations offer a practical approach to privacy in location based reasoning in RAG systems.

</details>


### [249] [SOCpilot: Verifying Policy Compliance for LLM-Assisted Incident Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05501)
*Sidnei Barbieri,Leonardo Vaz de Meneses,Ágney Lopes Roth Ferraz,Lourenço Alves Pereira Júnior*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SOCpilot是一个可复现的合规性验证框架，用于检测LLM驱动的安全编排（SOAR）协作者在起草事件响应计划时是否违反策略规则（如强制步骤、顺序要求、审批门控）。它通过固定的公共工件（事件包、动作目录、策略规则、验证器、证据表面）对200个真实金融行业SOC事件进行评估，发现确定性验证器能消除466个不合规动作且不降低召回率，但相同的内联策略文本会使两个LLM提供商产生相反的合规倾向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全运营中心（SOC）开始使用LLM作为协作者起草事件响应计划，但这些计划虽然动作在目录中有效，却可能违反强制步骤、所需顺序或审批门控等策略约束。现有方法缺乏可测量的合规性检查手段，尤其是在计划边界（即用户审查之前）发现违规问题。

Method: SOCpilot固定了五个可复现的公共组件：事件包（incident package）、动作目录（action catalog）、策略规则（policy rules）、验证器（verifier）和公开证据表面（public evidence surface）。然后利用验证器检查协作者提出的动作轨迹（action trace）是否合规。在200个来自金融行业生产SOC的匿名真实事件上，对两个LLM提供商进行评估，并与相同SOAR案例中分析师撰写的参考方案进行对比。

Result: 1）相同的内联策略文本使两个LLM提供商的合规性呈现相反方向的变化；2）确定性验证器消除了466个不合规的、需要审批门控的动作，且不降低基线任务召回率；3）在固定语料库的3次重复运行中，聚合率保持稳定；4）官方证据聚焦于恢复和遏制相关的审批门控决策，工件还暴露了强制步骤和排序修复的零成本就绪检查。

Conclusion: SOCpilot使得LLM协作者在事件响应中的合规性变得可测量和可复现。验证器能有效移除不合规动作而不牺牲召回率。但不同LLM对相同策略文本的响应可能截然相反，说明单纯依赖提示文本不足以确保合规，必须结合确定性验证机制。

Abstract: Security operations centers (SOCs) are beginning to use large language models (LLMs) as copilots to draft incident-response plans. These plans may include actions that are valid per the catalog but still violate mandatory steps, required ordering, or approval gates before analyst review. SOCpilot makes this compliance question measurable at the plan boundary. It fixes the incident package, action catalog, policy rules, verifier, and public evidence surface. Next, it verifies the copilot's proposed action trace. We evaluate two LLM providers on 200 real incidents from an anonymized production SOC in a financial-sector case study. We compare their plans to paired analyst-authored references from the same security orchestration, automation, and response (SOAR) cases. An identical inline policy text moves the two providers in opposite directions. A deterministic verifier removes 466 non-compliant, approval-gated actions, without reducing baseline-task recall. Aggregate rates remain stable across 3 reruns of the fixed corpus. The official evidence focuses on approval-gated decisions regarding recovery and containment. Separately, the artifact exposes zero-cost readiness checks for mandatory and ordering repairs. We release the runnable artifact so independent reviewers can rederive the public results without access to private incident data.

</details>


### [250] [Architecture Matters: Comparing RAG Systems under Knowledge Base Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05632)
*Samuel Korn*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了四种RAG架构（Vanilla RAG、Agentic RAG、MADAM-RAG、Recursive Language Models）在单文档投毒攻击下的鲁棒性，发现架构是影响对抗鲁棒性的关键变量：攻击成功率从81.9%（Vanilla）到24.4%（RLM），跨度近58个百分点。分解发现，对抗性框架（而非检索优化）是攻击优势的主要来源，并将脆弱性定位在内容推理阶段。MADAM-RAG虽检测矛盾率最高（但LLM评判器精度仅~48.5%），但无法可靠解决矛盾，且干净输入下非回答率达41.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG对抗攻击研究几乎仅针对Vanilla RAG，而设计了处理冲突信息能力的高级架构（如多智能体辩论、智能检索、递归语言模型）在面对对抗性优化过的矛盾信息时的鲁棒性尚不清楚。

Method: 在921个Natural Questions QA对上，对四种RAG架构进行单文档投毒（N=1）控制实验，比较干净基线、朴素注入和CorruptRAG-AK（一种元认知框架攻击）；引入七类别行为分类法分析矛盾检测、 hedging和失败模式。

Result: CorruptRAG-AK下攻击成功率从Vanilla的81.9%到RLM的24.4%，跨度近58个百分点；对抗性框架（而非检索优化）是攻击优势主因；MADAM-RAG矛盾检测率最高但精度仅~48.5%，且干净输入下非回答率高达41.4%。

Conclusion: 架构是影响RAG对抗鲁棒性的高影响变量；跨架构的共性脆弱点位于内容推理阶段而非检索阶段；当前高级架构虽能部分检测矛盾但无法可靠解决，面临准确率与鲁棒性的权衡。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems are vulnerable to knowledge base poisoning, yet existing attacks have been evaluated almost exclusively against vanilla retrieve-then-generate pipelines. Architectures designed to handle conflicting retrieved information - multi-agent debate, agentic retrieval, recursive language models - remain untested against adversarially optimized contradictions. We evaluate four RAG architectures (vanilla RAG, agentic RAG, MADAM-RAG, and Recursive Language Models) under controlled single-document (N=1) poisoning on 921 Natural Questions QA pairs, comparing a clean baseline, naive injection, and CorruptRAG-AK - an adversarial attack whose meta-epistemic framing targets credibility assessment. Architecture is a high-impact variable in adversarial robustness: under CorruptRAG-AK, attack success rates range from 81.9% (vanilla) to 24.4% (RLM) - a spread of nearly 58 percentage points across architectures with comparable clean accuracy (~92%). Decomposing this gap, once the poisoned document is retrieved, adversarial framing - not retrieval optimization - drives the majority of CorruptRAG-AK's advantage for three of four architectures, localizing the cross-architecture vulnerability at the content-reasoning stage. Our MADAM-RAG reimplementation shows the highest apparent contradiction detection rate, though our LLM judge over-identifies this behavior (~48.5% precision), so reported rates are upper bounds. Regardless of detection, MADAM-RAG cannot resolve contradictions reliably, producing a 41.4% non-answer rate even on clean inputs - though implementation divergences from the original may contribute. We introduce a seven-category behavioral taxonomy capturing contradiction detection, hedging, and failure modes beyond binary accuracy. Code, data, and analysis notebooks are publicly available.

</details>


### [251] [AoI-Guided Client Selection for Robust and Timely Federated Intrusion Detection in Cloud-Edge Security Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05644)
*Chun Yin Chiu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究联邦学习在云边入侵检测中的客户端参与问题，提出三种基于信息时效性（AoI）的轻量级调度策略（AoI优先、效用优先、混合策略），实验表明AoI感知选择可将平均AoI降低39-41%、峰值AoI降低约70%，同时保持检测性能，混合策略提供了可解释的新鲜度-质量平衡旋钮。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在云边联邦学习入侵检测生产环境中，只有部分客户端每轮参与，异构带宽、掉队者和掉线导致服务器依赖过时信息，而现有研究未将客户端参与视为时效性感知的系统问题。

Method: 提出三种轻量级客户端选择策略：AoI优先、效用优先、混合AoI+效用规则（含可调权衡参数），在CIC-IDS2017、NSL-KDD、ToN-IoT及合成漂移基准上评估，涵盖干净、投毒及投毒+鲁棒聚合场景。

Result: AoI感知选择在固定通信预算下相比随机采样降低平均AoI约39-41%、峰值AoI约70%；混合策略通常保持Macro-F1/AUC，提供可解释的新鲜度-质量-鲁棒性平衡旋钮，但在纳入误报率后并非均匀Pareto占优。

Conclusion: 云边隐私保护入侵分析可通过轻量级调度层改善时效性，无需改变底层FL参与预算；选择策略本身并非独立的拜占庭防御机制，需结合鲁棒聚合（如修剪均值聚合）应对标签翻转投毒。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) is attractive for cloud-edge intrusion detection because it enables collaborative training over distributed telemetry without centralizing raw logs. In production security analytics pipelines, however, only a subset of clients participates in each round, and heterogeneous bandwidth, stragglers, and dropouts can cause the server to rely on stale client information. This paper studies client participation as a timeliness-aware systems problem using Age of Information (AoI). We compare three lightweight policies for federated intrusion detection: AoI-first, utility-first, and a hybrid AoI+utility rule with a tunable trade-off parameter.
  Across a CIC-IDS2017 DDoS/PortScan mini subset, NSL-KDD, ToN-IoT, and a synthetic drift benchmark under clean, poisoning, and poisoning-plus-robust-aggregation settings, AoI-aware selection reduces average AoI by about 39--41% and peak AoI by about 70% relative to random sampling while keeping the per-round communication budget fixed. The hybrid policy usually preserves Macro-F1/AUC and provides an interpretable knob for balancing freshness, detection quality, and robustness, although it is not uniformly Pareto-dominant once false positive rate is included. Robustness is evaluated by combining AoI-guided selection with trimmed-mean aggregation under label-flip poisoning; the selection policy itself is not intended as a standalone Byzantine defense. The main practical message is that cloud-edge, privacy-preserving intrusion analytics can improve timeliness through a lightweight scheduling layer without changing the underlying FL participation budget.

</details>


### [252] [$α$-Wasserstein Mechanism for Rényi Pufferfish Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05723)
*Ni Ding,Wenjin Yang,Zijian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出基于α-Wasserstein距离的Rényi Pufferfish隐私机制，通过Hölder不等式校准Laplace和Gaussian噪声尺度参数，实现精确的(α, ε)-Rényi Pufferfish隐私保护，实验表明该机制相比传统W∞方法显著降低噪声功率，且无需δ近似松弛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Pufferfish隐私框架主要基于W∞度量（对应ε-差分隐私），但缺乏对Rényi散度框架下更精细隐私度量的支持，且传统方法噪声功率较大、需要δ近似松弛。

Method: 利用Hölder不等式建立Wα度量与Rényi散度之间的上界关系，据此校准Laplace和Gaussian机制的噪声尺度参数；将Wα机制推广至指数机制；针对Gaussian噪声设计专门的Wα机制。

Result: 1) 当α=∞时退化为经典的W∞机制；2) Gaussian机制相比Laplace机制提供更优的效用；3) α-Wasserstein机制相比W∞方法显著降低噪声功率；4) 实现精确的(α, ε)-Rényi Pufferfish Privacy，无需δ近似。

Conclusion: 提出的α-Wasserstein机制是首个实现精确(α, ε)-Rényi Pufferfish Privacy的方法，在隐私-效用权衡上优于传统W∞方法，且Gaussian机制优于Laplace机制。

Abstract: This paper introduces the $α$-Wasserstein mechanism for achieving Rényi Pufferfish Privacy using Laplace and Gaussian noise. By leveraging Hölder's inequality, we demonstrate that the scale parameter of the Laplace mechanism can be calibrated via an upper bound on the $W_α$ metric to satisfy $(α, ε)$-Rényi Pufferfish Privacy for $α\in (1, \infty]$. We show that at the limit $α= \infty$, this framework recovers the established $W_\infty$ mechanism for $ε$-pufferfish privacy. This result is subsequently extended to the exponential mechanism. Furthermore, we propose a $W_α$ mechanism for Gaussian noise for $α\in (1, \infty)$, demonstrating that it generalizes existing results within the Rényi Differential Privacy framework. Experimental evaluations reveal that our $α$-Wasserstein mechanism significantly reduces noise power compared to the conventional $W_\infty$-based approach, with the Gaussian mechanism providing superior utility over the Laplace mechanism. Notably, the mechanisms derived in this work achieve exact $(α, ε)$-Rényi Pufferfish Privacy without requiring additional relaxations, such as $δ$-approximations.

</details>


### [253] [SuperPaymaster: Eliminating Centralized Signer Authority via Asset-Oriented Abstraction to Reconcile Usability and Decentralization in Account Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05774)
*Huifeng Jiao,Nathapon Udomlertsakul*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于链上资产（Gas Card）的面向资产抽象（AOA）方案替代传统的面向过程抽象（POA），在Optimism主网实现SuperPaymaster，消除了中心化离线签名者的审查瓶颈，并验证了其在Gas消耗和去中心化方面的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ERC-4337 Paymaster依赖中心化离线签名服务器（POA），存在审查瓶颈和单点故障问题，需要在可用性、赞助去中心化和经济效率之间寻找更好的平衡。

Method: 采用设计科学研究方法，提出AOA范式：将支付能力封装在用户持有的链上资产Gas Card中，通过Soulbound Token状态和确定性策略规则锚定赞助有效性。在Optimism主网实现SuperPaymaster系统，并进行trace级Gas分解分析与故障切换模拟。

Result: SuperPaymaster在纯L2执行Gas上（167,830）低于两个商业样本，相比DEX路由ERC-20基线（328,937）降低49%；消除中心化签名的执行成本约32k Gas；总计费Gas差距主要来自bundler定价而非架构；故障模拟表明可通过替代中继器绕过非合作中继。

Conclusion: AOA能够有效降低可用性、赞助去中心化和经济效率之间的矛盾，为ERC-4337 Paymaster的去中心化提供可行方案。

Abstract: Most production ERC-4337 Paymasters rely on Process-Oriented Abstraction (POA): a centralized off-chain server signs each sponsorship request and therefore acts as a potential censorship bottleneck. We propose Asset-Oriented Abstraction (AOA), which encapsulates payment capability in a persistent, user-owned on-chain asset -- the ``Gas Card'' -- rather than in an off-chain signing process. Following Design Science Research, we implement SuperPaymaster on Optimism Mainnet. Its sponsorship validity is anchored in on-chain Soulbound Token state and deterministic policy rules, removing the off-chain signer as a hard validity gate. We evaluate gas cost on Optimism Mainnet using single-UserOp ERC-20 transfers ($n{=}50$ per system). Trace-level decomposition isolates an approximately 32k-gas delta as the execution cost of eliminating centralized signing. In pure L2 execution gas, SuperPaymaster (167,830) is lower than both vendor-as-deployed commercial samples, including a 49\% reduction against the DEX-routed ERC-20 baseline (328,937), because it replaces an on-chain liquidation path with an internal balance update. In total billed gas, the remaining gap to the cheapest baseline is explained primarily by bundler pricing rather than paymaster architecture. A failover simulation shows that non-cooperative relayers can be bypassed when an alternative relayer is available. These findings suggest that AOA can reduce the tension among usability, sponsorship decentralization, and economic efficiency.

</details>


### [254] [Stego Battlefield: Evaluating Image Steganography Attacks and Steganalysis Defenses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05789)
*Zhen Sun,Zongmin Zhang,Leyi Sheng,Yule Liu,Yifan Liao,Ke Li,Xinhu Zheng,Jiaheng Wei,Wenyuan Yang,Xinlei He*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SADBench是一个系统评估图像隐写攻击与防御能力的基准框架，包含4个核心任务，涵盖图片和文本载荷，揭示了INN和自编码器架构的稳定性、域内检测近乎完美、攻击可迁移而防御不可迁移，以及社交媒体上真实威胁持续存在等关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐写术在保护隐私的同时可被对手滥用作隐蔽通道，绕过内容审核、传播有害语义、向大模型注入恶意指令，而现有研究缺乏统一、系统的评估框架。

Method: 构建SADBench基准，包含4个核心任务：隐写攻击能力评估、隐写分析防御能力评估、效率评估和迁移性评估；覆盖图像载荷和文本载荷两种隐写方式，利用有害视觉语义和有毒指令模拟恶意攻击。

Result: (i) INN和自编码器方法架构稳定性最优；(ii) 域内检测近乎完美且成本低于生成；(iii) 攻击可稳健泛化到新分布而检测器无法适应，存在关键迁移性不对称；(iv) 社交媒体上真实威胁持续存在，载荷可通过模拟训练适应有损压缩。

Conclusion: SADBench建立了一个系统、可复现、可扩展的量化风险框架，推动隐写术防御领域可度量的安全驱动型进展。

Abstract: Image steganography is widely used to protect user privacy and enable covert communication. However, it can also be abused by the adversary as a covert channel to bypass content moderation, disseminate harmful semantics, and even hide malicious instructions in images to elicit dangerous outputs from large models, posing a practical security risk that continues to evolve. To address the lack of a unified and systematic evaluation framework, we propose SADBench, a systematic benchmark that assesses the adversary's ability to inject harmful secrets via steganography and the defender's ability to detect such threats through steganalysis. Crucially, SADBench comprises $4$ core tasks, namely steganography attack capability evaluation, steganalysis defense capability evaluation, efficiency evaluation, and transferability evaluation. It evaluates both image-payload and text-payload steganography across diverse cover distributions, utilizing harmful visual semantics and toxic instructions to simulate malicious attacks. Across a broad set of attacks and detectors, SADBench reveals that (i) INN and autoencoder-based methods demonstrate superior stability compared to other architectures, (ii) in-domain detection is near-perfect and cheaper than generation, (iii) a critical asymmetry exists in transferability where attacks robustly generalize to new distributions while detectors fail to adapt, and (iv) real-world threats persist on social media, where payloads either survive minimal compression or effectively adapt to aggressive compression via simulated training. Overall, SADBench establishes a systematic, reproducible, and extensible framework to quantify risks, paving the way for measurable and security-driven advancements in steganography defense.

</details>


### [255] [LCC-LLM: Leveraging Code-Centric Large Language Models for Malware Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05807)
*Christopher G. Pedraza Pohlenz,Hassan Jalil Hadi,Ali Hassan,Ali Shoker*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出LCC-LLM框架，包含约34K PE样本的LCCD数据集，结合LangGraph编排的静态分析、七层RAG流水线、CoVe验证和课程微调，在43种恶意软件分析任务上平均语义相似度0.634，真实案例通过率10/10，证明了以代码为中心、检索增强和验证引导推理能提升LLM辅助恶意软件归因的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在恶意软件归因中受限于不支持的指标和缺乏代码级依据，难以识别恶意和脆弱代码段，亟需一种以代码为中心、基于证据的框架来提升归因的可靠性和可操作性。

Method: 构建LCCD数据集（约34K PE样本，含反编译C代码、汇编、CFG/FCG、十六进制数据、PE元数据、可疑API证据及结构特征）；采用LangGraph编排七层RAG流水线，集成CoVE进行IoC验证和多维质量门控；使用课程排序指令数据通过QLoRA微调DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-14B和Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B。

Result: 在43种恶意软件分析任务上平均语义相似度0.634，结构化报告生成、IoC提取、漏洞评估、恶意软件配置提取和恶意软件类别检测等任务表现最佳；MalwareBazaar真实案例中结构化分析通过率10/10，生成CFG/FCG证据、MITRE ATT&CK映射和检测指导。

Conclusion: 以代码为中心的表示、检索依据和验证引导推理能显著提升LLM辅助恶意软件归因的可靠性和操作实用性。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly explored for malware analysis; however, current LLM-based malware attribution remains limited by unsupported indicators and insufficient code-level grounding for identifying malicious and vulnerable code segments. To address these limitations, this research introduces LCC-LLM, a code-centric benchmark dataset and evidence-grounded framework for malware attribution and multi-task static malware analysis. The proposed LCCD dataset contains approximately 34K PE samples processed through a large-scale reverse-engineering pipeline and represented using decompiled C code, assembly code, CFG/FCG artifacts, hexadecimal data, PE metadata, suspicious API evidence, and structural features. Beyond dataset construction, LCC-LLM integrates LangGraph-orchestrated static analysis with multi-source cybersecurity knowledge to support evidence-grounded malware reasoning. The framework employs a seven-layer retrieval-augmented generation pipeline, CoVe for IoC validation, and a multi-dimensional quality gate to improve factual reliability and analyst-oriented decision support. Curriculum-ordered instruction data is used to fine-tune DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-14B and Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B using QLoRA. Evaluation across 43 malware-analysis task types achieves an average semantic similarity of 0.634, with the highest task-level performance in structured report generation, IoC extraction, vulnerability assessment, malware configuration extraction, and malware class detection. In a real-world case study using MalwareBazaar samples, the grounded pipeline achieves a 10/10 structured analysis pass rate, producing CFG/FCG evidence, MITRE ATT&CK mappings, detection guidance, and analyst-ready reports. These results show that code-centric representations, retrieval grounding, and verification-guided reasoning improve the reliability and operational usefulness of LLM-assisted malware attribution.

</details>


### [256] [Heimdallr: Characterizing and Detecting LLM-Induced Security Risks in GitHub CI Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05969)
*Bonan Ruan,Yeqi Fu,Chuqi Zhang,Jiahao Liu,Jun Zeng,Zhenkai Liang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了GitHub CI工作流中由LLM引发的安全风险，提出了风险分类体系，并设计了Heimdallr混合分析框架，在300个手工标注的工作流上实现了高精度检测，已负责任披露802个漏洞实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GitHub CI工作流越来越多地集成LLM，但LLM安全与CI安全的交叉领域尚未被充分研究，存在新的攻击面。

Method: 设计Heimdallr混合分析框架，将工作流归一化为LLM-工作流属性图（L-WPG），结合可触发分析、LLM辅助数据流摘要和确定性传播来合成具体威胁向量发现。

Result: 在300个手工标注的工作流上，LLM节点识别F1=0.994，可触发分类准确率99.8%，威胁向量检测微平均F1=0.917。已披露802个漏洞实例，获得71个确认。

Conclusion: LLM在CI工作流中的集成带来了新的安全风险，Heimdallr框架能够有效检测此类威胁，表明该问题具有广泛的实际影响。

Abstract: GitHub Continuous Integration (CI) workflows increasingly integrate Large Language Models (LLMs) to automate review, triage, content generation, and repository maintenance. This creates a new attack surface: externally controllable workflow inputs can shape LLM prompts and outputs, which may in turn affect security decisions, repository state, or privileged execution. Although LLM security and CI security have each been studied extensively, their intersection remains underexplored. In this paper, we present the first study of LLM-induced security risks in GitHub CI workflows. We characterize the problem along the full execution chain and develop a taxonomy of high-level risk classes and concrete threat vectors. To detect such risks in practice, we design Heimdallr, a hybrid analysis framework that normalizes workflows into an LLM-Workflow Property Graph (L-WPG) and combines triggerability analysis, LLM-assisted dataflow summarization, and deterministic propagation to synthesize concrete threat-vector findings. Evaluated on 300 manually annotated unique workflows, Heimdallr achieves high accuracy on LLM-node identification (F1~=~0.994), triggerability classification (99.8%), and threat-vector detection (micro-average F1~=~0.917). As part of an ongoing detection and disclosure effort, we have so far responsibly disclosed 802 vulnerable workflow instances across 759 repositories and received 71 acknowledgments.

</details>


### [257] [LeakDojo: Decoding the Leakage Threats of RAG Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05818)
*Maosen Zhang,Jianshuo Dong,Boting Lu,Wenyue Li,Xiaoping Zhang,Tianwei Zhang,Han Qiu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: LeakDojo是一个可配置的RAG泄漏评估框架，系统研究了六种攻击方法在14个LLM、4个数据集和多种RAG系统上的表现，发现查询生成与对抗指令独立贡献泄漏，更强的指令遵循能力与更高泄漏风险相关，且RAG忠实度改进可能增加泄漏风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统虽然增强了LLM利用外部知识的能力，但也使宝贵的RAG数据库面临泄漏攻击风险。现有研究缺乏系统评估RAG泄漏风险的方法，因此需要构建一个可控的评估框架。

Method: 提出了LeakDojo框架，系统评估六种已有攻击方法在14个LLM、4个数据集和多种RAG系统上的表现，分析查询生成、对抗指令等因素对泄漏的独立贡献及相互关系。
