Result: (1) 查询生成和对抗指令独立贡献泄漏，整体泄漏近似等于二者乘积；(2) 更强的指令遵循能力与更高泄漏风险正相关；(3) RAG忠实度的改进可能引入更高的泄漏风险。

Conclusion: 这些发现为理解和缓解实际RAG系统中的泄漏问题提供了可操作的见解。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enables large language models (LLMs) to leverage external knowledge, but also exposes valuable RAG databases to leakage attacks. As RAG systems grow more complex and LLMs exhibit stronger instruction-following capabilities, existing studies fall short of systematically assessing RAG leakage risks. We present LeakDojo, a configurable framework for controlled evaluation of RAG leakage. Using LeakDojo, we benchmark six existing attacks across fourteen LLMs, four datasets, and diverse RAG systems. Our study reveals that (1) query generation and adversarial instructions contribute independently to leakage, with overall leakage well approximated by their product; (2) stronger instruction-following capability correlates with higher leakage risk; and (3) improvements in RAG faithfulness can introduce increased leakage risk. These findings provide actionable insights for understanding and mitigating RAG leakage in practice. Our codebase is available at https://github.com/yeasen-z/LeakDojo.

</details>


### [258] [LoopTrap: Termination Poisoning Attacks on LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05846)
*Huiyu Xu,Zhibo Wang,Wenhui Zhang,Ziqi Zhu,Yaopeng Wang,Kui Ren,Chun Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM Agent的自主执行循环存在"终止中毒"漏洞——攻击者可通过注入恶意提示扭曲Agent的终止判断，导致无界计算。作者设计了10种攻击策略，并提出自动化红队框架LoopTrap，通过行为画像和自适应陷阱合成实现3.57倍平均步数放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Agent通过迭代执行循环自主完成任务，这种自我导向的循环虽然带来自主性，但也引入了安全风险：攻击者可注入恶意提示干扰Agent的终止判断，使其误以为任务未完成而持续计算，导致无界计算消耗。

Method: 首先定义并系统刻画了"终止中毒"威胁，设计10种代表性攻击策略。通过对8种LLM Agent和60个任务的实证研究，发现不同Agent存在可迁移的行为特征。基于此构建LoopTrap自动化红队框架：通过轻量级探测构建目标Agent在4个脆弱性维度上的行为画像，进行自适应陷阱合成，利用自评分机制选择最优注入策略，并通过技能库抽象和自我反思实现持续改进。

Result: LoopTrap在8种主流Agent上实现了平均3.57倍的步数放大，峰值达到25倍。不同LLM Agent展现出独特的行为特征，这些可迁移的模式可作为对未知Agent和任务发起有效攻击的原则性指导。

Conclusion: LLM Agent的自主执行循环存在严重的终止中毒漏洞，LoopTrap作为自动化红队框架能有效暴露这一风险。Agent的行为特征具有可迁移性，可用于超越手工模板的可扩展红队测试。

Abstract: Modern LLM agents solve complex tasks by operating in iterative execution loops, where they repeatedly reason, act, and self-evaluate progress to determine when a task is complete. In this work, we show that while this self-directed loop facilitates autonomy, it also introduces a critical risk: by injecting malicious prompts into the agent's context, an adversary can distort the agent's termination judgment, making it believe the task remains incomplete and leading to unbounded computation.To understand this threat, we define and systematically characterize it as Termination Poisoning and design 10 representative attack strategies. Through a empirical study spanning 8 LLM agents and 60 tasks, we demonstrate that different LLM agents exhibit distinct behavioral signatures that determine which strategies succeed. These transferable patterns can serve as principled guidance for crafting effective attacks against previously unseen agents and tasks, enabling scalable red-teaming beyond manually designed templates. Building on these insights, we introduce LoopTrap, an automated red-teaming framework that synthesizes target-specific malicious prompts by exploiting agent behavioral tendencies. LoopTrap first constructs a behavioral profile of the target agent along four vulnerability dimensions via lightweight probing. It then performs adaptive trap synthesis, routing to the most effective strategy and selecting optimal injections via a self-scoring mechanism. Finally, successful traps are abstracted into a reusable skill library, while failed attempts are refined through self-reflection, ensuring continuous improvement. Extensive evaluation shows that LoopTrap achieves an average of 3.57$\times$ step amplification across 8 mainstream agents, with a peak of 25$\times$.

</details>


### [259] [SkillScope: Toward Fine-Grained Least-Privilege Enforcement for Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05868)
*Jiangrong Wu,Yuhong Nan,Yixi Lin,Huaijin Wang,Yuming Xiao,Shuai Wang,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SkillScope是一个用于Agent Skill最小权限强制执行的框架，通过图分析方法建模指令级流程和代码级操作，检测并约束超出用户任务必要范围的过度权限行为。实验表明，该方法在过度权限检测上达到94.53% F1值，能减少88.56%的过度权限触发实例，同时保持任务完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent Skill生态系统中存在合规风险：一个Skill可能执行超出用户当前任务最小必要范围的高影响力操作，违反最小权限原则。现有检测方法无法解决这一问题，因为该问题本质上是任务条件化的——同一操作在一个用户提示下是必要的，在另一个提示下则可能是过度授权的。

Method: 采用基于图的分析方法，将指令级流程和代码级操作建模为细粒度动作节点；提取潜在的过度权限候选；通过基于重放的分析在图实例化的用户任务下进行验证；通过控制流权限约束来限制已验证的过度权限操作。

Result: SkillScope在Skill过度权限检测上达到94.53% F1值。在实际数据中验证了7,039个存在过度权限行为的Skill，表明最小权限违反在当前的Skill生态系统中普遍存在。在权限约束评估中，SkillScope减少了88.56%的触发过度权限操作的任务内实例，同时保留了合法的任务完成。

Conclusion: SkillScope能够有效检测并约束Agent Skill中的过度权限行为，显著缓解最小权限违反问题，且不影响合法任务的完成。

Abstract: Agent Skills have become a practical way to extend LLM agents by packaging metadata, natural-language instructions, and executable resources into reusable capability bundles. However, this growing Skill ecosystem introduces a new compliance risk: a Skill may perform high-impact actions that exceed the minimum necessary scope of the user's current task, thereby violating least-privilege. Existing skill detection approaches are insufficient for this problem because it is inherently task-conditioned: the same action may be necessary under one user prompt but over-privileged under another. In this paper, we present SkillScope, a framework for fine-grained least-privilege enforcement in Agent Skills. SkillScope adopts a graph-based analysis approach that models instruction-level procedures and code-level operations as fine-grained action nodes. It extracts potential over-privilege candidates, validates them under graph-instantiated user tasks through replay-based analysis, and constrains validated over-privileged actions via control-flow privilege constraining. We evaluate SkillScope through effectiveness experiments and large-scale real-world measurement. SkillScope achieves 94.53% F1 for skill over-privilege detection. In the wild, SkillScope validates 7,039 Skills with over-privileged behaviors, showing that least-privilege violations are prevalent in current Skill ecosystems. In the privilege-constraining evaluation, SkillScope reduces triggered over-privileged action-in-task instances by 88.56% while preserving legitimate task completion.

</details>


### [260] [PragLocker: Protecting Agent Intellectual Property in Untrusted Deployments via Non-Portable Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05974)
*Qinfeng Li,Yuntai Bao,Jianghui Hu,Wenqi Zhang,Jintao Chen,Huifeng Zhu,Yier Jin,Xuhong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PragLocker提出了一种针对LLM智能体提示词的保护方案，通过语义锚定和目标模型反馈注入噪声，生成功能保留但仅能在目标LLM上工作的混淆提示词，有效降低跨模型可移植性并保持目标性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体依赖提示词实现任务能力，提示词成为重要的知识产权，但在不可信部署环境中攻击者可以复制并复用到其他专有LLM上，造成经济损失。现有方法无法满足主动性、运行时保护、可用性和不可移植性四个关键需求。

Method: PragLocker通过两步构建功能保留的混淆提示词：首先用代码符号锚定语义，然后利用目标模型反馈注入噪声，使得混淆后的提示词仅能在目标LLM上正常工作。

Result: 在多个智能体系统、数据集和基础LLM上的实验表明，PragLocker大幅降低了跨LLM的可移植性，同时维持了目标性能，并对自适应攻击者具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PragLocker有效地解决了LLM智能体提示词保护问题，满足了主动性、运行时保护、可用性和不可移植性四个关键需求，为提示词知识产权保护提供了可行方案。

Abstract: LLM agents rely on prompts to implement task-specific capabilities based on foundation LLMs, making agent prompts valuable intellectual property. However, in untrusted deployments, adversaries can copy and reuse these prompts with other proprietary LLMs, causing economic losses. To protect these prompts, we identify four key challenges: proactivity, runtime protection, usability, and non-portability that existing approaches fail to address. We present PragLocker, a prompt protection scheme that satisfies these requirements. PragLocker constructs function-preserving obfuscated prompts by anchoring semantics with code symbols and then using target-model feedback to inject noise, yielding prompts that only work on the target LLM. Experiments across multiple agent systems, datasets, and foundation LLMs show that PragLocker substantially reduces cross-LLM portability, maintains target performance, and remains robust against adaptive attackers.

</details>


### [261] [Safety Anchor: Defending Harmful Fine-tuning via Geometric Bottlenecks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05995)
*Guoxin Lu,Letian Sha,Qing Wang,Peijie Sun,Hao Zhou,Hua Dai,Fu Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出安全瓶颈正则化（SBR）方法，通过将防御焦点从冗余的参数空间转移到作为几何瓶颈的unembedding层，将有害查询的最终隐藏状态锚定到安全对齐模型的隐藏状态，从而有效抵御持续的有害微调攻击，将有害分数降至10以下同时保持良性任务的竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法虽然对参数、梯度或内部表示施加约束，但在持续的有害微调下仍能被有效绕过。根本原因在于高维参数空间存在固有冗余性，攻击者可以利用与防御约束正交的优化轨迹，在欺骗性地遵守安全限制的同时恢复有害能力。

Method: 提出安全瓶颈正则化（SBR），将防御焦点从冗余的参数空间转移到unembedding层（几何瓶颈），通过将有害查询的最终隐藏状态锚定到安全对齐模型的隐藏状态，使模型在持续有害微调下仍能维持安全响应。

Result: 实验证实仅使用单个安全锚点即可将有害分数降至10以下，同时在良性下游任务上保持竞争性能。

Conclusion: SBR通过聚焦于几何瓶颈而非参数空间，能有效抵御持续有害微调，是一种简洁而有效的安全对齐防御方法。

Abstract: The safety alignment of Large Language Models (LLMs) remains vulnerable to Harmful Fine-tuning (HFT). While existing defenses impose constraints on parameters, gradients, or internal representations, we observe that they can be effectively circumvented under persistent HFT. Our analysis traces this failure to the inherent redundancy of the high-dimensional parameter space: attackers exploit optimization trajectories that are orthogonal to defense constraints to restore harmful capabilities while deceptively adhering to safety restrictions. To address this, we propose Safety Bottleneck Regularization (SBR). SBR shifts the defensive focus from the redundant parameter space to the unembedding layer, which serves as a geometric bottleneck. By anchoring the final hidden states of harmful queries to those of the safety-aligned model, SBR enables the model to maintain safe responses even under persistent HFT. Extensive experiments confirm SBR's effectiveness, demonstrating that utilizing just a single safety anchor is sufficient to reduce the Harmful Score to $<$10 while preserving competitive performance on benign downstream tasks.

</details>


### [262] [Secure Seed-Based Multi-bit Watermarking for Diffusion Models from First Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06153)
*Enoal Gesny,Eva Giboulot*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对生成式图像水印技术缺乏严格理论分析的问题，提出了一种将模型相关部分与决策机制解耦的正式评估框架，基于安全性、鲁棒性和保真度三维度进行系统比较，并提出了可覆盖任意安全-鲁棒-保真度权衡空间的通用种子嵌入方法SSB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式图像水印技术的评估主要依赖经验性方法，结果高度依赖于具体的生成和反演模型架构，缺乏严格的安全性定义，导致无法对方法性能得出明确结论。

Method: 通过将水印系统中模型依赖部分与实际决策机制解耦，建立基于安全性、鲁棒性和保真度的形式化评估框架，利用特征曲面量化三者间的权衡关系，并提出SSB方法实现对该曲面的全域覆盖。

Result: 提出了独立于生成模型的水印系统比较框架，SSB方法能够达到特征曲面上任意安全-鲁棒-保真度组合，为理论保证下的水印系统设计提供了可行路径。

Conclusion: 该工作为现代水印系统提供了具有理论保证的设计范式，避免了昂贵经验评估的必要性，推动了生成式水印技术从经验驱动向理论驱动的转变。

Abstract: The rapid emergence of generative image models has led to the development of specialized watermarking techniques, particularly in-generation methods such as seed-based embedding. However, current evaluations in this area remain largely empirical, making them heavily reliant on the specific model architectures used for generation and inversion. This prevents any clear conclusion on the performance of any method, especially regarding security, for which a rigorous definition is lacking. Against this approach, we argue that the effectiveness of a watermarking scheme should be established purely through a thorough theoretical analysis. This is enabled by decoupling the model-dependent part from the actual decision mechanism of the watermarking system. Using this decoupling, we introduce a formal evaluation framework based on security, robustness, and fidelity. This allows precise comparisons between watermarking systems through a characteristic surface representing the trade-off between these three quantities, independent of any generative model. Based on this framework, we propose SSB, a novel watermarking method that generalizes previous seed-based methods by allowing to reach any security-robustness-fidelity regime on its characteristic surface. This work opens the door to the design of modern watermarking systems with theoretical guarantees that do not necessitate any costly empirical evaluations.

</details>


### [263] [Stateful Agent Backdoor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06158)
*Zhengchunmin Dai,Jiaxiong Tang,Liantao Wu,Peng Sun,Honglong Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向大语言模型智能体的“有状态”（stateful）后门攻击方法，通过持久化组件将攻击生命周期扩展到多个会话，在权限隔离条件下实现跨会话的自主、增量执行。攻击被形式化为Mealy机模型，并推导出可独立构建每个状态转换数据的分解框架。主攻击在四个模型上达到80%-95%成功率，扩展变体也验证了一致有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对基于大语言模型智能体的后门攻击是无状态的（stateless），攻击行为局限于单个会话内执行固定操作，无法在权限隔离的多会话场景下持续发挥作用。作者希望设计一种能跨越多个会话、利用持久化组件维护状态、在一次触发注入后自主增量执行的后门攻击方法。

Method: 1）将攻击形式化为Mealy机模型，每个状态转换对应一次跨会话的攻击行为；2）推导出分解框架，使得每个状态转换的数据可以独立构建，降低构造复杂度；3）基于该框架实现一个主攻击变体和两种扩展变体（不同拓扑结构和持久化组件）。

Result: 主攻击在四个模型上实现80%-95%的攻击成功率；每个状态转换的独立分析验证了分解框架的有效性；两个扩展变体在不同拓扑和持久化组件下也表现出一致的有效性。

Conclusion: 有状态后门攻击能够有效扩展攻击生命周期至多个会话，在权限隔离条件下维持攻击状态并自主增量执行。分解框架降低了数据构建成本，攻击具有可扩展性。代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: Existing backdoor attacks on Large Language Model-based agents remain stateless, executing fixed behaviors confined to a single session. We propose a stateful agent backdoor that extends the attack lifecycle across multiple sessions under permission isolation. The attack maintains state through persistent components, enabling autonomous, incremental execution across sessions following a one-time trigger injection. Formally, we model the attack as a Mealy machine and derive a decomposition framework that enables independent per-transition data construction. We instantiate this framework with a primary attack and two extensibility variants. The primary instantiation achieves an attack success rate of 80\%--95\% across four models, with per-transition analysis demonstrating the effectiveness of the decomposition. Extensibility variants with alternative topologies and persistent components demonstrate consistent effectiveness. Code and data are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/stateful_agent_backdoor-E89F.

</details>


### [264] [Profiling for Pennies: Unveiling the Privacy Iceberg of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06232)
*Jiahao Chen,Qi Zhang,Ruixiao Lin,Chunyi Zhou,Tianyu Du,Qingming Li,Tong Zhang,Junhao Li,Yuwen Pu,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文揭示了LLM集成平台中隐私风险的严重性，提出PrivacyIceberg三级隐私分类框架和IcebergExplorer审计工具，能在10分钟内以低于3美元的成本实现90%以上的人物画像准确率，并识别了六大根因及多利益相关方对策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要从LLM训练管线角度关注通过记忆泄露PII，缺乏从人类中心视角研究真实世界中的隐私感知和实践，存在平台技术/政策应对与公众担忧之间的显著不匹配。

Method: 通过实证调查人类隐私感知与LLM集成平台实践，提出PrivacyIceberg三级分类（显式搜索、上下文推断、深度聚合），开发IcebergExplorer审计工具，以最小PII为种子重建高保真画像。

Result: IcebergExplorer在真实场景中实现超过90%的事实准确率，耗时不到10分钟，成本低于3美元；识别出六大根因导致隐私泄露。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的隐私泄露风险严峻且易于利用，需LLM供应商、个人和数据发布者等多方采取协同对策。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized how information are collected, aggregated, and reasoned. However, this enables a novel and accessible vector of privacy intrusion: the automated and in-depth personal profiling; this engenders a chilling effect of "peepers everywhere". Existing research primarily unfolds from the training pipeline of LLM, emphasizing the exposure of Personally Identifiable Information (PII) through memorization, while privacy studies from a human-centric perspective remain underexplored. To fill this void, we empirically investigate privacy perception in the real world through the lens of human awareness and the practices of LLM-integrated platforms, revealing a significant dissonance: platforms fail to technically or policy-wise address public privacy concerns. To facilitate a systematic and quantifiable study of privacy risk, we propose the PrivacyIceberg, which categorizes real-world human privacy risks into three tiers: explicitly searched, contextually inferred, and deeply aggregated, based on the sophistication of LLM exploitation. We developed IcebergExplorer to audit privacy exposure, utilizing minimal PII as a search seed to reconstruct high-fidelity profiles, achieving over 90% factual accuracy within 10 minutes at a cost under $3, for real-world scenarios. Additionally, we identify six root causes contributing to such privacy disclosures and propose multi-stakeholder countermeasures for LLM vendors, individuals, and data publishers.

</details>


### [265] [Fine-Tuning Small Language Models for Solution-Oriented Windows Event Log Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06330)
*Siraaj Akhtar,Saad Khan,Simon Parkinson*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了小语言模型（SLM）在事件日志分析中的应用。通过微调SLM，发现其在识别问题和提供修复方案方面优于大型语言模型（LLM），且计算资源需求更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在事件日志分析中虽有潜力，但存在计算成本高、依赖云基础设施、安全风险等问题；现有方法仅识别问题而不提供修复建议。SLM作为轻量级替代方案，值得探索其在实际部署中的可行性。

Method: 1) 使用高性能LLM生成大规模合成Windows事件日志数据集（含修复操作）；2) 采用LoRA参数高效微调技术对多个SLM和LLM进行微调；3) 通过专家评估对比模型性能。

Result: 微调后的SLM在识别问题和提供相关修复方案方面持续优于LLM，同时所需计算资源更少；生成的数据集准确反映了真实场景。

Conclusion: SLM作为轻量级替代方案，在事件日志分析任务上不仅可行，而且在性能和效率上均优于LLM，更适合实际部署。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for event log analysis, but their high computational requirements, reliance on cloud infrastructure, and security concerns limit practical deployment. In addition, most existing approaches focus only on the identification of the problem and do not provide actionable remediation. Small language models (SLMs) present a light-weight alternative that can be fine-tuned for a specific purpose and hosted locally. This paper investigates whether SLMs, when fine-tuned for a specific task, can serve as a practical alternative for event log analysis while also generating solutions. We first create a large-scale synthetic Windows event log dataset that contains remediation actions using a high-performing LLM. We then fine-tune multiple SLMs and LLMs using the LoRA parameter-efficient fine-tuning technique and evaluate their performance by comparing with expert assessment. The results show that the dataset accurately reflects real-world scenarios and that fine-tuned SLMs consistently outperform LLMs in identifying issues and providing relevant remediation, while requiring fewer computational resources.

</details>


### [266] [Pop Quiz Attack: Black-box Membership Inference Attacks Against Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06423)
*Zeyuan Chen,Yihan Ma,Xinyue Shen,Michael Backes,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PopQuiz Attack，一种将训练数据转化为选择题形式的黑盒成员推断攻击方法，在六个主流LLM和四个数据集上取得平均ROC-AUC 0.873，比现有方法提升20.6%，并分析了影响攻击成功的因素及多种防御手段的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型虽然性能强大，但其记忆训练数据的能力引发了严重的隐私担忧，现有成员推断攻击方法效果有限，亟需更有效的攻击手段来评估和暴露模型隐私漏洞。

Method: 将目标数据转化为多项选择形式的测验问题，通过模型对选择题的回答情况来推断该数据是否属于训练集成员，属于黑盒成员推断攻击。

Result: 在GPT-3.5、GPT-4o、LLaMA2-7b、LLaMA2-13b、Mistral-7b、Vicuna-7b六个模型和四个数据集上，平均ROC-AUC达0.873，优于现有方法20.6%。

Conclusion: 现代大型语言模型存在持续性的隐私漏洞，现有的基于指令、过滤和差分隐私的防御手段虽能降低攻击性能但无法完全消除风险。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) show strong performance across many applications, but their ability to memorize and potentially reveal training data raises serious privacy concerns. We introduce the PopQuiz Attack, a black-box membership inference attack that tests whether a model can recall specific training examples. The core idea is to turn target data into quiz-style multiple-choice questions and infer membership from the model's answers. Across six widely used LLMs (GPT-3.5, GPT-4o, LLaMA2-7b, LLaMA2-13b, Mistral-7b, and Vicuna-7b) and four datasets, our method achieves an average ROC-AUC of 0.873 and outperforms existing approaches by 20.6%. We further analyze factors affecting attack success, including query complexity, data type, data structure, and training settings. We also evaluate instruction-based, filter-based, and differential privacy-based defenses, which reduce performance but do not eliminate the risk. Our results highlight persistent privacy vulnerabilities in modern LLMs.

</details>


### [267] [Privacy by Postprocessing the Discrete Laplace Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06502)
*Quentin Hillebrand,Jacob Imola,Rasmus Pagh,Sia Sejer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文证明了经典离散拉普拉斯机制经过后处理可以：(1) 对任意次指数函数 f 生成简单无偏估计量；(2) 输出与拉普拉斯或阶梯机制相同分布。因此离散拉普拉斯应优先用于离散数据场景。同时给出了方差界和高效计算条件，并通过实验验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战传统认知，证明经典离散拉普拉斯机制（"老狗"）能够"学会新把戏"，即通过后处理实现无偏估计和分布等价，从而在离散数据场景下替代拉普拉斯和阶梯机制。

Method: 理论推导离散拉普拉斯机制的后处理方法：(1) 对任意次指数函数构造无偏估计器；(2) 输出与拉普拉斯/阶梯机制相同分布。分析估计器方差并与有偏估计器的均方误差对比，研究特定结构函数的线性/多项式时间计算条件。

Result: 离散拉普拉斯机制经后处理可实现无偏估计，方差有界；在特定结构函数上可在线性/多项式时间内计算；在剖面估计、熵估计、分布式/联邦数据分析中实验验证了效果。

Conclusion: 离散拉普拉斯机制是一种多功能的差分隐私机制，在数据为离散或可通过控制 ℓ₁ 敏感度离散化时，应优先于拉普拉斯和阶梯机制使用。

Abstract: We show that an "old dog", the classical discrete Laplace (aka.~geometric) mechanism, can "perform new tricks":
  1. It can be post-processed to yield a simple, unbiased estimator of any subexponential function $f$ of the original data, giving a simple, discrete, multivariate version of the recent unbiasing result for the Laplace mechanism by Calmon et al. (FORC '25).
  2. It can be post-processed to output the same distribution as the Laplace mechanism or the Staircase mechanism with identical privacy parameters.
  Thus, the discrete Laplace mechanism is a versatile mechanism that should be preferred over the Laplace and Staircase mechanisms whenever the data is discrete (or can be made discrete while controlling $\ell_1$-sensitivity).
  We show bounds on the variance of our estimator, compared to the mean square error of the biased estimator that simply evaluates the $f$ on the output of the mechanism. Though our unbiased estimator has exponential running time for worst-case functions, we show that it can often be computed in linear or polynomial time for some common functions exhibiting structure. We showcase the properties of our methods empirically with several use cases including profile and entropy estimation, as well as distributed/federated data analysis applications in which unbiasedness is key to accuracy.

</details>


### [268] [On the Security of Research Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06508)
*Nanda Rani,Christian Rossow*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了509个顶级安全会议的研究工件，发现41.60%存在安全风险。作者提出了SAFE框架，通过语义分析、执行上下文和实际可利用性评估，以84.80%的准确率和84.63%的F1分数自动区分安全与非安全风险，呼吁在工件评估中纳入安全检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工件评估（AE）主要关注可重复性，忽视了共享工件可能引入的安全风险。研究工件被公开发布和重用，可能无意中为滥用创造机会，引发安全和负责任共享的担忧。

Method: 收集509个来自顶级安全会议的研究工件，提出上下文感知安全评估分类法，进行静态分析并过滤误报，构建SAFE（安全感知工件评估框架）自动分析工具报告发现，考虑代码语义、执行上下文和实际可利用性。

Result: 41.60%的普遍发现存在实际使用中的安全风险；SAFE框架实现84.80%准确率和84.63%的F1分数。

Conclusion: 安全性在工件评估中同样重要，应纳入AE流程以促进安全、负责任的研究共享。

Abstract: Research artifacts are widely shared to support reproducibility, and artifact evaluation (AE) has become common at many leading conferences. However, AE mainly checks whether artifacts work as claimed and can be reproduced. It largely overlooks potential security risks. Since these artifacts are publicly released and reused, they may unintentionally create opportunities for misuse and raise concerns about safe and responsible sharing. We study 509 research artifacts from top-tier security venues and find that many contain insecure code patterns that may introduce potential attack vectors. We propose a taxonomy for context-aware security assessment to enable structured analysis of such risks. We perform static analysis and examine the resulting findings, filtering false positives and identifying real security risks. Our analysis shows that 41.60% of the prevalent findings may pose security concerns under practical usage. To support scalable analysis, we introduce SAFE (Security-Aware Framework for Artifact Evaluation), a first step toward an autonomous framework that analyzes tool-reported findings by considering code semantics, execution context, and practical exploitability. SAFE achieves 84.80% accuracy and 84.63% F1-score in distinguishing security and non-security risks. Overall, our results show that security is also important in AE for promoting safe and responsible research sharing. The source code is available at: https://github.com/nanda-rani/SAFE

</details>


### [269] [FedAttr: Towards Privacy-preserving Client-Level Attribution in Federated LLM Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06596)
*Su Zhang,Junfeng Guo,Heng Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出FedAttr，一种用于联邦学习中客户端级别水印归属的协议，通过成对子集差分机制在保护安全聚合隐私的同时，准确识别哪些客户端使用了水印数据进行训练，实现了100% TPR和0% FPR，优于基线方法至少44.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水印放射性检测方法在中心化LLM微调中有效，但在联邦学习场景下仍面临挑战——安全聚合机制保护了客户端更新隐私，使得难以识别哪个客户端使用了水印文档进行训练。因此需要一种既保护隐私又能进行客户端级别归属的方法。

Method: FedAttr包含三个步骤：(i) 通过差分两个安全聚合查询来估计每个客户端的更新；(ii) 使用差分评分对估计结果进行水印检测器评分；(iii) 通过Stouffer方法跨轮次组合得分。采用成对子集差分机制实现客户端归因。

Result: 理论证明FedAttr产生客户端更新的无偏估计，每轮互信息泄露有界(O(d^*/N))；实验达到100% TPR和0% FPR，在TPR或FPR上优于所有基线至少44.4%或19.1%，仅增加6.3%的FL训练时间开销。

Conclusion: FedAttr在保护联邦学习隐私的同时实现了高精度的水印归属，对协议参数和配置具有鲁棒性，是联邦学习场景下数据所有权保护的有效工具。

Abstract: Watermark radioactivity testing type of methods can detect whether a model was trained on watermarked documents, and have become key tools for protecting data ownership in the fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs). Existing works have proved their effectiveness in centralized LLM fine-tuning. However, this type of method faces several challenges and remains underexplored in federated learning (FL), a widely-applied paradigm for fine-tuning LLMs collaboratively on private data across different users. FL mainly ensures privacy through secure aggregation (SA), which allows the server to aggregate updates while keeping clients' updates private. This mechanism preserves privacy but makes it difficult to identify which client trained on watermarked documents. In this work, we propose FedAttr, a new client-level attribution protocol for FL. FedAttr identifies which clients trained on watermarked data via a paired-subset-difference mechanism, while preserving the privacy guarantees of SA and FL performance. FedAttr proceeds in three steps: (i) estimate each client's update by differencing two SA queries, (ii) score the estimate with the watermark detector via differential scoring, and (iii) combine scores across rounds via Stouffer method. We theoretically show that FedAttr produces an unbiased estimator of each client's update with bounded mutual information leakage (i.e., $O(d^*/N)$ per-round update). Moreover, FedAttr empirically achieves 100% TPR and 0% FPR, outperforming all baselines by at least 44.4% in TPR or 19.1% in FPR, with only 6.3% overhead relative to FL training time. Ablation studies confirm that FedAttr is robust to protocol parameters and configurations.

</details>
